arXiv:2608.31075v1 [cs.AI] 2026 年 8 月 31 日arXiv:2608.31075v1 [cs.AI] 31 Aug 2026
 

将大型推理模型扩展到人类监督之外：通往超级智能的道路Scaling Large Reasoning Models beyond Human Supervision: A Path toward Superintelligence

杨志勤*Zhiqin Yang*1, 付静文*, Jingwen Fu*2, 刘玉涵*, Yuhan Liu*3, 刘恒宇*, Hengyu Liu*4, 张永刚*, Yonggang Zhang*1, 曹凯南, Kainan Cao5, 张子卓, Zizhuo Zhang6, 李晨新, Chenxin Li7, 袁瑞斌, Ruibin Yuan1, 潘家豪, Jiahao Pan1, 孙建凯, Jiankai Sun4, 张振元, Zhenyuan Zhang1, 李一波, Yibo Li8, 林云龙, Yunlong Lin9, 熊敬, Jing Xiong5, 林思达, Sida Lin1, 韩博, Bo Han6†, 魏雪, Wei Xue1†, 郭一可, Yike Guo1†

1香港科技大学The Hong Kong University of Science and Technology2中关村书院Zhongguancun Academy
3西安交通大学Xi’an Jiaotong University4香港中文大学The Chinese University of Hong Kong

5香港大学The University of Hong Kong6香港浸会大学Hong Kong Baptist University
7混源腾讯Hunyuan Tencent8新加坡国立大学National University of Singapore9厦门大学Xiamen University

GitHub：GitHub:https://github.com/visitworld123/Awesome-Scaling-LRM-Beyond-Human-Supervision

 

* 核心贡献* Core contribution†通讯作者Corresponding author

1. 简介1. Introduction

大型推理模型（LRM）的出现The emergence of large reasoning models (LRMs)(Hui et al., 2024; Yang et al., 2025a; Li et al., 2025k; Chen et al., 2025e; Achiam et al., 2023; Comanici et al., 2025; Team et al., 2025a; Team et al., 2025b; Sun et al., 2025b)极大地提高了语言模型的推理能力。最近的系统将大规模预训练与强化学习和增加的推理时间计算相结合。 DeepSeek-R1等型号has substantially advanced the reasoning capabilities of language models. Recent systems combine large-scale pretraining with reinforcement learning and increased inference-time computation. Models such as DeepSeek-R1(Guo et al., 2025a; Shao et al., 2024)和 OpenAI o1and OpenAI o1(Jaech et al., 2024)在数学和代码生成方面取得了出色的表现，可以检查答案或执行程序以产生信息丰富的训练信号。这些进展表明，进一步的进展不仅取决于模型规模和预训练数据，还取决于生成轨迹、获取反馈和从交互中学习的能力。这种转变推动了体验时代have achieved strong performance in mathematics and code generation, where answers can be checked or programs can be executed to produce informative training signals. These advances suggest that further progress depends not only on model scale and pretraining data, but also on the ability to generate trajectories, obtain feedback, and learn from interaction. This shift motivates the Experience Era(Silver & Sutton, 2025)，其中经验成为能力提升越来越重要的来源。, in which experience becomes an increasingly important source of capability improvement.

具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）为从经验中学习提供了坚实的基础。数学答案可以与已知的解决方案进行比较，而生成的代码可以自动编译和测试Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) provides a strong foundation for learning from experience. Mathematical answers can be compared with known solutions, while generated code can be compiled and tested automatically(Yu et al., 2025c; Zhou et al., 2025f; Lin et al., 2025b; Deng et al., 2025)。因此，可以评估大量的部署，而无需要求人员检查每个响应。然而，对于实践中重要的许多任务，确定性验证是不可用的。创意写作. As a result, large numbers of rollouts can be evaluated without asking a person to inspect each response. However, deterministic verification is unavailable for many tasks that matter in practice. Creative writing(Lu, 2025), 开放式问答, open-ended question answering(Yu et al., 2025d; Ma et al., 2025)，复杂的代理交互可能会产生几种有效的结果，其质量取决于上下文、偏好或长期后果。学习奖励模型和语言模型法官将反馈扩展到这些设置，但他们的分数仍然是预期目标的代理。此外，随着推理轨迹变长并且交互跨越更多步骤，直接的人类评估变得越来越昂贵, and complex agent interactions may admit several valid outcomes whose quality depends on context, preferences, or long-term consequences. Learned reward models and language-model judges extend feedback to these settings, but their scores remain proxies for the intended objective. Moreover, direct human evaluation becomes increasingly costly as reasoning traces grow longer and interactions span more steps(Casper et al., 2023)。当任务接近或超过专家难度时，评估人员可能还需要模型辅助来识别错误并可靠地评估输出. When tasks approach or exceed expert difficulty, evaluators may also require model assistance to identify errors and assess outputs reliably(Saunders et al., 2022)。因此，挑战不仅仅是用更便宜的分数取代昂贵的标签，而是当人类判断和人类设计不再能够覆盖整个学习体验流时，维持有意义的改进。. Consequently, the challenge is not simply to replace an expensive label with a cheaper score, but to sustain meaningful improvement when human judgment and human design can no longer cover the full stream of learning experience.

我们使用We use超越人类监督scaling beyond human supervision描述一种转变，即学习过程中需要持续人力提供的部分更少。人类意图仍然至关重要，可以通过目标、初始数据、工具、环境、安全约束和独立审计来表达。核心变化在于学习过程中所需的重复努力，因为模型越来越需要获得有用的反馈并获得信息丰富的经验，而不依赖于人们来评估或设计每个训练实例。我们通过两个相连的轴来研究这种转变。的to describe a shift in which fewer components of the learning process require continued human provision. Human intent remains essential and may be expressed through objectives, initial data, tools, environments, safety constraints, and independent audits. The central change lies in the recurring effort required during learning, as models increasingly need to obtain useful feedback and encounter informative experience without depending on people to evaluate or design every training instance. We study this shift through two connected axes. The奖励轴reward axis涉及用于评估行为的证据，而concerns the evidence used to evaluate behavior, while the体验轴experience axis涉及产生模型学习轨迹的任务和环境。concerns the tasks and environments that produce the trajectories from which models learn.

沿着奖励轴，可验证的任务提供了获得可扩展反馈的最直接途径，因为正确性基于答案检查或执行。对于没有确切答案的任务，学习的奖励模型、评分标准和语言模型法官可以编码评估标准并将其应用于许多轨迹Along the reward axis, verifiable tasks provide the most direct route to scalable feedback because correctness is grounded in answer checking or execution. For tasks without exact answers, learned reward models, rubrics, and language-model judges can encode evaluative criteria and apply them across many trajectories(Seed et al., 2025; Ren et al., 2025a; Liu et al., 2025j; Su et al., 2025)。其他方法从模型内部确定性、采样响应之间的一致性或与可用参考的一致性中获得奖励. Other methods derive rewards from model-internal certainty, agreement across sampled responses, or consistency with available references(Prabhudesai et al., 2025; Zuo et al., 2025b; Zhang et al., 2025o; Tang et al., 2025; Zhou et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025d; Liu et al., 2025h)。当成功在环境中产生可观察到的变化时，互动结果提供了另一个基础来源。这些方法共同拓宽了支持强化学习的经验范围。与此同时，反馈变得更容易受到错误校准、相关错误和奖励利用的影响，因为它较少依赖于独立证据。因此，在扩大奖励获取的同时，还必须仔细分析每个信号的基础以及该基础在优化下的表现。. When success produces observable changes in an environment, interaction outcomes offer another source of grounding. Together, these approaches broaden the range of experiences that can support reinforcement learning. At the same time, feedback becomes more vulnerable to miscalibration, correlated errors, and reward exploitation as it depends less on independent evidence. Scaling reward acquisition must therefore be accompanied by careful analysis of what grounds each signal and how that grounding behaves under optimization.

仅靠可靠的反馈并不能确保持续进步，因为学习还取决于应用反馈的经验。最初，模型可以在固定环境中训练人类编写的任务。他们随后可以生成推理轨迹、综合指令或通过提议者和求解者交互调整任务难度Reliable feedback alone does not ensure continued progress because learning also depends on the experience to which that feedback is applied. Initially, models may train on human-written tasks in fixed environments. They can subsequently generate reasoning traces, synthesize instructions, or adjust task difficulty through proposer-and-solver interaction(Fang et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025b)。更集成的方法构建可执行环境，从实时界面提出任务，并保留以前交互中的可重用信息. More integrated approaches construct executable environments, propose tasks from live interfaces, and retain reusable information from previous interactions(Yang et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025a; Cai et al., 2025)。这些机制允许课程随政策而变化，并且可以暴露固定数据集不再揭示的弱点。然而，只有当任务保持多样性、可解决性和相关性，并且综合环境保留目标领域的动态和结果时，生成的经验才有用。因此，经验轴将注意力从采样轨迹的数量转移到产生轨迹的过程的质量和可持续性。. These mechanisms allow the curriculum to change with the policy and can expose weaknesses that a fixed dataset no longer reveals. Nevertheless, generated experience is useful only when tasks remain diverse, solvable, and relevant, and when synthesized environments preserve the dynamics and consequences of the target domain. The experience axis therefore shifts attention from the quantity of sampled trajectories to the quality and sustainability of the process that produces them.

这两个轴最终会收敛，因为除非可以评估其结果，否则生成的任务无法支持学习，而如果模型反复遇到熟悉或无信息的情况，则可扩展的奖励价值有限。因此，持续改进需要奖励和经验共同发展。在成熟的学习循环中，更强有力的政策揭示了新的弱点，这些弱点引导新任务和环境的生成，而可靠的评估将由此产生的轨迹转化为进一步的政策更新。这种共同进化提供了一条超越人类监管者的带宽和专业知识的能力增长的可能途径。然而，它也会产生紧密耦合的故障模式，因为策略、生成器和评估器可能会相互适应，而独立标准下的性能会停滞或下降。The two axes ultimately converge because a generated task cannot support learning unless its outcome can be evaluated, while a scalable reward has limited value if the model repeatedly encounters familiar or uninformative situations. Continued improvement therefore requires rewards and experience to develop together. In a mature learning loop, stronger policies reveal new weaknesses, those weaknesses guide the generation of new tasks and environments, and reliable evaluation turns the resulting trajectories into further policy updates. Such co-evolution offers a possible route to capability growth beyond the bandwidth and expertise of human supervisors. However, it also creates tightly coupled failure modes because policies, generators, and evaluators may adapt to one another while performance under independent criteria stagnates or declines.

为了组织这个进程，我们引入了从 L0 到 L4 的五级阶梯，如图所示To organize this progression, we introduce a five-level ladder from L0 to L4 in Figure1。该阶梯从每个实例的人类监督开始，通过可重复使用的基于人类的评估和超越人类评估获得的奖励，然后延伸到超越持续人类设计产生的经验。它的最终级别代表了一个理想化的循环，其中政策、奖励、任务和环境共同演化。通过确定哪些组件仍然依赖于持续的人力提供，阶梯描述了学习期间的操作责任，而不是作为能力、可靠性、一致性或学习基础设施中嵌入的人类知识的历史量的衡量标准。. The ladder begins with per-instance human supervision, proceeds through reusable human-grounded evaluation and rewards obtained beyond human evaluation, and then extends to experience generated beyond continued human design. Its final level represents an idealized loop in which policies, rewards, tasks, and environments co-evolve. By identifying which components still depend on continued human provision, the ladder characterizes operational responsibility during learning without serving as a measure of capability, reliability, alignment, or the historical amount of human knowledge embedded in the learning infrastructure.

我们的论文通过三种方式开发了这个帐户。首先，它通过连接的奖励和经验轴制定了超越人类监督的扩展，并引入了一个通用框架，用于在学习循环中定位持续的人类供应。其次，它根据现有方法如何获得奖励和产生经验来组织现有方法，同时检查与减少人类参与相关的假设和失败模式。第三，它开发了一个区分政策能力、反馈保真度和体验质量的评估框架。这种区别也影响了我们对可靠奖励、可持续课程、有效环境、高效学习和安全自主改进的讨论。Our paper develops this account in three ways. First, it formulates scaling beyond human supervision through the connected reward and experience axes and introduces a common framework for locating continued human provision within the learning loop. Second, it organizes existing methods according to how they obtain rewards and produce experience, while examining the assumptions and failure modes associated with decreasing human involvement. Third, it develops an evaluation framework that distinguishes policy capability, feedback fidelity, and experience quality. This distinction also informs our discussion of reliable rewards, sustainable curricula, valid environments, efficient learning, and safe autonomous improvement.

本文的其余部分将分阶段阐述这一观点。部分The remainder of the paper develops this account in stages. Section2介绍了正式设置和后退监督阶梯。部分introduces the formal setting and the receding-supervision ladder. Section3随着人类直接评价的消退，研究奖励的来源和基础。部分studies the sources and grounding of rewards as direct human evaluation recedes. Section4检查任务生成、环境构建以及它们最终与策略的共同演化。部分examines task generation, environment construction, and their eventual co-evolution with the policy. Section5提供用于评估能力、反馈和经验的数据集、基准和协议。最后，部分presents datasets, benchmarks, and protocols for evaluating capability, feedback, and experience. Finally, Section6讨论了主要挑战和研究方向，并且部分discusses the principal challenges and research directions, and Section7总结了我们分析的范围和局限性。summarizes the scope and limitations of our analysis.

2. 预备知识2. Preliminaries

本节介绍整篇论文中使用的符号和学习框架。我们首先描述常见 MDP 和 POMDP 公式中的单轮推理和顺序交互。然后，我们研究奖励如何从直接的人类监督转向从模型、参考和环境中获得的信号。在介绍后面章节中使用的策略优化目标之后，我们定义了模型派生奖励方法所需的不确定性度量。这些元素支持本节末尾提出的五级阶梯，它将超越人类评估的奖励与超越人类设计的经验联系起来。表This section introduces the notation and learning framework used throughout the paper. We first describe single-turn reasoning and sequential interaction within a common MDP and POMDP formulation. We then examine how reward can move from direct human supervision toward signals obtained from models, references, and environments. After introducing the policy-optimization objectives used in later sections, we define the uncertainty measures required by model-derived reward methods. These elements support the five-level ladder presented at the end of the section, which connects reward beyond human evaluation with experience beyond human design. Table1总结了我们论文中的符号。summarizes the notation in our paper.

2.1. 从单匝 MDP 到顺序 POMDP2.1. From Single-Turn MDP to Sequential POMDP

从经验中学习可以表示为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）Learning from experience can be represented as a Markov decision process (MDP)(Sutton et al., 1998)，表示为, denoted byℳ:=(𝒮,𝒜,𝒫,ℛ,γ)\mathcal{M}:=(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{A},\mathcal{P},\mathcal{R},\gamma)。在步骤. At steptt, 政策, the policyπθ\pi_{\theta}选择一个动作selects an action𝒂t∈𝒜\bm{a}_{t}\in\mathcal{A}来自州from state𝒔t∈𝒮\bm{s}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}根据according to𝒂t∼πθ(⋅∣𝒔t)\bm{a}_{t}\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{s}_{t})。对于 LRM，操作可能包含推理跟踪. For an LRM, the action may contain a reasoning trace𝒛t\bm{z}_{t}与答案或工具命令一起together with an answer or tool command𝒚t\bm{y}_{t}。转换函数通过以下方式确定下一个状态. The transition function determines the next state through𝒔t+1∼𝒫(⋅∣𝒔t,𝒂t)\bm{s}_{t+1}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot\mid\bm{s}_{t},\bm{a}_{t})，而奖励函数分配一个标量信号, while the reward function assigns a scalar signalrt=ℛ⁡(𝒔t,𝒂t)r_{t}=\mathcal{R}(\bm{s}_{t},\bm{a}_{t})。多种评估标准可能有助于这种标量奖励。折扣系数. Multiple evaluation criteria may contribute to this scalar reward. The discount factorγ∈[0,1]\gamma\in[0,1]控制未来奖励的贡献。controls the contribution of future rewards.

MDP 的表述假设政策遵守决策所需的状态。许多代理设置只能部分观察到。因此，部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）通过观察空间增强了公式The MDP formulation assumes that the policy observes the state needed for decision-making. Many agentic settings are only partially observable. A Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) therefore augments the formulation with an observation space𝒪\mathcal{O}和观察模型。环境保持潜在状态and an observation model. The environment retains a latent state𝒔t\bm{s}_{t}，当模型收到观察结果时, while the model receives an observation𝒐t\bm{o}_{t}并从交互历史中选择其动作and selects its action from the interaction history𝒉t=(𝒐0,𝒂0,…,𝒐t)\bm{h}_{t}=(\bm{o}_{0},\bm{a}_{0},\ldots,\bm{o}_{t})。 GUI 应用程序、工具系统和对话通常具有这种结构，因为模型仅观察部分底层环境状态。. GUI applications, tool systems, and conversations commonly have this structure because the model observes only part of the underlying environment state.

体验轨迹An experience trajectoryτ\tau记录模型与其环境之间的交互records the interaction between the model and its environmentℰ\mathcal{E}。为了一个地平线. For a horizonTT，基本轨迹可以写为, the underlying trajectory can be written asτ=(𝒔0,𝒂0,r0,…,𝒔T−1,𝒂T−1,rT−1)\tau=(\bm{s}_{0},\bm{a}_{0},r_{0},\ldots,\bm{s}_{T-1},\bm{a}_{T-1},r_{T-1})，尽管 POMDP 中的策略是从, although the policy in a POMDP acts from𝒉t\bm{h}_{t}而不是直接来自rather than directly from𝒔t\bm{s}_{t}。给定任务分配. Given a task distributionΠ\Pi和初始状态分布and an initial-state distributionρ0(⋅∣𝒒)\rho_{0}(\cdot\mid\bm{q})，学习目标为：, the learning objective is:

maxθ𝔼𝒒∼Π,𝒔0∼ρ0(⋅∣𝒒),τ∼pθ(⋅∣𝒔0,ℰ)[∑t=0T−1γtℛ(𝒔t,𝒂t)].\max_{\theta}\,\mathbb{E}_{\bm{q}\sim\Pi,\,\bm{s}_{0}\sim\rho_{0}(\cdot\mid\bm{q}),\,\tau\sim p_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{s}_{0},\mathcal{E})}\left[\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\gamma^{t}\,\mathcal{R}(\bm{s}_{t},\bm{a}_{t})\right]. (1)

地平线The horizonTT将单轮推理和顺序交互置于同一目标内。当places single-turn reasoning and sequential interaction within the same objective. WhenT=1T{=}1和andγ=1\gamma{=}1，一个查询会产生一个推理和答案轨迹，然后是一个结果奖励。此设置捕获数学和代码中常见的 RLVR 公式。当, a query produces one reasoning and answer trajectory, followed by an outcome reward. This setting captures common RLVR formulations in mathematics and code. WhenT>1T{>}1，模型重复作用于, the model acts repeatedly inℰ\mathcal{E}，而后来的结果取决于先前的决定。那么局部可观察性和长期信用分配就成为中心。因此，单匝设置是更广泛的顺序公式的特例。, and later outcomes depend on earlier decisions. Then the partial observability and long-horizon credit assignment become the central. Therefore, the single-turn setting is a special case of the broader sequential formulation.

2.2. 作为缩放对象的奖励函数2.2. The Reward Function as the Object of Scaling

方程Equation1将经验来源与用于评估经验的信号分开。其中的组成部分separates the source of experience from the signal used to evaluate it. Among the components ofℳ\mathcal{M}，奖励函数, the reward functionℛ\mathcal{R}为追踪人类监督提供了最直接的起点。在常见的 RLVR 设置中，基于规则的过程将模型输出与特定于实例的答案进行比较provides the most immediate starting point for tracing human supervision. In a common RLVR setting, a rule-based procedure compares the model output with an instance-specific answer𝒚∗\bm{y}^{*}或随任务提供的测试套件。在基于偏好的训练中，学习奖励模型or test suite supplied with the task. In preference-based training, a learned reward modelℛϕ\mathcal{R}_{\phi}或验证者or verifierπv\pi_{v}应用源自人类判断的标准。第一个设置保留了人类对每项任务所附加目标的影响。第二个将人类的判断转移到可重用的评估器中。因此，它们的差异涉及评估标准的出处和可重用性，而不是分数产生的时间。applies criteria derived from human judgments. The first setting retains human influence in the target attached to each task. The second transfers human judgments into a reusable evaluator. Their difference therefore concerns the provenance and reusability of the evaluation criterion rather than when the score is produced.

其他奖励来源以不同方式减少对人类评估的依赖。模型派生方法使用策略分布的属性，例如采样答案之间的确定性或一致性。依赖于参考的方法通过可用目标自动评分，而基于环境的方法则使用执行结果、正式接受、游戏结果或可观察的状态变化等结果。这些信号可能仍然依赖于人类创建的参考、规则或环境。相关的转变是人类判断不再作为评估每条新轨迹的主要机制。部分Other reward sources reduce dependence on human evaluation in different ways. Model-derived methods use properties of the policy distribution, such as certainty or agreement among sampled answers. Reference-dependent methods automate scoring through an available target, while environment-grounded methods use consequences such as execution results, formal acceptance, game outcomes, or observable state changes. These signals may still depend on human-created references, rules, or environments. The relevant transition is that human judgment no longer serves as the primary mechanism for evaluating each new trajectory. Section3通过奖励来源和外部基础来组织这些方法。部分organizes these methods by reward provenance and external grounding. Section2.5将这种转变与任务和环境的来源联系起来。connects this transition with the source of tasks and environments.

2.3. 政策优化2.3. Policy Optimization

一旦指定了奖励源，策略优化就会决定该信号如何更新模型。对于单匝情况，令Once a reward source has been specified, policy optimization determines how that signal updates the model. For the single-turn case, let𝒟\mathcal{D}表示任务分布的经验近似denote an empirical approximation to the task distributionΠ\Pi。奖励驱动方法优化. Reward-driven methods optimize

并将其梯度估计为and estimate its gradient as(Sutton et al., 1998)

∇θ𝒥(θ)=𝔼𝒒∼𝒟,𝒂∼πθ(⋅|𝒒)[A^(𝒒,𝒂)∇θlogπθ(𝒂∣𝒒)].\nabla_{\theta}\mathcal{J}(\theta)=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{q}\sim\mathcal{D},\,\bm{a}\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot|\bm{q})}\left[\hat{A}(\bm{q},\bm{a})\,\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta}(\bm{a}\mid\bm{q})\right]. (3)

优势估计The advantage estimateA^​(𝒒,𝒂)\hat{A}(\bm{q},\bm{a})根据相对于基线的性能对对数似然梯度进行加权。奖励来源和优化器设计在此表达式中发挥不同的作用。奖励来源定义weights the log-likelihood gradient according to performance relative to a baseline. Reward provenance and optimizer design play different roles in this expression. The reward source definesℛ\mathcal{R}，而优化器确定如何估计优势以及更新期间策略可能移动多远。 PPO 和 GRPO 说明了两种广泛使用的选择：基于批评家的方法和无批评家的方法。, while the optimizer determines how advantages are estimated and how far the policy may move during an update. PPO and GRPO illustrate two widely used choices: critic-based and critic-free methods.

聚苯醚。PPO.

近端策略优化 (PPO)Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)(Schulman et al., 2017)通常使用学习值函数commonly uses a learned value functionVψV_{\psi}作为其基线。它估计代币级别的优势as its baseline. It estimates the token-level advantageA^t\hat{A}_{t}并通过重要性比限制每次更新and limits each update through the importance ratiort​(θ)=πθ​(at|𝒔t)πθold​(at|𝒔t)r_{t}(\theta)=\frac{\pi_{\theta}(a_{t}|\bm{s}_{t})}{\pi_{\theta_{\mathrm{old}}}(a_{t}|\bm{s}_{t})}:

𝒥PPO​(θ)=𝔼t​[min⁡(rt​(θ)​A^t,clip​(rt​(θ),1−ε,1+ε)​A^t)].\mathcal{J}_{\text{PPO}}(\theta)=\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\min\left(r_{t}(\theta)\hat{A}_{t},\ \text{clip}(r_{t}(\theta),1-\varepsilon,1+\varepsilon)\hat{A}_{t}\right)\right]. (4)

这一被剪裁的目标阻碍了可能破坏培训稳定的重大政策变化。在常见的 LRM 实现中，PPO 还需要训练和存储与策略大小相当的价值模型，这会增加大量的内存和计算成本。This clipped objective discourages large policy changes that could destabilize training. In common LRM implementations, PPO also requires training and storing a value model comparable in size to the policy, which adds substantial memory and computation costs.

GRPO。GRPO.

组相关策略优化 (GRPO)Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)(Guo et al., 2025a)用根据一组采样的首次展示计算出的统计数据替换学习值基线。给定一个查询replaces the learned value baseline with statistics computed from a group of sampled rollouts. Given a query𝒒\bm{q}，它采样, it samplesGG推出rollouts𝒂i=(𝒛i,𝒚i)\bm{a}_{i}=(\bm{z}_{i},\bm{y}_{i})并为每次部署分配奖励and assigns each rollout a rewardrir_{i}。标准化的群体相对优势是. The normalized group-relative advantage is

A^i=ri−mean​({rj}j=1G)std​({rj}j=1G).\hat{A}_{i}=\frac{r_{i}-\text{mean}(\{r_{j}\}_{j=1}^{G})}{\text{std}(\{r_{j}\}_{j=1}^{G})}. (5)

使用该群体作为隐式基线可以避免单独的价值网络。由此产生的裁剪目标是Using the group as an implicit baseline avoids a separate value network. The resulting clipped objective is

惩罚偏离参考政策的行为penalizes deviation from the reference policyπref\pi_{\text{ref}}。通过将群体相对优势与截断更新和 KL 正则化相结合，GRPO 提供了广泛用于 LRM 训练的无批评目标。. By combining group-relative advantages with clipped updates and KL regularization, GRPO provides a critic-free objective that is widely used in LRM training.

2.4. 不确定性措施2.4. Uncertainty Measures

方程中的优化器。The optimizer in Eq.2不需要来自外部验证者的奖励。节中的几种方法does not require rewards to come from an external verifier. Several methods in Section3相反，从生成轨迹的不确定性中获得模型内部奖励。这些方法使用的两个量需要明确区分。序列意外聚合了采样完成的负对数似然instead derive model-internal rewards from uncertainty over generated trajectories. Two quantities used by these methods require a clear distinction. Sequence surprisal aggregates the negative log-likelihood of a sampled completion𝒐\bm{o}:

Useq(𝒐,πθ)=−1|𝒐|∑t=1|𝒐|logπθ(𝒐t∣𝒐<t).U_{\mathrm{seq}}(\bm{o},\pi_{\theta})=-\frac{1}{|\bm{o}|}\sum_{t=1}^{|\bm{o}|}\log\pi_{\theta}(\bm{o}_{t}\mid\bm{o}_{<t}). (8)

较低的序列意外意味着在该策略下采样完成的可能性更大，但它并不能证明完成是正确的。相反，预测令牌熵衡量整个下一个令牌分布的不确定性：Lower sequence surprisal means that the sampled completion is more probable under the policy, but it does not establish that the completion is correct. Predictive token entropy instead measures uncertainty over the full next-token distribution:

Ht(πθ)=−∑v∈𝒱πθ(v∣𝒐<t)logπθ(v∣𝒐<t).H_{t}(\pi_{\theta})=-\sum_{v\in\mathcal{V}}\pi_{\theta}(v\mid\bm{o}_{<t})\log\pi_{\theta}(v\mid\bm{o}_{<t}). (9)

较低的令牌熵表示特定生成步骤的分布更加集中。方法可以聚合Lower token entropy indicates a more concentrated distribution at a particular generation step. A method may aggregateHtH_{t}穿过一条轨迹，其归一化决定了序列长度如何影响结果。序列意外熵和预测熵都可以用作内部信号，但在没有额外校准或基础的情况下，两者都不应被解释为正确性概率。across a trajectory, with its normalization determining how sequence length affects the result. Sequence surprisal and predictive entropy can both serve as internal signals, but neither should be interpreted as a correctness probability without additional calibration or grounding.

2.5. 监管后退的五级阶梯2.5. A Five-Level Ladder of Receding Supervision

该框架区分了两个问题：用于改进政策的反馈从何而来，以及由谁决定产生其经验的任务和环境。的The framework separates two questions: where the feedback used to improve a policy comes from, and who determines the tasks and environments that produce its experience. The奖励轴reward axis解决第一个问题，从直接的人类判断开始，扩展到可重用的评估器和其他反馈来源。的addresses the first question, beginning with direct human judgments and extending to reusable evaluators and other sources of feedback. The体验轴experience axis解决了第二个问题，涵盖从固定的、人为设计的培训环境到学习过程中生成或发现的经验的转变。 图addresses the second, covering the shift from fixed, human-designed training settings to experience that is generated or discovered during learning. Figure1根据学习循环的哪些部分仍然需要持续的人类参与，将这种进展分为五种模式。分类关注的是学习如何持续，而不是模型、数据、工具或环境是否最初是由人类创建的。然后我们将这个五级阶梯制定如下：groups this progression into five regimes according to which parts of the learning loop still require ongoing human involvement. The classification concerns how learning is sustained, not whether the models, data, tools, or environments were originally created by humans. We then formulate this five-level ladder as follows:

  1. L0

    每个实例的人工监督Per-instance Human Supervision。每个训练实例都与人类提供的答案、偏好或判断配对，作为其评估目标。. Each training instance is paired with a human-provided answer, preference, or judgment that serves as its evaluation target.

  2. L1

    以人为本的评估Human-grounded Evaluation。人类标准被编码在可重复使用的奖励模型、评分标准或语言模型法官中，允许在许多轨迹上应用相同的评估标准。. Human criteria are encoded in reusable reward models, rubrics, or language-model judges, allowing the same evaluative standard to be applied across many trajectories.

  3. L2

    超越人类评估的奖励Reward beyond Human Evaluation。训练不是主要依赖于人类判断，而是从模型置信度、样本之间的一致性、可用参考或可观察的环境结果中获得奖励，而任务和环境仍然主要由外部提供。. Rather than relying primarily on human judgment, training derives rewards from model confidence, agreement across samples, available references, or observable environment outcomes, while tasks and environments remain largely externally provided.

  4. L3

    超越人类设计的体验Experience beyond Human Design。随着学习过程中任务、课程和环境的生成、发现、选择或重组，训练分布变得具有适应性，同时人类继续设定广泛的目标、约束和初始条件。. The training distribution becomes adaptive as tasks, curricula, and environments are generated, discovered, selected, or reorganized during learning, while humans continue to set broad goals, constraints, and initial conditions.

  5. L4

    自主协同进化Autonomous Co-evolution。反馈、经验和政策改进在一个持续的循环中一起适应，而人类的参与则集中在意图、安全约束和独立监督的层面上。. Feedback, experience, and policy improvement adapt together in a persistent loop, while human involvement is concentrated at the level of intent, safety constraints, and independent oversight.

该阶梯对监督源进行分类，而不是对整个培训渠道进行分类。标准 RLHF 跨越 L0 和 L1，例如，人类偏好提供 L0 监督，而由此产生的奖励模型充当可重用的 L1 评估器。这些级别之间的界限并不总是清晰的，因为一种方法可能会减少人类对一个组件的参与，同时将其保留在另一个组件中。例如，即使参考答案是由专家编写的，奖励计算也可以自动化。一种方法还可以在继续使用人类设计的环境的同时生成新任务。因此，我们根据学习循环中不太依赖人类输入的部分对每种方法进行分类，同时注意到它仍然需要人类输入。The ladder classifies supervision sources rather than entire training pipelines. Standard RLHF spans L0 and L1 e.g., human preferences provide L0 supervision, while the resulting reward model serves as a reusable L1 evaluator. The boundaries between these levels are not always sharp because a method may reduce human involvement in one component while retaining it in another. For example, reward computation can be automated even when the reference answer was written by an expert. A method may also generate new tasks while continuing to use an environment designed by humans. We therefore classify each method by the part of the learning loop that becomes less dependent on human input, while noting the human input it still requires.

远离人类直接监督的每一步也会引入不同的错误来源。基于模型置信度或一致性的奖励可能会加剧模型输出中共享的错误。当评估规则不完整时，基于测试或环境结果的奖励可能会助长意外行为。自动生成的任务和环境可能无效、太简单或太困难，而自我游戏可能会反复产生狭窄的经验范围。部分Each step away from direct human supervision also introduces a different source of error. Rewards based on model confidence or agreement can reinforce mistakes shared across the model’s outputs. Rewards based on tests or environment outcomes can favor unintended behavior when the evaluation rules are incomplete. Automatically generated tasks and environments may be invalid, too easy, or too difficult, while self-play may repeatedly produce a narrow range of experience. Sections3.4和and4.5检查这些故障模式以及建议的缓解机制。examine these failure modes together with the mechanisms proposed to mitigate them.

3. 超越人类评价的奖励3. Reward beyond Human Evaluation

第节介绍的奖励轴The reward axis introduced in Section2.5涉及用于评估推出的证据。在每个实例的人类监督中，每个任务都带有人类提供的答案、偏好或接受标准。以人为本的评估将此类判断转变为可重用的评估器，可以对许多输出进行评分。超越人类评估的奖励更进一步，通过从模型、可用参考或环境中可观察的后果中获取学习信号。这些制度的评估来源有所不同。策略是在线优化还是离线优化是单独的设计选择。concerns the evidence used to evaluate a rollout. At Per-Instance Human Supervision, each task comes with a human-provided answer, preference, or acceptance criterion. Human-Grounded Evaluation turns such judgments into reusable evaluators that can score many outputs. Reward beyond Human Evaluation goes one step further by deriving learning signals from the model, an available reference, or observable consequences in an environment. These regimes differ in the source of evaluation. Whether the policy is optimized online or offline is a separate design choice.

3.1. 每个实例的人工监督3.1. Per-Instance Human Supervision

每个实例的人工监督将每个训练实例与人员或专家提供的评估目标相关联。在基于偏好的训练中，该目标可能是比较或质量判断，而可验证的任务可能使用参考答案、正式规范或特定于任务的测试套件。这个状态对应于我们阶梯中的 L0。一旦目标可用，奖励计算就可以自动进行。然而，将训练扩展到新任务需要新目标，因此仅自动评分并不能消除每个实例的监督Per-instance human supervision associates each training instance with an evaluation target supplied by people or experts. In preference-based training, this target may be a comparison or quality judgment, while verifiable tasks may use a reference answer, formal specification, or task-specific test suite. This regime corresponds to L0 in our ladder. Once the target is available, reward computation can be automatic. Extending training to new tasks nevertheless requires new targets, so automatic scoring alone does not remove per-instance supervision(Ouyang et al., 2022; Guo et al., 2025a).

深度探索数学DeepSeekMath(Shao et al., 2024)和 DeepSeek-R1and DeepSeek-R1(Guo et al., 2025a)当输出可以自动检查时，证明基于规则的奖励的有效性。可以提取数学答案，并通过等价或匹配规则与真实答案进行比较。生成的程序可以根据预定义的测试进行编译和执行。这种方法推动了数学推理的实质性进展demonstrate the effectiveness of rule-based rewards when outputs can be checked automatically. Mathematical answers can be extracted and compared with a ground-truth answer through equivalence or matching rules. Generated programs can be compiled and executed against predefined tests. This approach has driven substantial progress in mathematical reasoning(Yu et al., 2025c; Lin et al., 2025b; Mu et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2026c)和代码生成and code generation(Guo et al., 2025a).

随着经验量和难度的增加，局限性就会显现出来。每一项新任务仍然需要一个答案、测试、偏好或可比较的评估标准。专家领域使得这些目标的生成成本高昂，而开放式任务则带来了更深层次的困难，因为在依赖于上下文的标准下，多个响应可能是有效的。因此，将奖励扩大到超出此范围需要在许多情况下重复使用人类判断，或者从不同的来源（对应于 L1 和 L2）进行评估。The limitation appears as the amount and difficulty of experience increase. Every new task still needs an answer, test, preference, or comparable evaluation criterion. Expert domains make these targets expensive to produce, and open-ended tasks present a deeper difficulty because several responses may be valid under context-dependent standards. Scaling reward beyond this regime consequently requires either reusing human judgment across many instances or drawing evaluation from a different source, corresponding to L1 and L2.

3.2. 以人为本的评估3.2. Human-Grounded Evaluation

在 L1，人类偏好、专家注释或明确的标准被编码在可重用的评估器中。学习奖励模型At L1, human preferences, expert annotations, or explicit criteria are encoded in a reusable evaluator. A learned reward modelℛϕ\mathcal{R}_{\phi}或LLM法官or LLM judgeπv\pi_{v}可以在许多查询中应用相同的标准can apply the same standard across many queries𝒒\bm{q} (Ouyang et al., 2022; Zhu et al., 2025a)。与精确匹配不同，这些评估者可以分配评分、评估中间推理并评估答案，而无需唯一的参考答案。它们的范围比特定任务的规则更广泛，但它们的标准仍然以人们提供的数据和标准为基础。 DeepSeekMath V2 在自然语言定理证明中阐释了这种机制，其中生成验证器评估正确性、完整性和严谨性，并奖励解决已识别的缺陷. Unlike exact matching, these evaluators can assign graded scores, assess intermediate reasoning, and evaluate responses without a unique reference answer. Their reach is broader than that of task-specific rules, but their standards remain grounded in the data and criteria provided by people. DeepSeekMath V2 illustrates this regime in natural-language theorem proving, where a generative verifier assesses correctness, completeness, and rigor and rewards the resolution of identified flaws(Shao et al., 2025b).

对学习评估器的需求取决于评估程序，而不是领域之间的固定划分。一些任务实例允许廉价的确定性检查，包括简短的数学、可执行代码和基于约束的谜题The need for a learned evaluator depends on the evaluation procedure, not on a fixed division between domains. Some task instances admit inexpensive deterministic checks, including short-form mathematics, executable code, and constraint-based puzzles(Cobbe et al., 2021; Chen et al., 2021; Lin et al., 2025a)。完全开放式的生成通常缺乏独特的目标和基于规则的检查器，因此评估依赖于人类偏好或学习代理. Fully open-ended generation often lacks both a unique target and a rule-based checker, so evaluation relies on human preferences or learned proxies(Ouyang et al., 2022; Stiennon et al., 2020; Zheng et al., 2023)。在这些情况之间是半可验证的任务，支持部分检查、可验证的重新制定、特定于指令的标准或参考条件评分. Between these cases are semi-verifiable tasks that support partial checking, verifiable reformulation, instruction-specific criteria, or reference-conditioned scoring(Tang et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025e; Viswanathan et al., 2025; Zhou et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025d; Liu et al., 2025h)。由于评估更加依赖于学习代理，偏见、错误指定、奖励黑客和过度优化变得更加严重. As evaluation relies more heavily on learned proxies, bias, misspecification, reward hacking, and overoptimization become more consequential(Casper et al., 2023; Skalse et al., 2022; Gao et al., 2023).

3.2.1. 结果和过程奖励模型3.2.1. Outcome and Process Reward Models

学习奖励模型可以通过部分信用或过程级反馈来补充稀疏结果检查。当正确的响应可能以意想不到的形式出现或当最终结果几乎无法揭示推理跟踪失败的位置时，这非常有用。过程奖励模型和逐步批评最初是为了数学推理而广泛开发的Learned reward models can supplement sparse outcome checks with partial-credit or process-level feedback. This is useful when a correct response may appear in an unexpected form or when the final outcome reveals little about where a reasoning trace failed. Process reward models and step-wise critiques were first developed extensively for mathematical reasoning(Uesato et al., 2022; Wang et al., 2024a; Lightman et al., 2023)。他们的核心挑战是在没有详尽的人工注释的情况下获得可靠的步骤标签。. Their central challenge is to obtain reliable step labels without exhaustive human annotation.

Math-Shepherd 通过自动流程注释解决了这个问题Math-Shepherd addresses this problem through automatic process annotation(Wang et al., 2024a)。对于推理前缀. For a reasoning prefix𝒛≤t\bm{z}_{\leq t}，更完整的模型样本, a completer model samplesNN延续，该步骤接收达到参考答案的分数continuations, and the step receives the fraction that reach the reference answer𝒚∗\bm{y}^{*}:

ℛ(𝒛≤t)=1N∑j=1N𝕀{𝒚j=𝒚∗},𝒚j∼πθ(⋅∣𝒒,𝒛≤t).\mathcal{R}(\bm{z}_{\leq t})=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{I}\{\bm{y}_{j}=\bm{y}^{*}\},\qquad\bm{y}_{j}\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{q},\bm{z}_{\leq t}). (10)

这种蒙特卡罗估计消除了每个步骤的直接人工注释，但仍然锚定于最终目标。 PRIME 通过从结果标签中导出隐式过程奖励并通过策略推出更新奖励模型来消除单独的注释阶段This Monte Carlo estimate removes direct human annotation at each step but remains anchored to the final target. PRIME removes the separate annotation stage by deriving an implicit process reward from outcome labels and updating the reward model with policy rollouts(Cui et al., 2025a)。比较研究表明，自动生成的步骤标签仍然落后于 LLM 判断或人工注释，尽管共识过滤可以提高其可靠性. Comparative studies show that automatically generated step labels can still lag behind LLM judgments or human annotations, although consensus filtering can improve their reliability(Zhang et al., 2025n)。相关工作将过程奖励扩展到各个领域，并开发基于推理或熵正则化的变体. Related work extends process rewards across domains and develops reasoning-based or entropy-regularized variants(Zeng et al., 2025b; She et al., 2025b; Zhang et al., 2024a).

生成验证者将评估制定为下一个令牌预测，并在做出判断之前进行分析Generative verifiers formulate evaluation as next-token prediction and produce an analysis before issuing a judgment(Zhang et al., 2025d)。长推理和生成 PRM 在步骤级别应用此公式. Long-reasoning and generative PRMs apply this formulation at the step level(Zhao et al., 2026; Khalifa et al., 2025)。 xVerify 和 TinyV 等紧凑型系统专注于基于规则的匹配的答案提取、语义等价和漏报. Compact systems such as xVerify and TinyV focus on answer extraction, semantic equivalence, and false negatives from rule-based matching(Chen et al., 2025a; Xu et al., 2025c)。 CompassVerifier 扩展了跨域的答案检查. CompassVerifier extends answer checking across domains(Liu et al., 2025g)，而 RL Tango 联合训练生成器和生成过程验证器，并为两个组件提供结果奖励, while RL Tango jointly trains a generator and a generative process verifier with outcome rewards for both components(Zha et al., 2025)。同样的方法现在涵盖了多模式推理和多轮代理。多模态 PRM 得分为中级推理. The same approach now covers multimodal reasoning and multi-turn agents. Multimodal PRMs score intermediate reasoning(Zhao et al., 2026; Wang et al., 2025h; Chen et al., 2025h)，多模式奖励模型评估已完成的产出, multimodal reward models assess completed outputs(Wang et al., 2025j; Zhang et al., 2025m; Wang et al., 2026a)，代理验证者评估工具使用轨迹, and agentic verifiers evaluate tool-use trajectories(Zhang et al., 2026a; Chae et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025a).

有几个系统训练涵盖可验证任务和开放式任务的验证者。 Seed1.5 Thinking 评估回答是否与参考文献一致，并在精确匹配不充分时提供分级反馈Several systems train verifiers that span both verifiable and open-ended tasks. Seed1.5 Thinking evaluates whether responses align with references and supplies graded feedback when exact matching is inadequate(Seed et al., 2025)。 General Reasoner 在源自 WebInstruct 的合成候选对象上训练紧凑的生成验证器. General Reasoner trains a compact generative verifier on synthetic candidates derived from WebInstruct(Ma et al., 2025; Yue et al., 2024b)。 StructVRM 表示奖励向量的部分正确性，其维度对应于子问题. StructVRM represents partial correctness with a reward vector whose dimensions correspond to subquestions(Zhang et al., 2025h)。后验 GRPO 为代码添加了推理质量奖励，但仅在最终程序正确时才应用它. Posterior GRPO adds a reasoning-quality reward for code but applies it only when the final program is correct(Fan et al., 2026)。这些方法共同表明，学习验证可以丰富可用目标，而无需改变目标的最终来源。. Together, these methods show that learned verification can enrich an available target without changing where the target ultimately originates.

一般奖励模型通过学习跨任务的偏好来采用更广泛的方法。 Skywork Reward 对精心策划的偏好对使用有区别的 Bradley-Terry 目标General reward models take a broader approach by learning preferences across tasks. Skywork Reward uses a discriminative Bradley-Terry objective over curated preference pairs(Liu et al., 2024a)，而 Nemotron Reward 则预测有用性、正确性、连贯性、复杂性和冗长等属性, while Nemotron Reward predicts attributes such as helpfulness, correctness, coherence, complexity, and verbosity(Adler et al., 2024)。面向推理的模型在分配偏好或分数之前生成分析。例如，RM R1 将推理蒸馏与 RLVR 相结合. Reasoning-oriented models generate an analysis before assigning a preference or score. For example, RM R1 combines reasoning distillation with RLVR(Chen et al., 2025g)，J1通过正确性和一致性奖励优化判断推理, J1 optimizes judgment reasoning through correctness and consistency rewards(Whitehouse et al., 2025)，并且RRM通过更长的推理或响应聚合分配额外的推理计算, and RRM allocates additional inference computation through longer reasoning or response aggregation(Guo et al., 2025b)。认为 RM、S2J 和 CAMEL 分别训练长式奖励推理，将问题解决与判断结合起来，并有选择地调用反射来进行不确定的比较. Think RM, S2J, and CAMEL respectively train long-form reward reasoning, couple problem solving with judging, and invoke reflection selectively for uncertain comparisons(Hong et al., 2025; Sun et al., 2025c; Zhu et al., 2026b)。相反，GRAM 在偏好调整之前对未标记的输入响应对进行预训练，并在没有显式长推理跟踪的情况下预测标签标记. GRAM instead pretrains on unlabeled input-response pairs before preference tuning and predicts a label token without an explicit long reasoning trace(Wang et al., 2025a).

奖励模型的开发还取决于数据规模和模式。 UnifiedReward 和 R1 Reward 将奖励模型扩展到图像和视频理解和生成Reward-model development also depends on data scale and modality. UnifiedReward and R1 Reward extend reward modeling to image and video understanding and generation(Wang et al., 2025l; Zhang et al., 2025l)。 Skywork Reward V2 在大型过滤偏好集合上进行训练. Skywork Reward V2 trains on a large filtered preference collection(Liu et al., 2025c)，而 HelpSteer3 Preference 支持带有人工注释的判别性和生成性奖励建模, while HelpSteer3 Preference supports both discriminative and generative reward modeling with human annotations(Wang et al., 2025m)。 Prometheus 2 将标题条件评估与成对排名相结合并产生口头反馈，尽管它主要作为开放评估器进行评估. Prometheus 2 combines rubric-conditioned assessment with pairwise ranking and produces verbal feedback, although it is primarily evaluated as an open evaluator(Kim et al., 2024b)。 POLAR 采取了不同的路线，通过在相同策略和不同策略轨迹对上预训练参考条件策略鉴别器，然后将其与人类定义的标准对齐. POLAR takes a different route by pretraining a reference-conditioned policy discriminator on same-policy and different-policy trajectory pairs before aligning it with human-defined criteria(Dou et al., 2025).

特定任务的奖励模型在更窄的约束下实例化了这些想法。自我原则批评调整生成特定于任务的原则和批评，然后使用基于规则的在线强化学习优化他们的判断Task-specific reward models instantiate these ideas under narrower constraints. Self-Principled Critique Tuning generates task-specific principles and critiques, then optimizes their judgments with rule-based online RL(Liu et al., 2025j; Bai et al., 2022)。写作零采用相同的程序来评估创意写作. Writing-Zero adapts the same procedure to the evaluation of creative writing(Lu, 2025)。对于以下说明，Ren 等人。. For instruction following, Ren et al.(Ren et al., 2025a)将硬约束的编程检查与软约束的学习二进制模型结合起来。 EvolvR 综合分数对齐的比较原理，并迭代检查、完善和攻击自己的判断combine programmatic checks for hard constraints with a learned binary model for soft constraints. EvolvR synthesizes score-aligned comparison rationales and iteratively checks, refines, and attacks its own judgments(Wang et al., 2025i)。在计算机使用中，SEAgent 训练世界状态模型来描述 GUI 变化并在完整轨迹上提供行动级别的奖励. In computer use, SEAgent trains a World State Model to describe GUI changes and provide action-level rewards over complete trajectories(Sun et al., 2025d).

3.2.2. 法学硕士法官和基于评分标准的评估员3.2.2. LLM Judges and Rubric-Based Evaluators

开放式评估通常同时需要多个标准。事实回答可能需要主张分解和证据检查，而写作和对话则需要对相关性、连贯性、风格和有用性进行判断Open-ended evaluation often requires several criteria at once. Factual responses may need claim decomposition and evidence checking, while writing and dialogue require judgments about relevance, coherence, style, and usefulness(Min et al., 2023; Lin et al., 2022; Fein et al., 2025)。法学硕士法官提供了一种可扩展的方式来应用此类标准。 RLAIF 使用 LLM 生成奖励模型训练的偏好标签或在策略优化期间直接提供奖励. LLM judges provide a scalable way to apply such criteria. RLAIF uses an LLM to generate preference labels for reward-model training or to supply rewards directly during policy optimization(Lee et al., 2023)。相关评估器涵盖自由格式文本、推理、创意写作和任务相关的 RLHF. Related evaluators cover free-form text, reasoning, creative writing, and task-dependent RLHF(Gu et al., 2024b; Badshah & Sajjad, 2024; Saha et al., 2025; Fein et al., 2025; Dong et al., 2024).

基于参考的 LLM 验证者可以颁发二元或分级奖励。苏等人。Reference-based LLM verifiers can issue binary or graded rewards. Su et al.(Su et al., 2025)研究收到提示的验证者study verifiers that receive a promptxx, 一个参考, a referenceaa，以及最后一步, and the final stepyiTy_{i}^{T}的回应。他们的二元奖励是of a response. Their binary reward is

rϕbin(x,a,yi)=𝕀[ci=1],ci∼πϕ(⋅∣x,a,yiT),r_{\phi}^{\mathrm{bin}}(x,a,y_{i})=\mathbb{I}[c_{i}=1],\qquad c_{i}\sim\pi_{\phi}(\cdot\mid x,a,y_{i}^{T}), (11)

而软奖励是while the soft reward is

rϕsoft​(x,a,yi)={πϕ​(1∣x,a,yiT),ci=1,1−πϕ​(0∣x,a,yiT),ci=0,0,otherwise.r_{\phi}^{\mathrm{soft}}(x,a,y_{i})=\begin{cases}\pi_{\phi}(1\mid x,a,y_{i}^{T}),&c_{i}=1,\\ 1-\pi_{\phi}(0\mid x,a,y_{i}^{T}),&c_{i}=0,\\ 0,&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases} (12)

VDS-TTT 使用预训练验证器仅在其分数超过置信阈值时选择候选者进行测试时训练VDS-TTT uses a pretrained verifier to select a candidate for test-time training only when its score exceeds a confidence threshold(Moradi et al., 2025)。 SSR-Zero 在机器翻译中的演员和法官角色之间交替使用相同的预训练模型，然后将判断重新调整为政策奖励. SSR-Zero alternates the same pretrained model between actor and judge roles in machine translation, then rescales the judgments as policy rewards(Yang et al., 2025c).

评估者还可以根据比单独文本更丰富的证据进行操作。 ZeroGUI 用接收任务指令和轨迹屏幕截图的视觉语言奖励估计器取代了手工制作的成功脚本The evaluator can also operate on richer evidence than text alone. ZeroGUI replaces handcrafted success scripts with a vision-language reward estimator that receives task instructions and trajectory screenshots(Yang et al., 2025b)。它排除了代理成功的文本声明，并需要对重复查询进行一致判断，从而减少误报奖励。对于长篇事实推理，可以将声明级验证与参考答案的单独比较相结合，以评估整体相关性和质量. It excludes the agent’s textual claim of success and requires unanimous judgments across repeated queries, which reduces false-positive rewards. For long-form factual reasoning, claim-level verification can be combined with a separate comparison against a reference answer to assess overall relevance and quality(Chen et al., 2025f).

细则和清单在应用于候选输出之前使多维标准变得明确。 Omni Thinker 将基于规则的奖励与符合标准的对话和写作偏好相结合Rubrics and checklists make multidimensional standards explicit before they are applied to candidate outputs. Omni Thinker combines rule-based rewards with rubric-aligned preferences for dialogue and writing(Li et al., 2025a)。 RLCF 提取特定于指令的检查表，并使用人工智能法官或专门程序评估每个项目. RLCF extracts instruction-specific checklists and evaluates each item with an AI judge or specialized program(Viswanathan et al., 2025)。 RaR 根据专家答案或强有力的参考文献综合提示特定的评分标准. RaR synthesizes prompt-specific rubrics from expert answers or strong references(Gunjal et al., 2025)，而 Rubicon 将人类生成和模型生成的标准与针对重复奖励漏洞的防御结合起来, while Rubicon combines human-generated and model-generated criteria with defenses against recurring reward exploits(Huang et al., 2025d)。 RuscaRL 使用清单来评分和指导生成，然后逐渐减少指导. RuscaRL uses checklists both to score and guide generation, then gradually reduces the guidance(Zhou et al., 2025d).

标准不必保持固定，因为标题结构本身可以变得适应性强。 Kimi K2 在数据合成和策略优化过程中使用明确的成功、工具使用和评估标准The criteria need not remain fixed because rubric construction can itself become adaptive. Kimi K2 uses explicit success, tool-use, and evaluation criteria during data synthesis and policy optimization(Team et al., 2025a)。 OpenRubrics 通过对比首选和拒绝的响应得出特定于提示的标准. OpenRubrics derives prompt-specific criteria by contrasting preferred and rejected responses(Liu et al., 2026b)。 EvoRubrics 联合优化策略和 rubric 生成器. EvoRubrics jointly optimizes the policy and rubric generator(Ding et al., 2026)，而 Rubric ARM 和 RUBRIC ARROW 在训练 rubric 生成器和 rubric 条件判断器之间交替, while Rubric ARM and RUBRIC ARROW alternate between training rubric generators and rubric-conditioned judges(Xu et al., 2026a; Jiang et al., 2026a)。 AMARIS 在政策培训期间使用推出分析的持久记忆修改了规则. AMARIS revises rubrics during policy training using a persistent memory of rollout analyses(Wu et al., 2026b)。当逐点评分无法可靠区分相同查询的推出时，Tournament-GRPO 和 ArenaRL 将重复的相对比较转换为分组奖励. When pointwise rubric scores cannot distinguish same-query rollouts reliably, Tournament-GRPO and ArenaRL convert repeated relative comparisons into group-wise rewards(Yang et al., 2026b; Zhang et al., 2026b).

当一般评估员缺乏领域知识时，专业法官可以提高可靠性。百川 M2 将患者模拟器与标题生成器相结合，并评估多个回合的医疗反应Specialized judges can improve reliability when a general evaluator lacks domain knowledge. Baichuan M2 combines a patient simulator with a rubric generator and evaluates medical responses across multiple turns(Team et al., 2025b)。好处来自特定领域的证据和标准，尽管由此产生的奖励仍然以人为本，因为医疗记录、对话和标题标准决定了评估者学会评估的内容。. The benefit comes from domain-specific evidence and criteria, although the resulting reward remains human-grounded because medical records, conversations, and rubric standards determine what the evaluator learns to value.

3.2.3. 自训练的评估者和边界情况3.2.3. Self-Trained Evaluators and Boundary Cases

自训练的评估者介于基于人为的评估和未经人为评估而获得的奖励之间。自我奖励语言模型使用相同的模型来生成响应并对其进行判断，然后对由得分最高和最低得分的候选者组成的对进行训练Self-trained evaluators sit between human-grounded evaluation and rewards obtained without human evaluation. Self-Rewarding Language Models use the same model to generate responses and judge them, then train on pairs formed from the highest- and lowest-scoring candidates(Yuan et al., 2024)。元奖励添加了一个元法官，可以比较采样的判断并与演员一起训练判断角色. Meta-Rewarding adds a meta-judge that compares sampled judgments and trains the judging role alongside the actor(Wu et al., 2025d)。自学评估器从未标记的人类编写的指令开始，构建首选和较差的响应，保留其结论与合成标签匹配的推理痕迹，并迭代地微调评估器. Self-Taught Evaluators begin with unlabeled human-written instructions, construct preferred and inferior responses, retain reasoning traces whose verdict matches the synthetic label, and iteratively fine-tune the evaluator(Wang et al., 2024b)。基于过程的自我奖励将相同的想法应用于步骤级别的数学偏好. Process-based Self-Rewarding applies the same idea to step-level mathematical preferences(Zhang et al., 2025g)。这些方法从评估者训练循环的不同部分中删除了标记的偏好，但许多方法保留了监督初始化、人工编写的提示或人工定义的判断格式。因此，他们在阶梯上的位置取决于持续锚定评估者的证据。. These methods remove labeled preferences from different parts of the evaluator-training loop, but many retain supervised initialization, human-written prompts, or human-defined judging formats. Their position on the ladder consequently depends on the evidence that continues to anchor the evaluator.

政策优化不提供第二个阶梯维度。 DPO、IPO、SimPO 和 ORPO 从固定偏好对中学习，而 KTO 使用逐点期望或不良反馈Policy optimization does not provide a second ladder dimension. DPO, IPO, SimPO, and ORPO learn from fixed preference pairs, while KTO uses pointwise desirable or undesirable feedback(Rafailov et al., 2023; Azar et al., 2024; Ethayarajh et al., 2024; Meng et al., 2024; Hong et al., 2024)。这些对可能来自人、可重复使用的判断者或自行生成的比较。 SPIN、ReST 和自我奖励语言模型在刷新和过滤策略数据的方式上同样有所不同. The pairs may come from people, reusable judges, or self-generated comparisons. SPIN, ReST, and Self-Rewarding Language Models likewise differ in how they refresh and filter policy data(Chen et al., 2024f; Gulcehre et al., 2023; Yuan et al., 2024)。离线重用减少了优化过程中评估器的查询，但冻结了反馈功能及其数据支持，这可能会随着策略的变化而导致覆盖范围很差. Offline reuse reduces evaluator queries during optimization but freezes the feedback function and its data support, which can leave poor coverage as the policy changes(Casper et al., 2023; Xiong et al., 2024)。因此，线上和线下目标描述的是如何使用反馈，而不是其评估标准的来源。. For this reason, online and offline objectives describe how feedback is consumed, not where its evaluative standard originates.

以人为本的评估人员减少了每次推出时都需要做出新的判断。然而，他们的标准仍然可以追溯到人们提供的偏好、注释、标题或专家标准。超越这种依赖性需要奖励信号，其直接证据来自另一个来源。Human-grounded evaluators reduce the need for a new judgment on every rollout. However, their criteria still trace back to preferences, annotations, rubrics, or expert standards supplied by people. Moving beyond this dependence requires reward signals whose immediate evidence comes from another source.

3.3. 超越人类评估的奖励来源3.3. Reward Sources beyond Human Evaluation

在 L2 阶段，奖励不再主要取决于受过训练以重现人类判断的评估者。在这个制度中，我们区分了三种证据来源。模型导出的信号使用策略或其采样输出的属性，包括确定性和一致性。参考锚定信号测量推理轨迹如何支持或重建可用目标。基于环境的信号评估可观察的后果，例如执行、正式接受或游戏结果。At L2, the reward no longer depends primarily on an evaluator trained to reproduce human judgments. Within this regime, we distinguish three sources of evidence. Model-derived signals use properties of the policy or its sampled outputs, including certainty and agreement. Reference-anchored signals measure how a reasoning trajectory supports or reconstructs an available target. Environment-grounded signals evaluate observable consequences such as execution, formal acceptance, or game outcomes.

这些类别涉及对首次展示进行评分时使用的证据，而不是管道中每个工件的历史起源。参考文献可能来自人类编写的语料库，人们可能设计了测试、工具或游戏规则These categories concern the evidence used when a rollout is scored, not the historical origin of every artifact in the pipeline. References may come from human-authored corpora, and people may have designed tests, tools, or game rules(Zhou et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025d; Liu et al., 2025h; Gehring et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2025a)。因此，剩余监督的数量取决于证据的可重复使用程度。为每个任务编写的单独答案仍然接近于每个实例的监督，而固定的环境动态可以评估许多新的轨迹。检索占据类似的边界，因为搜索提供了证据，但最终奖励可能仍然使用已知的答案或任务规则. The amount of remaining supervision consequently depends on how reusable that evidence is. A separate answer written for every task remains close to per-instance supervision, while fixed environment dynamics can evaluate many new trajectories. Retrieval occupies a similar boundary because search supplies evidence, but the terminal reward may still use a known answer or task rule(Jin et al., 2025; Song et al., 2025a).

3.3.1. 模型导出的信号3.3.1. Model-Derived Signals

内在的确定性。Intrinsic certainty.

内在确定性将模型的预测状态映射到标量奖励，而无需在评分时查阅参考答案。它的有用性取决于置信度和正确性之间的统计关系，而不仅仅是置信度。不确定性是比任何单个估计器更广泛的概念，因为序列似然、标记熵、语义熵和言语置信度描述不同的对象，并且不需要一致地对输出进行排序Intrinsic certainty maps the model’s predictive state to a scalar reward without consulting a reference answer at scoring time. Its usefulness depends on a statistical relationship between confidence and correctness, not on confidence alone. Uncertainty is a broader concept than any single estimator because sequence likelihood, token entropy, semantic entropy, and verbalized confidence describe different objects and need not rank outputs consistently(Liu et al., 2025i; Shorinwa et al., 2025)。 SLiC 使用参考校准序列似然. SLiC calibrates sequence likelihood with references(Zhao et al., 2023)，而 UaIT 从概率导出的伪目标中训练言语置信度, while UaIT trains verbalized confidence from probability-derived pseudo-targets(Liu et al., 2024c)。纯粹的内在奖励有所不同，因为它们的推出分数并不锚定于目标答案、环境结果或以人为本的评估者。. Purely intrinsic rewards differ because their rollout scores are not anchored to a target answer, environment outcome, or human-grounded evaluator.

熵最小化在两个条件下变得信息丰富：基础模型必须已经将有用的概率质量放在好的解决方案上，并且其置信度必须与目标分布的正确性相关Entropy minimization becomes informative under two conditions: the base model must already place useful probability mass on good solutions, and its confidence must correlate with correctness on the target distribution(Agarwal et al., 2025)。 EM-RL 通过在序列或令牌级别计算的负熵奖励来实现这些条件。对于采样轨迹. EM-RL implements these conditions through negative-entropy rewards computed at either the sequence or token level. For a sampled trajectory𝐲=(y1,…,yT)∼πθ(⋅∣q)\mathbf{y}=(y_{1},\ldots,y_{T})\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid q)，其序列级奖励为, its sequence-level reward is

rseq​(q,𝐲)=log⁡πθ​(𝐲∣q)=∑t=1Tlog⁡πθ​(yt∣q,𝐲<t),r_{\mathrm{seq}}(q,\mathbf{y})=\log\pi_{\theta}(\mathbf{y}\mid q)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\pi_{\theta}(y_{t}\mid q,\mathbf{y}_{<t}),

其期望是负轨迹熵。令牌级变体累积完整的下一个令牌分布的负香农熵：whose expectation is the negative trajectory entropy. The token-level variant accumulates the negative Shannon entropy of the full next-token distribution:

rtok(q,𝐲)=−∑t=1TH(πθ(⋅∣q,𝐲<t)),H(πθ(⋅∣q,𝐲<t))=−∑v∈𝒱πθ(v∣q,𝐲<t)logπθ(v∣q,𝐲<t).r_{\mathrm{tok}}(q,\mathbf{y})=-\sum_{t=1}^{T}H\!\left(\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid q,\mathbf{y}_{<t})\right),\qquad H\!\left(\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid q,\mathbf{y}_{<t})\right)=-\sum_{v\in\mathcal{V}}\pi_{\theta}(v\mid q,\mathbf{y}_{<t})\log\pi_{\theta}(v\mid q,\mathbf{y}_{<t}).

两种变体都将结果奖励分配给完整的轨迹，但它们是从不同的预测分布中得出的。所报告的数学和代码方面的进步最好理解为激发基本策略已经代表的能力。他们没有证明低熵可以保证正确的推理Both variants assign an outcome reward to a complete trajectory, but they derive it from different predictive distributions. Reported gains in mathematics and code are best understood as eliciting capabilities already represented by the base policy. They do not establish that low entropy guarantees correct reasoning(Agarwal et al., 2025).

其他方法构建语义或标记空间的置信度。 EMPO 通过语义相似性进行集群推出，并根据集群质量奖励答案Other methods construct confidence in semantic or token space. EMPO clusters rollouts by semantic similarity and rewards answers according to cluster mass(Zhang et al., 2025f)。 RENT 和 RSLC 使用代币级熵或置信度对部署进行排名并平滑策略更新. RENT and RSLC use token-level entropy or confidence to rank rollouts and smooth policy updates(Prabhudesai et al., 2025; Li et al., 2025e)。 Intuitor 将自我确定性定义为均匀分布之间的平均散度. Intuitor defines self-certainty as the average divergence between a uniform distribution𝕌\mathbb{U}以及词汇表上的下一个标记分布and the next-token distribution over vocabulary𝒱\mathcal{V} (Zhao et al., 2025b; Kang et al., 2025):

ℛi=1|𝒐i|∑t=1|𝒐i|KL(𝕌∥πθ(⋅∣𝒐i,<t)).\mathcal{R}_{i}=\frac{1}{|\bm{o}_{i}|}\sum_{t=1}^{|\bm{o}_{i}|}\mathrm{KL}\!\left(\mathbb{U}\,\|\,\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{o}_{i,<t})\right). (13)

值越大表示偏离均匀性越大，并被解释为更高的确定性。 Shop-R1将此信号应用于网上购物A larger value indicates a greater departure from uniformity and is interpreted as higher certainty. Shop-R1 applies this signal in online shopping(Zhang et al., 2025j)，以及相关工作在视觉语言推理中研究它, and related work studies it in vision-language reasoning(Li et al., 2026f).

当缺乏真实答案时，信心奖励仍然可用，但优化可能会使它们越来越脱离正确性。熵最小化对基本模型的初始熵敏感，最终可能会产生过度自信、遗漏转换标记和退化推理Confidence rewards remain available when ground-truth answers are absent, but optimization can make them increasingly detached from correctness. Entropy minimization is sensitive to the base model’s initial entropy and may eventually produce overconfidence, omitted transition tokens, and degraded reasoning(Zhang et al., 2025k)。即使这些信号不用于计算训练奖励，这种限制也会激发校准和外部评估。. This limitation motivates calibration and external evaluation even when those signals are not used to compute the training reward.

熵也可以影响优化，但不会成为奖励来源。协方差感知裁剪、高熵标记选择和提示级语义熵加权利用不确定性来稳定或集中学习Entropy can also influence optimization without becoming the reward source. Covariance-aware clipping, high-entropy token selection, and prompt-level semantic-entropy weighting use uncertainty to stabilize or focus learning(Cui et al., 2025b; Wang et al., 2025g; Chen et al., 2025d)。其他方法将不确定性与外部正确性信号相结合。 ACE 惩罚过度自信的错误. Other methods combine uncertainty with an external correctness signal. ACE penalizes overconfident errors(Xu et al., 2026b)，并且校准的语义熵目标支持言语不确定性, and calibrated semantic-entropy targets support verbalized uncertainty(Jenane et al., 2026)。探索推理、EDGE-GRPO、RLCR、Archer 和 CoDaPO 在优势估计、校正、校准、令牌约束或数据选择中使用熵或置信度，同时保留基于验证者的奖励. Reasoning with Exploration, EDGE-GRPO, RLCR, Archer, and CoDaPO use entropy or confidence in advantage estimation, correction, calibration, token constraints, or data selection while retaining verifier-based rewards(Cheng et al., 2026; Zhang et al., 2025i; Damani et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2025d; Zhou et al., 2025f)。 ETTLL 使用共识伪标签和熵引导分支. ETTRL uses consensus pseudo-labels and entropy-guided branching(Liu et al., 2025d)，而 CURE 在高熵标记处分支并重新连接轨迹以保留探索, while CURE branches and reconnects trajectories at high-entropy tokens to preserve exploration(Li et al., 2025f)。由于这些方法保留了单独的正确性信号，因此它们是熵辅助的 RLVR 方法，而不是独立的确定性奖励。. Because these methods retain a separate correctness signal, they are entropy-assisted RLVR methods, not standalone certainty rewards.

这里的术语“内在”是指从模型的预测分布中得出的置信度。经典强化学习使用相同的术语来表示寻求新奇的信号，例如好奇心和基于计数的探索The term intrinsic here refers to confidence derived from the model’s predictive distribution. Classic RL uses the same term for novelty-seeking signals such as curiosity and count-based exploration(Pathak et al., 2017)。这两个目标可以施加相反的压力，因为信心集中了概率质量，而新颖性则鼓励政策访问不熟悉的国家。 EVOL RL 将语义新颖性与多数票选择相结合，以抵消纯置信信号下的崩溃. The two objectives can exert opposite pressures because confidence concentrates probability mass, while novelty encourages the policy to visit unfamiliar states. EVOL RL combines semantic novelty with majority-vote selection to counteract collapse under pure confidence signals(Wu et al., 2025c).

答案级共识。Answer-level consensus.

共识通过重复样本的一致意见来取代不可用的答案。 TTRL抽签Consensus replaces an unavailable answer key with agreement across repeated samples. TTRL drawsGG回应responses{𝒐i}i=1G\{\bm{o}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{G}对于一个问题for a question𝒒\bm{q}并提取答案and extracts answers{𝒚i}i=1G\{\bm{y}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{G} (Zuo et al., 2025b)。如果. If𝒜\mathcal{A}是一组不同的答案，大多数伪标签是is the set of distinct answers, the majority pseudo-label is

𝒚^=argmaxa∈𝒜∑i=1G𝕀{𝒚i=a},\hat{\bm{y}}=\arg\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\sum_{i=1}^{G}\mathbb{I}\!\left\{\bm{y}_{i}=a\right\}, (14)

每次推出都会收到and each rollout receives

ri=𝕀{𝒚i=𝒚^}.r_{i}=\mathbb{I}\!\left\{\bm{y}_{i}=\hat{\bm{y}}\right\}. (15)

伪标签遵循当前模型分布，并且可能是错误的。因此，共识提供了模型衍生的监督，但没有独立的正确性保证。The pseudo-label follows the current model distribution and can be wrong. Consensus thus provides model-derived supervision but no independent guarantee of correctness.

共识奖励已通过 MM-UPT 扩展到多模态推理Consensus rewards have been extended to multimodal reasoning through MM-UPT(Wei et al., 2025b)。随着政策学会利用自己的伪标签，多数投票自我训练最初可能会有所改善，然后就会崩溃. Majority-vote self-training can improve initially and then collapse as the policy learns to exploit its own pseudo-labels(Shafayat et al., 2025)。 SeRL 将共识纳入自玩任务生成管道中. SeRL incorporates consensus into a self-play task-generation pipeline(Fang et al., 2025b)，而 ETTLL 将其与熵引导的推出探索结合起来, while ETTRL couples it with entropy-guided rollout exploration(Liu et al., 2025d)。共同奖励增加了语义相关问题和缓慢更新的教师之间的一致性. Co-Rewarding adds agreement across semantically related questions and a slowly updated teacher(Zhang et al., 2025o).

当协议不可靠时，有几种方法可以改进硬多数投票。 SCOPE 将阶梯级置信度与小组共识相结合Several methods refine hard majority voting when agreement is unreliable. SCOPE combines step-level confidence with subgroup consensus(Wang et al., 2026b)，而 SCRL 过滤不确定的正伪标签并引入熵门控负标签, while SCRL filters uncertain positive pseudo-labels and introduces entropy-gated negative labels(Yan et al., 2026)。 TTRL-Guard 可检测到正确答案何时从投票分布中消失，并在错误多数变得根深蒂固之前进行干预. TTRL-Guard detects when correct answers disappear from the vote distribution and intervenes before an incorrect majority becomes entrenched(Lin et al., 2026a)。尽管奖励仍然与模型自身的答案分布相关，但这些保护措施减少了确认偏差。. These safeguards reduce confirmation bias, although the reward remains tied to the model’s own answer distribution.

轨迹级共识。Trajectory-level consensus.

最终答案协议会丢弃有关如何生成解决方案的信息。自洽可以提高推理准确性Final-answer agreement discards information about how a solution was produced. Self-consistency can improve inference accuracy(Wang et al., 2023c)，但仅回答统计数据仍然容易受到奖励剥削, but answer-only statistics remain vulnerable to reward exploitation(Zhang et al., 2025c)。 CoVo 比较从中间状态到轨迹答案以及从其他轨迹得出的答案的似然距离. CoVo compares likelihood distances from intermediate states to the trajectory’s answer and to answers drawn from other trajectories(Zhang et al., 2025c)。它衡量轨迹支持其自身答案的一致性以及竞争答案保持合理的时间。跨轨迹汇总这些统计数据可以提供比多数投票更丰富的奖励，并且据报道可以提高稳定性。然而，统计数据仍然来自政策，并不能独立证明其正确性。. It measures how consistently a trajectory supports its own answer and how long competing answers remain plausible. Aggregating these statistics across trajectories gives a richer reward than majority vote and is reported to improve stability. However, the statistics still come from the policy and do not independently certify correctness.

3.3.2. 参考锚定信号3.3.2. Reference-Anchored Signals

参考锚定方法保留可用目标Reference-anchored methods retain an available target𝒚∗\bm{y}^{*}但避免每次推出时都配备经过单独培训的评估员。他们不只依赖于精确的答案匹配，而是衡量采样的推理轨迹如何but avoid a separately trained evaluator for every rollout. Instead of relying only on exact answer matching, they measure how a sampled reasoning trace𝒛\bm{z}更改目标的概率、重建或度量分数changes the probability, reconstruction, or metric score of the target(Zhou et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025d; Liu et al., 2025h)。参考文献提供了外部证据，可以防止直接的自我证实。同时，它可能继承大量的人类或专家监督，并且单个目标可能仅覆盖可接受答案空间的一部分。. The reference supplies external evidence and can prevent direct self-confirmation. At the same time, it may inherit substantial human or expert supervision, and a single target may cover only part of the acceptable answer space.

变化的目标。Variational objectives.

一个家庭将推理轨迹视为潜在变量，并优化以下结果的边际可能性：One family treats the reasoning trace as a latent variable and optimizes the marginal likelihood of𝒚∗\bm{y}^{*}。 TRICE 使用 MCMC EM 程序从基本原理的答案条件后验中进行采样. TRICE uses an MCMC EM procedure to sample from an answer-conditioned posterior over rationales(Phan et al., 2023)。 LaTRO 奖励答案对数似然，并将基本原理策略规范化为冻结的预优化模型. LaTRO rewards answer log-likelihood and regularizes the rationale policy toward a frozen pre-optimization model(Chen et al., 2024c)。 JEPO 将 Jensen 不等式应用于潜在理性分解，并获得用于推理的策略梯度项以及类似 SFT 的答案预测项. JEPO applies Jensen’s inequality to the latent-rationale factorization and obtains a policy-gradient term for reasoning together with an SFT-like answer-prediction term(Tang et al., 2025)。其单样本核心为. Its one-sample core is

log⁡πθ​(𝒚∗∣𝒒)\displaystyle\log\pi_{\theta}(\bm{y}^{*}\mid\bm{q}) :=log𝔼𝒛∼πθ(⋅∣𝒒)[πθ(𝒚∗∣𝒒,𝒛)]\displaystyle:=\log\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{q})}\!\left[\pi_{\theta}(\bm{y}^{*}\mid\bm{q},\bm{z})\right] (16)
≥ℒJ(1)(θ):=𝔼𝒛∼πθ(⋅∣𝒒)[logπθ(𝒚∗∣𝒒,𝒛)].\displaystyle\geq\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{J}}^{(1)}(\theta):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}\sim\pi_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{q})}\!\left[\log\pi_{\theta}(\bm{y}^{*}\mid\bm{q},\bm{z})\right].

该表达式是 Jensen 下界。一般的 ELBO 将引入答案条件变分后验和先验后验比。 JEPO 进一步收紧方程。This expression is a Jensen lower bound. A general ELBO would introduce an answer-conditioned variational posterior and a prior-posterior ratio. JEPO further tightens Eq.16具有多样本对数均值概率界限，并在训练期间添加前向 KL 正则化。with a multi-sample log-mean-probability bound and adds forward-KL regularization during training.

可能性和概率奖励。Likelihood and probability rewards.

直接似然法避免了显式变分机制。 VeriFree 奖励条件概率Direct likelihood methods avoid explicit variational machinery. VeriFree rewards the conditional probabilityπθ​(𝒚∗∣𝒒,𝒛)\pi_{\theta}(\bm{y}^{*}\mid\bm{q},\bm{z})采样迹线后的参考值of the reference after a sampled trace(Zhou et al., 2025b)。在唯一答案字符串和精确匹配下，其 Rao-Blackwellized 估计器与对答案进行采样并应用二进制验证器的估计器相比，没有更大的方差。 RLPR 平均参考标记概率，减去在没有采样轨迹的情况下计算的分数，并截断差异. Under a unique answer string and exact matching, its Rao-Blackwellized estimator has no greater variance than an estimator that samples an answer and applies a binary verifier. RLPR averages reference-token probabilities, subtracts a score computed without the sampled trace, and clips the difference(Yu et al., 2025d)。减法控制基线关联，但不识别迹线的因果效应。对于长参考，对数概率奖励通常比精确序列概率更实用，后者的概率可能会消失。即便如此，明显的收益可能部分反映了较短的轨迹，而不是一致更好的推理. The subtraction controls a baseline association but does not identify a causal effect of the trace. Log-probability rewards are generally more practical than exact sequence probabilities for long references, whose probabilities can vanish. Even then, apparent gains may partly reflect shorter traces instead of uniformly better reasoning(Kwiatkowski et al., 2026)。 DARL 添加了阈值多样性术语，以鼓励参考一致变体之间的受控探索. DARL adds a thresholded diversity term to encourage controlled exploration among reference-consistent variants(Huang et al., 2026a).

困惑奖励。Perplexity rewards.

NOVER 将参考条件的困惑度转化为组内排名奖励NOVER converts reference-conditioned perplexity into a within-group ranking reward(Liu et al., 2025h)。给定. Given𝒒\bm{q}, 𝒛\bm{z}, 和, and𝒚∗\bm{y}^{*}，同步代理模型计算, a synchronized proxy model computes

Pr=exp⁡(−∑ilog⁡πp​(yi∗∣𝒒,𝒛,𝒚<i∗)|𝒚∗|​N​(|𝒛|)),N⁡(|𝒛|)=max⁡(1,1+log⁡|𝒛|).P_{r}=\exp\!\left(-\frac{\sum_{i}\log\pi_{\mathrm{p}}(y_{i}^{*}\mid\bm{q},\bm{z},\bm{y}_{<i}^{*})}{|\bm{y}^{*}|N(|\bm{z}|)}\right),\qquad N(|\bm{z}|)=\max(1,1+\log|\bm{z}|).

较低的困惑度获得较高的排名。仅当额外的效率分数还可以提高参考复杂度时，才会奖励较短的跟踪。 VR-CLI 将相关结构应用于下一章预测Lower perplexity receives a higher rank. An additional efficiency score rewards a shorter trace only when it also improves reference perplexity. VR-CLI applies a related construction to next-chapter prediction(Gurung & Lapata, 2025)。冻结故事生成器评估生成的计划是否使观察到的延续更可预测。由此产生的奖励衡量了对该延续的支持，而不假设它是唯一有效的故事。. A frozen story generator evaluates whether a generated plan makes the observed continuation more predictable. The resulting reward measures support for that continuation without assuming that it is the only valid story.

代币选择性和流程奖励。Token-selective and process rewards.

有些方法会询问参考文献的哪些部分对于抽样推理来说信息最丰富。直接推理优化根据参考标记的条件概率在部署过程中的变化程度来对其进行加权，然后在标题和查询过滤器下聚合加权分数Some methods ask which parts of a reference are most informative about the sampled reasoning. Direct Reasoning Optimization weights reference tokens by how much their conditional probabilities vary across rollouts, then aggregates the weighted scores under rubric and query filters(Xu et al., 2025b)。 P2S根据问题和答案生成候选金链，对其进行过滤，并选择参考可能性最高的链. P2S generates candidate gold chains conditioned on the question and answer, filters them, and selects the chain with the highest reference likelihood(Zhong et al., 2026)。然后，段级奖励会衡量采样轨迹的每个部分对后续链段预测的改进程度。当直接结果奖励不可用时，这种结构提供了流程塑造。. Segment-level rewards then measure how much each part of a sampled trace improves prediction of a later chain segment. This construction provides process shaping when a direct outcome reward is unavailable.

重建和循环一致性。Reconstruction and cycle consistency.

其他方法从重建环路获取参考锚定信号。 DuPO 将查询分解为已知和未知组件，并奖励支持重建缺失组件的答案Other methods obtain reference-anchored signals from a reconstruction loop. DuPO decomposes a query into known and unknown components and rewards answers that support reconstruction of the missing component(She et al., 2025a)。 CycleReward 将跨模态重建分数转换为图像和文本生成的偏好. CycleReward converts cross-modal reconstruction scores into preferences for image and text generation(Bahng et al., 2025)。 TRANS ZERO 通过往返语义一致性训练单语言数据的翻译. TRANS ZERO trains translation from monolingual data through round-trip semantic consistency(Zou et al., 2025)，而 RTRL 根据冻结后向模型重建原始输入的可能性来奖励化学语言输出, while RTRL rewards chemical-language outputs according to the likelihood that a frozen backward model reconstructs the original input(Kong et al., 2026).

参考指标。Reference metrics.

基于参考的指标可以对采样输出进行评分，而无需在每次推出时进行新的判断。尽管如此，他们的训练数据可能会编码人类的偏好Reference-based metrics can score sampled outputs without a fresh judgment on every rollout. Their training data may nevertheless encode human preferences(Qi et al., 2026a)。机器翻译方法结合了参考指标、学习质量估计器、源一致性、对齐和格式检查. Machine-translation methods combine reference metrics, learned quality estimators, source consistency, alignment, and format checks(Yang et al., 2025c; Rei et al., 2020; Feng et al., 2025c; He et al., 2025; Li et al., 2025j)。类似的评分者将几代人与其他领域的参考答案或解释进行比较. Similar scorers compare generations with reference responses or explanations in other domains(Li et al., 2025i; Pappone et al., 2025)。 BLEUBERI 根据合成参考优化 BLEU. BLEUBERI optimizes BLEU against synthetic references(Chang et al., 2025)，并且 RLVRR 将源自参考的内容检查与自动生成的样式标准相结合, and RLVRR combines reference-derived content checks with automatically generated style criteria(Jiang et al., 2026b).

VeriFree 说明了单个目标如何影响推理和答案预测。在其留一法估计器中，推理项的权重为VeriFree illustrates how a single objective can influence both reasoning and answer prediction. In its leave-one-out estimator, the reasoning term is weighted byAi=Ri−1G−1​∑j≠iRjA_{i}=R_{i}-\frac{1}{G-1}\sum_{j\neq i}R_{j}，而答案项的权重为, while the answer term is weighted byRi=πθ​(y⋆∣x,zi)R_{i}=\pi_{\theta}(y^{\star}\mid x,z_{i}) (Zhou et al., 2025b)。分解阐明了为什么即使没有外部验证者直接评估该痕迹，参考锚定奖励也可以形成痕迹。. The decomposition clarifies why reference-anchored rewards can shape a trace even when no external verifier evaluates that trace directly.

3.3.3. 环境接地信号3.3.3. Environment-Grounded Signals

参考锚定奖励询问部署是否支持现有目标。基于环境的奖励而是评估在交互中执行或使用输出时发生的情况。可执行的测试、正式的内核、游戏规则和可观察的界面状态可以判定成功，而无需对每个轨迹进行新的人工判断。这种外部基础比信心或共识更强大，因为证据并非来自政策本身的分布。它仍然不完美，因为人们可能已经编写了测试、指定了目标或选择了可观察的结果。Reference-anchored rewards ask whether a rollout supports an existing target. Environment-grounded rewards instead evaluate what happens when the output is executed or used in an interaction. Executable tests, formal kernels, game rules, and observable interface states can adjudicate success without a new human judgment for each trajectory. This external grounding is stronger than confidence or consensus because the evidence does not come from the policy’s own distribution. It is still imperfect, since people may have written the tests, specified the goals, or chosen the observable outcome.

可执行的结果。Executable outcomes.

代码执行提供了最清晰的例子。对于一个程序Code execution provides the clearest example. For a program𝒚\bm{y}和测试套件and test suite𝒯\mathcal{T}，严格奖励可以写为, a strict reward can be written as

ℛ⁡(𝒚)=𝕀⁡{exec⁡(𝒚,𝒯)​passes}.\mathcal{R}(\bm{y})=\mathbb{I}\{\mathrm{exec}(\bm{y},\mathcal{T})\ \mathrm{passes}\}.

其他设计使用通过测试的分数或几轮交互后的最终成功信号Other designs use the fraction of passed tests or a terminal success signal after several rounds of interaction(Li et al., 2026d)。 RLEF 将竞争性编程视为迭代任务，其中执行反馈附加到对话中. RLEF treats competitive programming as an iterative task in which execution feedback is appended to the dialogue(Gehring et al., 2025)。公共测试的成功会触发对保留的私人测试的评估，PPO 通过无效代码惩罚和 KL 正则化来优化最终的所有测试通过结果。 ACECoder 从种子代码中综合任务和测试，使用测试通过率形成偏好对，并训练学习奖励模型或基于规则的策略奖励. Public-test success triggers evaluation on held-out private tests, and PPO optimizes the final all-tests-pass outcome with an invalid-code penalty and KL regularization. ACECoder synthesizes tasks and tests from seed code, uses test pass rates to form preference pairs, and trains either a learned reward model or a rule-based policy reward(Zeng et al., 2025a)。当模型还生成测试或任务时，相同的可执行结果可以支持自我生成的体验。因此，共同进化的编码器和测试生成器系统将此奖励源连接到经验轴. When a model also generates the tests or tasks, the same executable outcome can support self-generated experience. Co-evolving coder and test-generator systems therefore connect this reward source to the experience axis(Wang et al., 2025k).

推出期间的工具执行不会自动使奖励环境扎根。 ReTool和ToRL在推理时调用Python解释器，但它们的主要策略奖励是基于最终答案的正确性Tool execution during a rollout does not automatically make the reward environment-grounded. ReTool and ToRL call a Python interpreter while reasoning, but their main policy rewards are based on final-answer correctness(Feng et al., 2025a; Li et al., 2025h)。 ToolRL 奖励与真实工具名称和参数的一致. ToolRL rewards agreement with ground-truth tool names and parameters(Qian et al., 2025)。在这些情况下，执行会更改策略可用的信息，而最终评估仍然基于参考。这种区别很重要，因为工具获取和奖励基础涉及学习循环的不同部分。. In these cases, execution changes the information available to the policy, while the terminal evaluation remains reference-based. The distinction matters because tool access and reward grounding address different parts of the learning loop.

存储库级软件工程包含基于参考和基于执行的设计。 SWE-RL 根据与预言机补丁的相似性对生成的补丁进行评分，并使用预言机本地化上下文Repository-level software engineering contains both reference-based and execution-based designs. SWE-RL scores generated patches by similarity to an oracle patch and uses oracle-localized context(Wei et al., 2026)。因此，即使周围的支架包括测试和重新排名，它的奖励也固定在存储的目标上。 Kimi-Dev 使用二进制执行结果进行无代理代码编辑 RL，而其后来的端到端 SWE 代理适应则依赖于监督微调. Its reward is consequently anchored to a stored target even though the surrounding scaffold includes tests and reranking. Kimi-Dev uses binary execution outcomes for Agentless code-edit RL, while its later end-to-end SWE-agent adaptation relies on supervised fine-tuning(Yang et al., 2026d)。 SkyRL Agent 将多轮 RL 应用于软件工程，尽管其报告的目标结合了多个信号并且没有隔离单个已验证完成奖励. SkyRL Agent applies multi-turn RL to software engineering, although its reported objective combines several signals and does not isolate a single verified-completion reward(Cao et al., 2025).

检索和交互结果。Retrieval and interactive outcomes.

搜索和浏览提供环境观察，但许多系统仍然通过参考或基于模型的评估器来判断最终答案。搜索 R1 使用与目标答案的精确匹配，并从策略丢失中屏蔽检索到的令牌Search and browsing provide environmental observations, but many systems still judge the final answer with a reference or model-based evaluator. Search R1 uses exact matching against a target answer and masks retrieved tokens from the policy loss(Jin et al., 2025)。 R1 Searcher 将检索激励与答案奖励分开. R1 Searcher separates retrieval incentives from answer rewards(Song et al., 2025a)。 WebDancer、WebSailor、WebExplorer 和 ASearcher 支持长浏览轨迹，但保留基于参考文献或 LLM 评估器的最终判断. WebDancer, WebSailor, WebExplorer, and ASearcher support long browsing trajectories but retain terminal judgments based on references or LLM evaluators(Wu et al., 2025a; Li et al., 2025b; Liu et al., 2025e; Gao et al., 2025b)。过程奖励和模拟检索可以在相同的最终答案基础下改善搜索行为. Process rewards and simulated retrieval can improve search behavior under the same final-answer grounding(Zheng et al., 2025b; Nguyen et al., 2025b; Sun et al., 2025a)。计算机使用代理同样涵盖程序化状态检查、学习视觉结果模型、动作匹配、参考判断和离线偏好. Computer-use agents likewise span programmatic state checks, learned visual outcome models, action matching, reference judgments, and offline preferences(Li et al., 2025d; Ye et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2025c; Lu et al., 2026; Luo et al., 2025; Qin et al., 2025)。因此，可观察的交互是经验的来源，而只有与结果状态相关的结果才构成基于环境的奖励。. Observable interaction is thus a source of experience, while only an outcome tied to the resulting state constitutes environment-grounded reward.

比赛结果。Game outcomes.

游戏通过胜利、失败和规则定义的分数提供稀疏但可重复的奖励。 SPIRAL 通过两人零和语言游戏中的自我对弈来训练共享策略Games provide sparse but reproducible rewards through wins, losses, and rule-defined scores. SPIRAL trains a shared policy through self-play in two-player zero-sum language games(Liu et al., 2025a)。游戏结果提供奖励，而不断发展的对手则创建适应性课程。因此，该方法通过其终端信号属于奖励轴，通过自我游戏属于体验轴。角色条件优势估计解决了位置不对称问题，以及由此产生的策略转移到用于训练的游戏之外。 Vision Zero 通过视觉社交演绎游戏将基于结果的自我游戏扩展到多模态推理. The game outcome supplies the reward, while evolving opponents create an adaptive curriculum. The method therefore belongs to the reward axis through its terminal signal and to the experience axis through self-play. Role-conditioned advantage estimation addresses positional asymmetry, and the resulting policy transfers beyond the games used for training. Vision Zero extends outcome-based self-play to multimodal reasoning through a visual social-deduction game(Wang et al., 2025e)。 Absolute Zero 将 Python 执行器与自我提出的任务结合起来，对提议者的可学习性和求解器的正确性使用单独的奖励. Absolute Zero combines a Python executor with self-proposed tasks, using separate rewards for proposer learnability and solver correctness(Zhao et al., 2025a).

形式验证。Formal verification.

形式定理证明提供了最强的基于环境的信号之一，因为内核接受在固定的语句、环境和资源预算下给出了可重现的结果。正式工具还可以提供经过验证的步骤级反馈Formal theorem proving offers one of the strongest environment-grounded signals because kernel acceptance gives a reproducible outcome under a fixed statement, environment, and resource budget. Formal tools can also provide verified step-level feedback(Rahman & Alsharari, 2026)。即便如此，内核接受并不能验证定理的自然语言形式化，并且超时也不是逻辑反驳。. Even so, kernel acceptance does not validate the natural-language formalization of a theorem, and a timeout is not a logical refutation.

DeepSeek Prover V2 将困难目标分解为子目标后，通过精益验证的正确性和结构一致性奖励进行训练DeepSeek Prover V2 trains with Lean-verified correctness and structural-consistency rewards after decomposing difficult goals into subgoals(Ren et al., 2025b)。 Kimina Prover 通过二元精益接受来扩展强化学习. Kimina Prover scales RL with binary Lean acceptance(Wang et al., 2025b)，而 Seed Prover 使用精益反馈迭代地完善完整的证明, while Seed Prover iteratively refines complete proofs using Lean feedback(Chen et al., 2025d)。 Goedel Prover V2 将任务综合与验证者引导的校正相结合. Goedel Prover V2 combines task synthesis with verifier-guided correction(Lin et al., 2026b)，Leanabell Prover V2 通过与 Lean 的反复交互来训练长轨迹, and Leanabell Prover V2 trains long trajectories through repeated interactions with Lean(Ji et al., 2025)。 STP 将猜想者和证明者结合起来，并使用证明结果和引理使用检查来选择前沿猜想. STP couples a conjecturer and prover and selects frontier conjectures using proof outcomes and lemma-usage checks(Dong & Ma, 2025)。 InternLM2.5 StepProver 提供了基于正式库和专家迭代的对比固定语料库体系. InternLM2.5 StepProver provides a contrasting fixed-corpus regime based on formal libraries and expert iteration(Wu et al., 2025e).

总而言之，基于环境的奖励在政策和用于评分的证据之间提供了最清晰的区分。这种分离减少了直接的自我确认，但并没有消除规范错误。不完整的测试、不准确的模拟器、可利用的游戏规则以及自然语言意图和正式陈述之间的不匹配都可能保留高奖励，但不会产生预期的行为。因此，奖励接地必须与环境的有效性和覆盖范围一起评估。Taken together, environment-grounded rewards offer the clearest separation between the policy and the evidence used to score it. That separation reduces direct self-confirmation but does not eliminate specification error. Incomplete tests, inaccurate simulators, exploitable game rules, and mismatches between natural-language intent and formal statements can all preserve high reward without producing the intended behavior. Reward grounding must therefore be assessed together with the validity and coverage of the environment.

3.4. 限制和故障模式3.4. Limitations and Failure Modes

随着评估从每个实例目标转向可重用或自动获取的信号，监督变得更具可扩展性，但错误来源也发生了变化。以人为本的评估者可能会歪曲他们受过训练以重现的判断。模型衍生的奖励可能会加剧政策本身的错误。参考锚定的奖励可能会过度拟合不完整的目标，而基于环境的奖励可能会利用测试或规则中的遗漏。因此，相关问题不是奖励是否是自动的，而是什么证据使其在优化下值得信赖。As evaluation moves from per-instance targets to reusable or automatically obtained signals, supervision becomes more scalable but the source of error also changes. Human-grounded evaluators may misrepresent the judgments they were trained to reproduce. Model-derived rewards can reinforce the policy’s own mistakes. Reference-anchored rewards can overfit an incomplete target, while environment-grounded rewards can exploit omissions in tests or rules. The relevant question is consequently not whether a reward is automatic, but what evidence makes it trustworthy under optimization.

3.4.1. 奖励信号失败3.4.1. Failures of the Reward Signal

以人为本的评估者。Human-grounded evaluators.

LLM 评委表现出立场、冗长、自我增强和特定模型的偏见LLM judges exhibit position, verbosity, self-enhancement, and model-specific biases(Chen et al., 2024b)。多样化的评审小组可以减少一些特定于模型的影响. Diverse judge panels can reduce some model-specific effects(Verga et al., 2024)，而评分细则或清单使评估标准更加明确, and rubrics or checklists make the evaluation criteria more explicit(Gunjal et al., 2025; Viswanathan et al., 2025)。尽管如此，明确的标题仍然可以省略实质性要求，并且自动偏好分数可能有利于较长的响应或演示功能. Nevertheless, an explicit rubric can still omit substantive requirements, and automatic preference scores may favor longer responses or presentation features(Dubois et al., 2024; Singhal et al., 2023)。奖励模型集成可以减少过度优化，但不会消除它. Reward-model ensembles reduce overoptimization but do not remove it(Coste et al., 2023; Eisenstein et al., 2023)。以宪法和原则为指导，以批判和修正的方式明确监督的规范来源. Constitution- and principle-guided pipelines clarify the normative source of supervision through critique and revision(Bai et al., 2022; Sun et al., 2023)，尽管它们的可靠性仍然取决于所选择的原则和评估者。, although their reliability still depends on the chosen principles and evaluator.

模型导出的信号。Model-derived signals.

共识可能会强化最初占主导地位的错误答案，因为它缺乏正确性的独立证据。在长时间的多数投票训练下，代理奖励可能会与任务准确性分离并产生模板答案或崩溃Consensus can reinforce an initially dominant wrong answer because it lacks independent evidence of correctness. Under prolonged majority-vote training, proxy reward may separate from task accuracy and produce template answers or collapse(Shafayat et al., 2025)。熵和自我确定性奖励显示出相关的模式。一些基础模型早期改进，然后变得过度自信，而指令调整模型通常受益较少. Entropy and self-certainty rewards show a related pattern. Some base models improve early and then become overconfident, while instruction-tuned models often benefit less(Zhang et al., 2025k)。更广泛的分析将这种行为归因于分布锐化，并观察到反复上升然后下降. A broader analysis attributes this behavior to distribution sharpening and observes a recurring rise followed by decline(He et al., 2026)。超参数更改可以延迟失败，但它们不能提供缺失的外部锚点。尽管目前的证据并未建立通用的解决方案，但结构化的自我验证可能会扩展有用的制度。. Hyperparameter changes can delay the failure, but they do not supply the missing external anchor. Structured self-verification may extend the useful regime, although current evidence does not establish a general solution.

参考锚定和混合奖励。Reference-anchored and hybrid rewards.
