参考似然方法优化以采样轨迹为条件的固定目标的概率或复杂度Reference-likelihood methods optimize the probability or perplexity of a fixed target conditioned on a sampled trace(Zhou et al., 2025b; Liu et al., 2025h; Tang et al., 2025)。当一个参考仅代表有效答案空间的一部分时，目标就无法指定质量的其他方面。混合系统可以结合互补的证据，而无需形成单独的阶梯级别。 JEPO 将策略梯度推理目标与目标答案的监督预测结合起来. When one reference represents only part of the valid answer space, the objective leaves other aspects of quality unspecified. Hybrid systems can combine complementary evidence without forming a separate ladder level. JEPO couples a policy-gradient reasoning objective with supervised prediction of the target answer(Tang et al., 2025)。 Seed1.5 Thinking 使用验证器来执行可验证的任务，并使用学习的成对奖励模型来执行开放式任务. Seed1.5 Thinking uses verifiers for verifiable tasks and a learned pairwise reward model for open-ended tasks(Seed et al., 2025)。仅当其组件以不同方式发生故障时，此类组合才能提高覆盖范围。. Such combinations improve coverage only when their components fail in different ways.

奖励有效性和验证者噪音。Reward validity and verifier noise.

观察到的 RLVR 增益本身并不表明奖励捕获了预期的语义。由于基本模型先验和优化动态，随机或仅限格式的奖励可以提高某些设置中的性能An observed RLVR gain does not by itself show that the reward captured the intended semantics. Random or format-only rewards can improve performance in some settings because of base-model priors and optimization dynamics(Shao et al., 2025a)。受控验证者噪音是可以容忍的，但误报通常比漏报更具破坏性. Controlled verifier noise can be tolerated, but false positives are generally more damaging than false negatives(Plesner et al., 2026)。系统性的假阴性往往会延迟学习，而可利用的假阳性模式可能会造成次优的平台期或崩溃. Systematic false negatives tend to delay learning, while exploitable false-positive patterns can create suboptimal plateaus or collapse(Egashira et al., 2026)。相对于干净的监督，不正确的注释同样会降低性能. Incorrect annotations likewise reduce performance relative to clean supervision(Zhu & Kang, 2026)。只有当验证者规范、覆盖范围和实施本身健全时，外部裁决才是可靠的。. External adjudication is reliable only when the verifier specification, coverage, and implementation are themselves sound.

规范开发。Specification exploitation.

基于规则的数学验证器可以拒绝不熟悉格式的等效答案，而学习验证器可能会承认策略可以利用的误报Rule-based mathematical verifiers can reject equivalent answers in unfamiliar formats, while learned verifiers may admit false positives that a policy can exploit(Huang et al., 2025c)。生成法官可以奖励仅标点符号的回答或通用推理开场白. Generative judges can reward punctuation-only responses or generic reasoning openers(Zhao et al., 2025c)。当扩展检查奖励归纳逻辑中的枚举时，会出现类似的快捷方式. Similar shortcuts appear when extensional checks reward enumeration in inductive logic(Helff et al., 2026)或者当标题偏见成为强化学习期间的策略时or when rubric biases become strategies during RL(Wang et al., 2026c)。奖励黑客、受控测试台和验证者模糊测试的分类法提供了在大规模优化之前识别这些弱点的补充方法. Taxonomies of reward hacking, controlled testbeds, and verifier fuzzing offer complementary ways to identify these weaknesses before large-scale optimization(Wang et al., 2026d; Khalifa et al., 2026; Ray, 2026)。已执行的奖励仅在实施代表预期成功标准的情况下才有效。. Executed rewards remain grounded only to the extent that the implementation represents the intended success criterion.

耦合政策和评估者。Coupled policies and evaluators.

自我判断会产生额外的失败模式，因为政策和评估者可能会适应相同的错误。针对以候选人为条件、无参考资料的法官进行优化的策略可以学习使错误答案更有说服力，但不会使其更加准确。这些错误可能会在来自不同模型系列的法官之间传递Self-judging creates an additional failure mode because the policy and evaluator may adapt to the same errors. A policy optimized against a candidate-conditioned, reference-free judge can learn to make incorrect answers more convincing without making them more accurate. These errors can transfer across judges from different model families(Zhou, 2026)。当验证者仅根据自己的多数票伪标签进行更新时，SPELL 中会出现相关崩溃。然后错误会累积，直到每个响应者输出都被标记为正确。 SPELL 通过根据可验证问题的基于规则的结果检查验证者更新来限制这种偏差. A related collapse appears in SPELL when a verifier is updated only from its own majority-vote pseudo-labels. Errors can then accumulate until every responder output is labeled correct. SPELL limits this drift by checking verifier updates against rule-based outcomes on verifiable questions(Yang et al., 2026c)。结果说明了为什么递归自我评估需要独立于参与者和法官的证据。. The result illustrates why recursive self-evaluation needs evidence that remains independent of both actor and judge.

3.4.2. 能力扩展和转移3.4.2. Capability Expansion and Transfer

奖励的提升和能力的拓展是相关的，但并不等同。 RLVR 可以提高采样效率，同时缩小训练策略解决的问题范围Reward improvement and capability expansion are related but not equivalent. RLVR can improve sampling efficiency while narrowing the set of problems solved by the trained policy(Yue et al., 2025)。在标准在线优化下，精确支持不能超出最初具有非零概率的输出，并且有限采样可以缩小训练期间观察到的经验支持. Under standard online optimization, exact support cannot extend beyond outputs that initially have nonzero probability, and finite sampling can shrink the empirical support observed during training(Wu & Choi, 2025)。但是，此限制的严重程度取决于基本策略、任务和培训范围。长时间的训练可以从利用常见的解决方案转变为增加罕见的高奖励行动的概率. However, the severity of this limitation depends on the base policy, task, and training horizon. Prolonged training can shift from exploiting common solutions to increasing the probability of rare high-reward actions(Yao et al., 2025)。 ProRL 同样报告了一些困难编码和分布外任务的持续边界扩展，而其他任务则处于停滞状态. ProRL likewise reports sustained boundary expansion on some difficult coding and out-of-distribution tasks, alongside plateaus on others(Liu et al., 2025f).

转移提供了奖励优化是否改变一般能力的第二个测试。在受控数学实验中，RL 调优比 SFT 更可靠地保留或提高了多种非数学能力Transfer provides a second test of whether reward optimization changes general capability. In controlled mathematics experiments, RL tuning preserves or improves several nonmathematical abilities more reliably than SFT(Huan et al., 2026)。这一结果并不意味着 RLVR 在每种设置下都能产生领域通用推理。改进可以遵循组合优化中的表面启发法. This result does not imply that RLVR produces domain-general reasoning in every setting. Improvements can follow superficial heuristics in combinatorial optimization(Alam & Rastogi, 2025)，通过 RLVR 学习的推理轨迹对一般问题回答的贡献远小于对可验证推理的贡献, and reasoning traces learned through RLVR contribute much less to general question answering than to verifiable reasoning(Li et al., 2026b)。显然，相互矛盾的发现通常反映出基础模型与目标任务的一致性程度存在差异. Apparently conflicting findings often reflect differences in how well the base model is already aligned with the target task(Wu et al., 2026a).

总体而言，超越人类评估的奖励最好通过支持它们的证据进行比较。随着奖励变得越来越不依赖于策略，测量的收益更加依赖于基本模型、任务分配和评估器中可利用的规律。然而，外部执行本身是不够的，因为不完整的规范也会奖励意外的行为。奖励成本、奖励独立性和奖励有效性是不同的属性，必须单独评估。本节重点讨论奖励的来源。仅当方法还生成或发现提供其经验的任务、课程或环境时，才会向 L3 迈进，如第 1 节中所述Overall, rewards beyond human evaluation are best compared through the evidence that supports them. As the reward becomes less independent of the policy, measured gains depend more strongly on the base model, the task distribution, and exploitable regularities in the evaluator. Yet external execution is not sufficient on its own because incomplete specifications can also reward unintended behavior. Reward cost, reward independence, and reward validity are distinct properties and must be evaluated separately. This section has focused on the source of reward. A method moves toward L3 only when it also generates or discovers the tasks, curricula, or environments that provide its experience, as discussed in Section4.

4. 超越人类设计的体验4. Experience beyond Human Design

部分Section3检查评价来源如何沿着奖励轴变化。获得超出人类评估的奖励只能解决扩展问题的一部分。即使可以在不主要依赖人类判断的情况下评估轨迹，训练也可能仍然局限于由人和预先构建的环境选择的任务。持续的改进取决于经验分布是否能够适应策略，从而使模型面临新的问题和交互，而无需持续的人工管理。这引发了本节的中心问题：examines how the source of evaluation changes along the reward axis. Obtaining rewards beyond human evaluation addresses only one part of the scaling problem. Even when trajectories can be evaluated without relying primarily on human judgment, training may remain confined to tasks selected by people and environments built in advance. Continued improvement then depends on whether the experience distribution can adapt with the policy, exposing the model to new problems and interactions without continual human curation. This motivates the central question of this section:

改变奖励的来源并不一定会改变经验的来源。当经验流本身变得具有适应性时，沿着经验轴的进展就开始了，无论是通过固定环境中的任务生成还是通过围绕外部任务和种子的环境构建。结合这些方法可以维持课程，而无需持续的人工管理。这一进程最终形成一个闭环，其中奖励获取、经验生成和政策改进共同适应。如节中所定义Changing the source of reward does not necessarily change the source of experience. Progress along the experience axis begins when the experience stream itself becomes adaptive, either through task generation in a fixed environment or through environment construction around external tasks and seeds. Combining these approaches can sustain a curriculum with less continual human curation. The progression culminates in a closed loop in which reward acquisition, experience generation, and policy improvement adapt together. As defined in Section2.5，阶梯确定了维持学习所需的人类输入，而不是每个数据集、工具或环境的历史起源。, the ladder identifies the human input needed to sustain learning, not the historical origin of every dataset, tool, or environment.

相关工作已经从以模型为中心接近这一领域Related work has approached this area from model-centric(Gao et al., 2025a; Fang et al., 2025a)并以环境为中心and environment-centric(Huang et al., 2025b)观点。我们的论文通过跟踪奖励将这些观点结合在一起perspectives. Our paper brings these perspectives together by tracking the rewardℛ\mathcal{R}、任务分配, the task distributionΠ\Pi，以及环境动态, and the environment dynamics(𝒮,𝒫)(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{P})在同一个学习循环中。表within the same learning loop. Table3记录每个阶段哪些组件仍然由外部提供。讨论从固定的人类设计经验开始，然后将任务生成和环境构建视为集成经验生成的两条路径。最后得出了自主协同进化以及这些组件耦合时出现的故障模式。records which components remain externally supplied at each stage. The discussion begins with fixed human-designed experience and then considers task generation and environment construction as two paths toward integrated experience generation. It concludes with autonomous co-evolution and the failure modes that arise as these components become coupled.

4.1. 固定的人性设计体验4.1. Fixed Human-Designed Experience

在阶梯中，L2标志着奖励来源可能改变而经验保持不变的制度。奖励可能来自模型行为、可用参考或环境结果。查询仍然是从人工策划的任务分布中采样的In the ladder, L2 marks a regime in which the reward source may change while experience remains fixed. Rewards may come from model behavior, available references, or environment outcomes. The queries are still sampled from a human-curated task distributionΠH\Pi_{\mathrm{H}}，并且交互发生在人类提供的环境中, and interaction takes place in a human-provided environmentℰH=(𝒮H,𝒜H,𝒫H)\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{H}}=(\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{H}},\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{H}},\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{H}})。相应的目标可以示意性地写为. The corresponding objective can be written schematically as

maxθ𝔼𝒒∼ΠH,τ∼pθ(⋅∣𝒒,ℰH)[ℛ(𝒒,τ)].\max_{\theta}\,\mathbb{E}_{\bm{q}\sim\Pi_{\mathrm{H}},\,\tau\sim p_{\theta}(\cdot\mid\bm{q},\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{H}})}\left[\mathcal{R}(\bm{q},\tau)\right]. (17)

等式。Eq.17将奖励机制与经验来源分开。改变方式separates the reward mechanism from the source of experience. Changing howℛ\mathcal{R}获得的结果不会改变外部提供的任务分配或环境。该策略可以探索可用的任务和动态，但新任务和环境的选择仍然在学习循环之外。该固定设置提供了体验轴的出发点。is obtained does not alter the externally supplied task distribution or environment. The policy can explore the available tasks and dynamics, but the choice of new tasks and environments remains outside the learning loop. This fixed setting provides the departure point for the experience axis.

外部提供的任务分配。Externally supplied task distribution.

即使奖励不再主要取决于人类评价，Even when the reward no longer depends primarily on human evaluation,ΠH\Pi_{\mathrm{H}}仍然体现了人类对政策遇到的内容和频率的决定。语料库选择和领域边界决定了其覆盖范围，而任务模板、难度范围、过滤规则和采样频率则在该覆盖范围内分配训练工作。 对于数学推理，这可能意味着从 GSM8K 等精选集合中提取still embodies human decisions about what the policy encounters and how often. Corpus selection and domain boundaries determine its coverage, while task templates, difficulty ranges, filtering rules, and sampling frequencies allocate training effort within that coverage. For mathematical reasoning, this may mean drawing from curated collections such as GSM8K(Cobbe et al., 2021)。对于编码，这可能意味着使用人类编写的规范和 HumanEval 风格的测试. For coding, it may mean using human-written specifications and tests in the style of HumanEval(Chen et al., 2021)。对于指令遵循，这可能意味着在预先选择主题和风格覆盖范围的固定语料库上进行训练。 这些设计选择将梯度信号集中在特定功能上，并确定训练可以暴露哪些故障。模型派生或自动计算的奖励可以在狭窄的范围内提高绩效. For instruction following, it may mean training on a fixed corpus whose topical and stylistic coverage was selected in advance. These design choices concentrate gradient signal on particular capabilities and determine which failures training can expose. Model-derived or automatically computed rewards may improve performance within a narrowΠH\Pi_{\mathrm{H}}，但他们无法揭示数据中缺失的领域，也无法自行修复校准不佳的课程。, but they cannot reveal domains that are absent from the data or repair a poorly calibrated curriculum on their own.

外部提供的环境。Externally supplied environment.

人类也决定了轨迹展开的世界。在单轮推理中，“环境”可能几乎是不可见的，由静态提示、分词器级动作空间和固定答案界面组成。编码任务添加编程语言、沙箱、库和执行协议。检索和工具使用设置添加了语料库、API、工具模式和状态转换，而基于 GUI 的代理和游戏代理则依赖于定义其观察、法律行动和交互范围的应用程序或模拟器。 甚至诸如成功编译、检索或游戏玩法之类的结果也是在人类选择和设计的动态范围内产生的。该模型可以广泛地探索这些动态，但当缺少必要的工具、状态或因果机制时，它无法扩大或修复固定的环境。Humans also determine the world in which trajectories unfold. In single-turn reasoning, the “environment” may be nearly invisible, consisting of a static prompt, a tokenizer-level action space, and a fixed answer interface. Coding tasks add a programming language, sandbox, libraries, and execution protocol. Retrieval and tool-use settings add corpora, APIs, tool schemas, and state transitions, while GUI-based, embodied, and game agents depend on applications or simulators that define their observations, legal actions, and interaction horizons. Even outcomes such as successful compilation, retrieval, or gameplay are produced within dynamics selected and engineered by humans. The model may explore those dynamics extensively, but it cannot broaden or repair a fixed environment when the necessary tools, states, or causal mechanisms are missing.

固定经验下的奖励信号。Reward signals under fixed experience.

当应用于固定任务和环境时，第 1 节中的方法When applied to fixed tasks and environments, the methods in Section3.3改变评价的来源而不改变经验的来源。 TTRLchange the source of evaluation without changing the source of experience. TTRL(Zuo et al., 2025b)和MM-UPTand MM-UPT(Wei et al., 2025b)用多数票伪标签替换外部标签，同时保留提供的测试实例。 EM-RLreplace external labels with majority-vote pseudo-labels while retaining the supplied test instances. EM-RL(Agarwal et al., 2025)从策略自身的熵中获得奖励，但优化确定性的问题仍然是外部选择的。 VeriFree、RLPR 和 NOVERderives reward from the policy’s own entropy, but the questions on which certainty is optimized remain externally chosen. VeriFree, RLPR, and NOVER(Zhou et al., 2025b; Yu et al., 2025d; Liu et al., 2025h)使用所提供参考的可能性或复杂性作为软一致性信号。它们消除了对单独验证者的需要，同时保留了定义体验的查询和参考对。 同样，执行反馈可以提供use the likelihood or perplexity of a supplied reference as a soft consistency signal. They remove the need for a separate verifier while retaining the query and reference pairs that define the experience. Similarly, execution feedback can provideℛ\mathcal{R}在解释器、单元测试界面或游戏规则保持不变的情况下，通过程序结果。 在这些方法中，奖励来源发生变化，同时through program outcomes while the interpreter, unit-test interface, or game rules remain fixed. Across these methods, the reward source changes while(ΠH,ℰH)(\Pi_{\mathrm{H}},\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{H}})仍由外部供电。remains externally supplied.

固定经验的好处和局限性。Benefits and limits of fixed experience.

固定经验来源为比较奖励信号提供了稳定的基础，因为目标分布不会随策略而改变，并且保留了外部基础来源。与Fixing the source of experience provides a stable basis for comparing reward signals because the target distribution does not change with the policy and an external source of grounding is preserved. WithΠH\Pi_{\mathrm{H}}和and𝒫H\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{H}}如果保持不变，收益可以更明确地归因于政策优化而不是课程的变化。这种稳定性使固定经验成为有用的起点，使政策能够在开始产生新经验之前从共识、确定性、参考一致性或世界结果中学习held stationary, gains can be attributed more cleanly to policy optimization than to changes in the curriculum. This stability makes fixed experience a useful starting point, allowing a policy to learn from consensus, certainty, reference consistency, or world outcomes before it begins to generate new experience(Zuo et al., 2025b; Agarwal et al., 2025; Zhou et al., 2025b; Guo et al., 2025a).

同样的稳定性也限制了进一步的改进，因为经验的多样性和视野不能超过The same stability also limits further improvement because the diversity and horizon of experience cannot exceed whatΠH\Pi_{\mathrm{H}}和andℰH\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{H}}支持。重复采样和额外的测试时计算可以提高对现有问题的覆盖范围，但它们本身并不能扩大任务或环境分布support. Repeated sampling and additional test-time compute can improve coverage on existing problems, but they do not broaden the task or environment distribution by themselves(Brown et al., 2024; Snell et al., 2024)。超越这个设置需要适应. Moving beyond this setting requires adaptation in什么what遇到问题，problems are encountered,哪里where它们的后果逐渐显现，或者两者兼而有之。适应性课程和开放式环境生成通过改变学习中的任务或环境来实现这种扩展their consequences unfold, or both. Adaptive curricula and open-ended environment generation pursue this expansion by changing which tasks or environments enter learning(Chen et al., 2025i; Wang et al., 2019; Faldor et al., 2024)。仅靠新的奖励来源并不能提供这种适应。. A new reward source alone does not provide that adaptation.

4.2. 从固定体验到生成体验4.2. From Fixed to Generated Experience

最近的一些方法通常一次放宽人类设计的一个来源。有些在保留固定环境的同时生成任务，而另一些则围绕外部提供的任务或种子构建环境，以适应Some recent methods generally relax one source of human design at a time. Some generate tasks while retaining a fixed environment, whereas others construct environments around externally supplied tasks or seeds, adaptingΠ\Pi和and(𝒮,𝒫)(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{P})，分别。这两种路线本身都不会产生新任务和执行它们所需的上下文的连续流，但每种路线都为第 1 节中讨论的集成系统提供了构建块, respectively. Neither route by itself produces a continuing stream of both new tasks and the contexts needed to execute them, but each provides a building block for the integrated systems discussed in Section4.3.

4.2.1. 固定环境中的任务生成4.2.1. Task Generation in Fixed Environments

减少对人工管理任务分配的依赖的一种方法是让模型生成或选择任务，同时保持环境固定。根据领域的不同，这个固定基础可能包括代码解释器、数学检查器或检索语料库。在方程的目标中。One way to reduce reliance on a human-curated task distribution is to let the model generate or select tasks while keeping the environment fixed. Depending on the domain, this fixed substrate may include a code interpreter, mathematical checker, or retrieval corpus. In the objective of Eq.1，查询是从模型参数化的提议者中提取的, queries are drawn from a model-parameterized proposer𝒒∼Πθ\bm{q}\sim\Pi_{\theta}而不是固定分布instead of the fixed distributionΠH\Pi_{\mathrm{H}}，而过渡动力学, while the transition dynamics𝒫\mathcal{P}验证器仍然由外部供电。生成的任务必须是可解决的、多样化的，并且根据政策的当前能力进行校准。提案者的目标通常通过以下方式捕捉这一要求and the verifier remain externally supplied. Generated tasks must be solvable, diverse, and calibrated to the policy’s current ability. Proposer objectives often capture this requirement through易学性learnability，它有利于那些保持信息丰富而又不会变得微不足道或实际上无法解决的任务。由于验证仍然依赖于外部动态和规则，因此这些方法在尚未适应环境的情况下适应任务分配。, which favors tasks that remain informative without becoming trivial or effectively unsolvable. Because validation still relies on external dynamics and rules, these methods adapt the task distribution without yet adapting the environment.

自生成推理轨迹和指令数据。Self-generated reasoning traces and instruction data.

斯塔尔STaR(Zelikman et al., 2022)通过保持问题、环境和答案检查奖励固定，同时生成中间推理轨迹来提供早期模板provides an early template by keeping the questions, environment, and answer-checking reward fixed while generating intermediate reasoning traces𝒛\bm{z}并只保留那些得出正确答案的and retaining only those that reach the correct answer𝒚∗\bm{y}^{*}。后来的工作将生成从痕迹扩展到问题本身。自学. Later work extends generation from traces to the problems themselves. Self-Instruct(Wang et al., 2022)合成指令、输入和输出元组，并在微调之前删除近似重复项，而 Evol-Instructsynthesizes instruction, input, and output tuples and removes near-duplicates before fine-tuning, while Evol-Instruct(Xu et al., 2023)用更严格的约束或新主题重写种子指令。硒化酶rewrites seed instructions with harder constraints or new topics. SeRL(Fang et al., 2025b)从有限的种子集中生成推理实例，按内容、长度、相似性和难度对其进行过滤，并使用基于共识的奖励对保留的实例进行训练。 CoT-自学generates reasoning instances from a limited seed set, filters them by content, length, similarity, and difficulty, and trains on the retained instances with consensus-based rewards. CoT-Self-Instruct(Yu et al., 2025b)对预期领域和复杂性进行条件综合，使用答案一致性来过滤可验证的任务，并使用偏好模型来过滤不可验证的任务。任等人。conditions synthesis on the intended domain and complexity, using answer consistency to filter verifiable tasks and a preference model to filter unverifiable ones. Ren et al.(Ren et al., 2025a)通过添加或删除约束来控制构图难度。同样的模式扩展到代理设置，其中 ZeroGUIcontrol compositional difficulty by adding or removing constraints. The same pattern extends to agentic settings, where ZeroGUI(Yang et al., 2025b)从当前界面和 AgentSynth 提出 GUI 任务proposes GUI tasks from the current interface and AgentSynth(Xie et al., 2025)将单独可解决的子任务组合成更长的计算机使用任务。这些管道减少了对人类编写的语料库的依赖，同时保持验证环境固定。combines individually solvable subtasks into longer computer-use tasks. These pipelines reduce dependence on human-authored corpora while leaving the verification environment fixed.

提议者和求解者自我对弈。Proposer and solver self-play.

任务生成也可以被视为一个不对称博弈Task generation can also be framed as an asymmetric game between a提议者proposer πθP\pi_{\theta_{P}}和一个and a求解器solver πθS\pi_{\theta_{S}}，它们通常由相同的基础模型在不同的角色中实例化。提议者奖励决定了这个游戏是否产生有用的课程。仅奖励求解器失败的推送, which are often instantiated by the same base model in different roles. The proposer reward determines whether this game yields a useful curriculum. Rewarding only solver failure pushesΠ\Pi解决无法解决的噪音，而只奖励成功的解决方案则鼓励琐碎的任务。一个toward unsolvable noise, while rewarding only successful solutions encourages trivial tasks. A易学性learnability信号通过在中间求解器成功时达到峰值来避免两个极端。写作signal avoids both extremes by peaking at intermediate solver success. Writingp¯θS​(𝒒)\bar{p}_{\theta_{S}}(\bm{q})对于所提出的查询的经验解决率for the empirical solve rate of a proposed query overGG求解器推出，solver rollouts,

p¯θS(𝒒)=1G∑i=1G[𝒚i=𝒚^∗(𝒒)],ℛpropose(𝒒)=p¯θS(𝒒)(1−p¯θS(𝒒)),\bar{p}_{\theta_{S}}(\bm{q})=\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i=1}^{G}\mathbb{1}\!\left[\bm{y}_{i}=\hat{\bm{y}}^{*}(\bm{q})\right],\qquad\mathcal{R}_{\text{propose}}(\bm{q})=\bar{p}_{\theta_{S}}(\bm{q})\bigl(1-\bar{p}_{\theta_{S}}(\bm{q})\bigr), (18)

这样，既不总是解决的查询（so that queries that are neither always solved (p¯=1\bar{p}{=}1）也从未解决过（) nor never solved (p¯=0\bar{p}{=}0）获得最高的提议者奖励。绝对零推理机 (AZR)) receive the highest proposer reward. Absolute Zero Reasoner (AZR)(Zhao et al., 2025a)让模型同时充当提议者和求解者，结合方程式中形式的可学习性分数。lets the model act as both proposer and solver, combining a learnability score of the form in Eq.18通过经过环境验证的反馈来瞄准解决者的能力前沿。 R-零with environment-validated feedback to target the solver’s competence frontier. R-Zero(Huang et al., 2025a)训练挑战者在边界附近提出问题，而求解者则从多数票伪标签中学习。 SQLMtrains a challenger to pose questions near that frontier while the solver learns from majority-vote pseudo-labels. SQLM(Chen et al., 2025c)使用相同的结构并获取代理标签uses the same structure and obtains the proxy label𝒚^∗\hat{\bm{y}}^{*}通过独立解决者的投票。语言自玩 (LSP)through voting across independent solvers. Language Self-Play (LSP)(Kuba et al., 2025)通过将任务生成制定为极小极大游戏来删除初始数据集：removes the initial dataset by formulating task generation as a minimax game:

maxθSminθP𝔼𝒒∼πθP,𝒂∼πθS(⋅|𝒒)[ℛ(𝒒,𝒂)].\max_{\theta_{S}}\ \min_{\theta_{P}}\ \mathbb{E}_{\bm{q}\sim\pi_{\theta_{P}},\ \bm{a}\sim\pi_{\theta_{S}}(\cdot|\bm{q})}\!\left[\mathcal{R}(\bm{q},\bm{a})\right]. (19)

极小极大耦合允许任务分配在没有初始数据集的情况下响应求解器不断变化的性能。最近的工作通过额外的角色和内部信号丰富了游戏。多智能体进化The minimax coupling allows the task distribution to respond to the solver’s changing performance without an initial dataset. Recent work enriches the game with additional roles and internal signals. Multi-Agent Evolve(Chen et al., 2025j)使用共享骨干网中的提议者、求解者和判断者角色来估计没有规则可检查答案的开放式问题的难度和质量。苏格拉底式零uses proposer, solver, and judge roles from a shared backbone to estimate the difficulty and quality of open-ended questions without rule-checkable answers. Socratic-Zero(Wang et al., 2025f)协调教师、求解器和生成器的角色，使教师能够针对求解器的弱点，而生成器则可以将教学策略提炼成可扩展的课程。航空航天coordinates teacher, solver, and generator roles, allowing the teacher to target the solver’s weaknesses and the generator to distill that teaching strategy into a scalable curriculum. AERO(Gao et al., 2026)使用基于熵的定位和反事实校正来定位求解器的学习前沿。 G-零uses entropy-based positioning and counterfactual correction to locate the solver’s learning frontier. G-Zero(Huang et al., 2026b)用内在判断取代外部判断replaces an external judge with an intrinsicHint-​δ\text{Hint-}\delta奖励，衡量自我生成的提示如何改变求解器的预测分布。在这些方法中，任务供应会进行调整，而周围环境或验证机制保持固定。进一步扩展循环需要评估和经验生成共同适应。reward that measures how a self-generated hint shifts the solver’s predictive distribution. Across these methods, the task supply adapts while the surrounding environment or validation mechanism remains fixed. Extending the loop further requires evaluation and experience generation to adapt together.

基于语料库和文档的自我对弈。Corpus- and document-grounded self-play.

由于无根据的自我对弈往往会重复使用求解器已知的概念，因此基于语料库的方法会从外部文档中提取任务内容。香料Because ungrounded self-play tends to recycle concepts the solver already knows, corpus-grounded methods draw task content from external documents. SPICE(Liu et al., 2025b)在挖掘文档并编写推理问题的挑战者和解决这些问题的推理者之间交替使用一种模型。范围alternates one model between a Challenger that mines documents and writes reasoning problems and a Reasoner that solves them. SCOPE(Kwan et al., 2026)将相关设计应用于开放式任务，其中挑战者在求解器边界附近编写基于文档的问题，以及基本模型的冻结副本，用于生成特定于任务的评分标准并对答案进行评分。搜索自玩applies a related design to open-ended tasks, with a Challenger that writes document-grounded questions near the Solver’s frontier and a frozen copy of the base model that produces task-specific rubrics and grades the responses. Search Self-play(Lu et al., 2025b)通过生成搜索查询并在每个搜索轨迹上使用检索增强生成来提供训练目标，以深度搜索代理为目标。在每种情况下，接地都会将任务内容扩展到提议者现有的先验之外。targets deep-search agents by generating search queries and using retrieval-augmented generation over each search trajectory to provide training targets. In each case, grounding expands the task content beyond the proposer’s existing priors.

无奖励的自我培训和自行生成的课程。Reward-free self-training and self-generated curricula.

几种边界方法使任务源和环境基本上保持不变，但会调整模型根据其自身输出进行训练的方式。硒PTSeveral boundary methods leave the source of tasks and the environment essentially unchanged but adapt how the model trains on its own outputs. SePT(Li et al., 2025c)在刷新的自生成轨迹上交替进行响应采样和微调，无需奖励模型、验证者或教师。自我发展的课程alternates between response sampling and fine-tuning on refreshed self-generated traces without a reward model, verifier, or teacher. Self-Evolving Curriculum(Chen et al., 2025i)将任务排序建模为非固定多臂老虎机，其臂代表难度级别或问题类别，其回报是绝对策略梯度优势。 E2H推理机models task ordering as a non-stationary multi-armed bandit whose arms represent difficulty levels or problem categories and whose payoff is the absolute policy-gradient advantage. E2H Reasoner(Parashar et al., 2025)同样，将任务从简单到困难排序，并逐渐减少遇到简单问题的机会。在测试时，TTCSsimilarly orders tasks from easy to hard and gradually reduces exposure to easy problems. At test time, TTCS(Yang et al., 2026a)通过生成本地课程变体来共同进化合成器和求解器，这些变体帮助求解器在自我一致性奖励下从更容易的相邻问题中引导。开放式SIRco-evolves a synthesizer and solver by generating local curriculum variants that help the solver bootstrap from easier neighboring problems under self-consistency rewards. OpenSIR(Kwan et al., 2025)结合了多样性和难度奖励，以便教师和学生对可以扩展到超越最小种子所代表的概念。这些方法改进了经验的组织，而无需独立改变其任务和环境来源。combines diversity and difficulty rewards so that a teacher and student pair can expand beyond concepts represented by a minimal seed. These methods improve the organization of experience without independently changing both its task and environment sources.

4.2.2. 固定任务或种子任务的环境搭建4.2.2. Environment Construction for Fixed or Seeded Tasks

补充路线使任务分配基本保持不变，但可以让模型构建或扩展环境A complementary route leaves the task distribution largely intact but lets the model construct or expand the environment(𝒮,𝒫)(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{P})其中积累了经验。限制因素通常是生成和验证轨迹所需的交互世界的成本和脆弱性，而不是问题的可用性。这种约束对于长期代理任务尤其重要in which experience is gathered. The limiting factor is often the cost and brittleness of the interactive world needed to produce and verify trajectories, not the availability of questions. This constraint is especially important for long-horizon agentic tasks(Li et al., 2026e)在连续的 POMDP 体系中（in the sequential POMDP regime (T>1T{>}1）在章节中介绍) introduced in Section2.1，其中人工编写的模拟器或验证器可能占开发工作的大部分。当前的方法使用对抗性环境设计、可执行代码合成、学习世界模型、实时应用程序和持久内存来构建动态和界面，通过这些动态和界面，种子任务成为有用的经验。, where a human-authored simulator or verifier may account for much of the development effort. Current approaches use adversarial environment design, executable code synthesis, learned world models, live applications, and persistent memory to construct the dynamics and interfaces through which seeded tasks become useful experience.

自动环境设计。Automatic environment design.

无监督环境设计 (UED) 的工作早于 LRM，与 PAIREDWork on Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) predates LRMs, with PAIRED(Dennis et al., 2020)通过环境生成对手与主角和对手之间的游戏来形式化该方法。 为了避免生成根本不可能的世界，对手最大化formalizing the approach through a game between an environment-generating adversary and a protagonist and antagonist pair. To avoid generating worlds that are merely impossible, the adversary maximizes遗憾regret而不是主角的失败。遗憾是与反应最佳的对手回归之间的差距instead of the protagonist’s failure. Regret is the gap between the return of the best-responding antagonistπA\pi^{A}和主角and the protagonistπP\pi^{P}在生成的查询上on a generated query𝒒\bm{q},

Regret⁡(𝒒)=U⁡(πA,𝒒)−U⁡(πP,𝒒),\mathrm{Regret}(\bm{q})=U(\pi^{A},\bm{q})-U(\pi^{P},\bm{q}), (20)

当对手能够解决挑战主角的环境时，它就会提供信息。 对于环境构建，遗憾与方程中的可学习性信号具有相同的目的。which becomes informative when the antagonist can solve an environment that challenges the protagonist. For environment construction, regret serves the same purpose that the learnability signal in Eq.18用于任务生成。两者都创建了一个自动课程，有利于政策能力边界附近的经验。随后的 UED 变体完善了后悔的估计和环境参数的采样，但保留了有用的环境既不应该挑战也不应该压倒当前政策的原则。serves for task generation. Both create an automatic curriculum that favors experience near the policy’s competence frontier. Subsequent UED variants refine the estimation of regret and the sampling of environment parameters, but retain the principle that useful environments should neither under-challenge nor overwhelm the current policy.

环境作为可执行代码。Environments as executable code.

将环境表示为代码为 LRM 提供了一条实用的途径。尤里卡Representing the environment as code offers a practical route for LRMs. Eureka(Ma et al., 2023)使用法学硕士编写奖励代码并通过模拟器反馈对其进行完善，从而自动化部分奖励设计uses an LLM to write reward code and refine it through simulator feedback, thereby automating part of reward design withinℰ\mathcal{E}。全能史诗. OMNI-EPIC(Faldor et al., 2024)生成环境和终止代码，然后应用兴趣模型来保留可学习且新颖的任务。对于工具使用，自我挑战代理generates environment and termination code and then applies an interestingness model to retain tasks that are both learnable and novel. For tool use, Self-Challenging agents(Zhou et al., 2025c)将每个生成的任务与可执行验证函数配对，允许代码执行来评估生成的轨迹。pair each generated task with an executable verification function, allowing code execution to evaluate the resulting trajectory.

可执行工具环境的可扩展综合。Scalable synthesis of executable tool environments.

由于交互环境通常比代理强化学习中的问题更稀缺，因此最近的工作试图在不牺牲可靠性的情况下增加其供应。特工世界模型Because interactive environments are often scarcer than questions in agentic RL, recent work seeks to increase their supply without sacrificing reliability. Agent World Model(Wang et al., 2026e)将环境分解为有状态的后端、工具接口和成功标准，然后使用分阶段的 LLM 管道来综合数据库支持的环境并纠正无效组件。缩放环境decomposes an environment into a stateful backend, tool interface, and success criteria, then uses a staged LLM pipeline to synthesize database-backed environments and correct invalid components. ScaleEnv(Tu et al., 2026)扩展工具依赖关系图并通过执行验证操作，生成交互式环境以及可验证的任务。随机世界expands tool-dependency graphs and verifies actions through execution, producing interactive environments together with verifiable tasks. RandomWorld(Sullivan et al., 2025)从细粒度类型层次结构中按程序生成工具和组合任务。硫酸亚铜procedurally generates tools and compositional tasks from a fine-grained type hierarchy. CuES(Mai et al., 2025)通过内在的好奇心探索新环境，并将观察到的交互抽象为可重用的任务模式。进化环境explores a new environment through intrinsic curiosity and abstracts observed interactions into reusable task schemas. EvoEnv(Shi et al., 2026)通过分阶段验证、语义审查、难度校准和新颖性检查来构建 Python 环境。环境调整constructs Python environments through staged validation, semantic review, difficulty calibration, and novelty checks. Environment Tuning(Lu et al., 2025c)通过纠正反馈和基于进度的课程来增强环境，使培训超越静态轨迹。augments the environment with corrective feedback and a progress-based curriculum, allowing training to move beyond static trajectories.

生成世界模型。Generative world models.

符号代码的替代方法是学习动态An alternative to symbolic code is to learn the dynamics𝒫\mathcal{P}直接。精灵directly. Genie(Bruce et al., 2024)从没有真实动作标签的未标记视频中训练动作可控的世界模型，展示了如何trains an action-controllable world model from unlabeled videos without ground-truth action labels, showing how(𝒮,𝒫)(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{P})可以从被动数据中学习，而不是完全手动指定。can be learned from passive data instead of being specified entirely by hand.

持久且自适应的交互环境。Persistent and adaptive interaction contexts.

实时应用程序和持久内存提供了另一种扩展经验收集环境的方法。零图形用户界面Live applications and persistent memory offer another way to extend the context in which experience is gathered. ZeroGUI(Yang et al., 2025b)使用视觉语言模型从当前界面生成任务，并根据轨迹屏幕截图估计奖励，减少对手写验证脚本和注释成功轨迹的依赖。 SEA代理uses a vision-language model to generate tasks from the current interface and estimate rewards from trajectory screenshots, reducing its reliance on handwritten verifier scripts and annotated success trajectories. SEAgent(Sun et al., 2025d)训练世界状态模型来描述计算机环境的状态并评估沿轨迹的操作。内存代理trains a World State Model to describe the state of a computer environment and evaluate actions along a trajectory. MemAgent(Yu et al., 2025a)学习可以在对话过程中读取和更新的记忆，从而扩大了经验积累的范围。航行者号learns memory that can be read and updated across conversation turns, extending the horizon over which experience can accumulate. Voyager(Wang et al., 2023a)在具体环境中将自动课程与不断增长的可执行技能库相结合。更广泛地说，经验驱动的终身学习框架combines an automatic curriculum with a growing library of executable skills in an embodied environment. More broadly, experience-driven lifelong learning frameworks(Cai et al., 2025)强调过去交互的积累和重用，与体验时代持续交互可以支持持久能力增长的观点一致emphasize the accumulation and reuse of past interactions, consistent with the Experience Era view that sustained interaction can support durable capability growth(Silver & Sutton, 2025).

4.3. 综合体验生成4.3. Integrated Experience Generation

集成经验生成对应于阶梯中的 L3，其中任务和环境管道可以在仅接收有限的外部种子后继续产生训练轨迹。该策略不需要创建每个工件本身，因为另一个模型可以综合经验，语料库可以提供其内容，或者实时环境可以提供交互。这些资源可以继续为学习奠定基础，而不需要人们策划每项新任务和设置。当一种方法生成或发现的经验支持在有限的额外管理下继续训练时，该方法就进入了这种状态。Integrated experience generation corresponds to L3 in the ladder, where the task and environment pipeline can continue to produce training trajectories after receiving only limited external seeds. The policy need not create every artifact itself because another model may synthesize the experience, a corpus may supply its content, or a live environment may provide the interactions. Such sources can continue to ground learning without requiring people to curate each new task and setting. A method enters this regime when its generated or discovered experience supports continued training with limited additional curation.

集成的任务和环境管道。Integrated task and environment pipelines.

最近的几种系统将自动提供的任务、可执行环境和自动反馈结合在一个管道中。零图形用户界面Several recent systems combine automatically supplied tasks, executable environments, and automatic feedback in one pipeline. ZeroGUI(Yang et al., 2025b)通过使用视觉语言模型从实时界面提出任务并在多数确认下根据轨迹屏幕截图估计奖励，说明了 GUI 领域的边界情况。由于应用程序动态和预训练的视觉评估器仍然是外部提供的，因此该方法减少了任务管理和手工奖励注释，而无需独立生成其验证器或环境。 特工世界模型illustrates a boundary case in the GUI domain by using a vision-language model to propose tasks from a live interface and estimate rewards from trajectory screenshots under majority confirmation. Because the application dynamics and pretrained visual evaluator remain externally supplied, the method reduces task curation and handcrafted reward annotation without independently generating its verifier or environment. Agent World Model(Wang et al., 2026e)使用具有自动校正功能的分阶段 LLM 管道来综合紧凑模式中的有状态后端、工具接口、任务和成功标准。缩放环境uses a staged LLM pipeline with automated correction to synthesize stateful backends, tool interfaces, tasks, and success criteria from compact schemas. ScaleEnv(Tu et al., 2026)将工具依赖图扩展与可执行操作验证相结合，构建跨多个域的可解决任务和交互环境。进化环境combines tool-dependency-graph expansion with executable action verification to construct solvable tasks and interactive environments across multiple domains. EvoEnv(Shi et al., 2026)仅在验证、语义审查、难度校准和新颖性检查之后才承认合成的 Python 环境。这些系统通过生成用于交互的设置和任务来减少持续的人工管理。硫酸亚铜admits a synthesized Python environment only after validation, semantic review, difficulty calibration, and novelty checks. These systems reduce continued human curation by generating both the setting and the tasks used for interaction. CuES(Mai et al., 2025)标志着一个边界，因为它通过好奇心驱动的探索得出可重用的任务模式，无需手工制作任务种子，同时保留外部提供的初始环境。marks a boundary because it derives reusable task schemas through curiosity-driven exploration without handcrafted task seeds while retaining an externally supplied initial environment.

边界情况。Boundary cases.

当提议者和求解器循环依赖固定的 Python 解释器发明数学问题时，任务分配会进行调整，但执行和检查仍然是外部设计的。生成世界模型，例如 GenieWhen a proposer and solver loop invents mathematical questions while relying on a fixed Python interpreter, the task distribution adapts but execution and checking remain externally designed. A generative world model such as Genie(Bruce et al., 2024)改变环境，同时让下游任务分配和奖励由外部指定。这些案例仅沿体验轴的一部分减少了人性化设计。集成体验生成需要生成或发现的任务以及执行它们所需的上下文，以提供持续的序列changes the environment while leaving the downstream task distribution and reward externally specified. These cases reduce human design along only one part of the experience axis. Integrated experience generation requires the generated or discovered tasks and the contexts needed to execute them to provide an ongoing sequence of(𝒒,τ,ℛ)(\bm{q},\tau,\mathcal{R})用于培训。该标准仍然允许种子、语料库和实时应用程序提供外部基础。for training. This criterion still permits seeds, corpora, and live applications to provide external grounding.

4.4. 自主协同进化4.4. Autonomous Co-evolution

自主协同进化对应于阶梯中的L4，其中奖励获取、任务生成、环境构建和策略改进形成一个持久的自适应循环，其中一个组件的变化重塑其他组件。人们仍然可以定义广泛的目标、操作边界和独立审计，而无需在整个培训过程中维护课程或评估员。这种机制将当前的 LRM 研究与早期的开放式学习工作联系起来。诗人Autonomous co-evolution corresponds to L4 in the ladder, where reward acquisition, task generation, environment construction, and policy improvement form a persistent adaptive loop in which changes to one component reshape the others. Humans may still define broad objectives, operational boundaries, and independent audits without maintaining the curriculum or evaluator throughout training. This regime connects current LRM research with earlier work on open-ended learning. POET(Wang et al., 2019)尽管其奖励仍然是外部指定的，但环境和代理群体共同进化，同时在不断扩展的课程中转移解决方案。全能史诗co-evolves populations of environments and agents while transferring solutions across an expanding curriculum, although its reward remains externally specified. OMNI-EPIC(Faldor et al., 2024)在过滤可学习且有趣的任务之前生成环境和奖励代码。自我奖励语言模型generates environment and reward code before filtering for tasks that are both learnable and interesting. Self-Rewarding Language Models(Yuan et al., 2024)通过在连续迭代中使用策略作为自己的判断来耦合奖励和策略，同时让任务分配和环境由人为设计。这些例子结合了学习循环的不同子集，而完整的制度需要经验生成和评估来共同持续改进，而无需外部维护的课程。couple reward and policy by using the policy as its own judge across successive iterations, while leaving the task distribution and environment human-designed. These examples couple different subsets of the learning loop, whereas the full regime requires experience generation and evaluation to continue improving together without an externally maintained curriculum.

耦合生成器和验证器循环。Coupled generator and verifier loops.

治愈CURE(Wang et al., 2025k)将代码和单元测试生成结合在一起，以便程序暴露生成的测试中的弱点，并且更强的测试为后续程序提供更多信息反馈。可执行运行时保持固定，但有效的验证环境与生成器相适应。 AZRcouples code and unit-test generation so that programs expose weaknesses in generated tests and stronger tests provide more informative feedback for subsequent programs. The executable runtime remains fixed, but the effective verification environment adapts with the generator. AZR(Zhao et al., 2025a)和R-零and R-Zero(Huang et al., 2025a)关闭提议者和求解者之间的循环，同时保留固定的执行或验证机制，使它们成为外部定义环境中共同发展课程的例子。自我挑战特工close the loop between proposer and solver while retaining a fixed execution or validation mechanism, making them examples of co-evolving curricula within an externally defined environment. Self-Challenging agents(Zhou et al., 2025c)综合synthesize代码即任务Code-as-Task捆绑指令、可执行验证函数、参考解决方案和失败案例的实例，从而将任务生成与可执行反馈耦合起来，同时使底层工具系统由外部定义。instances that bundle an instruction, executable verification function, reference solution, and failure cases, thereby coupling task generation with executable feedback while leaving the underlying tool system externally defined.

自我游戏和适应性课程。Self-play and adaptive curricula.

螺旋SPIRAL(Liu et al., 2025a)在两人零和语言游戏的两个角色中训练单一策略，以对抗改进自身的副本。当对手在等式形式的目标下增强时。trains a single policy in both roles of a two-player zero-sum language game against improving copies of itself. As the opponent strengthens under an objective of the form in Eq.19，配对序列成为一种适应性课程，可以引发可迁移的推理策略。由于游戏规则保持不变，SPIRAL 会调整其对手和课程，而无需生成底层环境。不断变化的对手还引入了非平稳性和更高的梯度方差。角色条件优势估计通过根据其角色的预期回报标准化每次部署来解决这种不稳定性，类似于等式 1 中 GRPO 的群体相对基线。, the sequence of matchups becomes an adaptive curriculum that can elicit transferable reasoning strategies. Because the game rules remain fixed, SPIRAL adapts its opponents and curriculum without generating the underlying environment. The shifting opponent also introduces non-stationarity and higher gradient variance. Role-conditioned advantage estimation addresses this instability by normalizing each rollout against the expected return of its role, analogous to the group-relative baseline of GRPO in Eq.6。闭环经验的生成依赖于相同的循环关系：政策改进必须引发更多信息丰富的任务和环境，然后这些任务和环境必须支持进一步的政策改进。诗人. Closed-loop experience generation depends on the same recurrent relationship: policy improvements must induce more informative tasks and environments, which must then support further policy improvement. POET(Wang et al., 2019)和 OMNI-EPICand OMNI-EPIC(Faldor et al., 2024)在 RL 设置中追求这种形式的开放式耦合pursue this form of open-ended coupling in RL settings(Silver & Sutton, 2025).

难度一致的智能体和环境共同进化。Difficulty-aligned agent and environment co-evolution.

基因环境GenEnv(Guo et al., 2025c)通过将代理 LLM 与生成模拟器配对，使环境成为可学习的组件。安makes the environment a learned component by pairing an agent LLM with a generative simulator. Anα\alpha-课程奖励将生成的任务引导至智能体的最近发展区域，扩展了等式中的遗憾目标。-curriculum reward steers the generated tasks toward the agent’s zone of proximal development, extending the regret objective in Eq.20和方程中的可学习性信号。and the learnability signal in Eq.18到一个学习的环境。特工世界to a learned environment. Agent-World(Dong et al., 2026)从数据库和可执行工具集构建一个竞技场，然后使用连续几轮的多环境强化学习来诊断能力差距并综合目标任务。竞技游戏引擎提供了自适应体验的另一个来源。文本竞技场constructs an arena from databases and executable toolsets, then uses successive rounds of multi-environment RL to diagnose capability gaps and synthesize targeted tasks. Competitive game engines provide another source of adaptive experience. TextArena(Guertler et al., 2025)提供语言游戏，其自我游戏动态为社交和战略技能创建自动难度课程，同时为 SPIRAL 等方法提供基础。offers language games whose self-play dynamics create an automatic difficulty curriculum for social and strategic skills while providing a substrate for methods such as SPIRAL.

代理人本身的开放式自我完善。Open-ended self-improvement of the agent itself.

一些系统将协同进化从任务和环境扩展到代理本身的设计Some systems extend co-evolution beyond tasks and environments to the design of the agent itself(Liu et al., 2026e; Liu et al., 2026f; Liu et al., 2026d)。达尔文哥德尔机器. The Darwin Gödel Machine(Zhang et al., 2025b)维护编码代理的档案，这些编码代理修改自己的代码并从存档的祖先中分支以探索替代设计。基础模型自我对弈maintains an archive of coding agents that modify their own code and branch from archived ancestors to explore alternative designs. Foundation Model Self-Play(Dharna et al., 2025)通过竞争性比赛和质量多样性搜索提出并完善代理策略。自动化能力发现proposes and refines agent strategies through competitive play and quality-diversity search. Automated Capability Discovery(Lu et al., 2025a)使用一种基础模型来提出和评估另一种模型的任务，从而在不依赖于固定的手工设计基准的情况下暴露功能和失败。uses one foundation model to propose and evaluate tasks for another, exposing capabilities and failures without relying on a fixed hand-designed benchmark.

4.5. 跨经验轴的故障模式4.5. Failure Modes across the Experience Axis

随着任务和环境生成变得具有适应性，生成的经验中的错误可以直接反馈到后续的策略更新中。在任务方面，较差的难度校准和有限的多样性可能会剥夺有用的学习信号的策略。在环境方面，不准确或昂贵的模拟可能会破坏它们产生的轨迹。一旦这些组件耦合起来，非平稳性和相互强化就会为错误的持续存在创造额外的路径。As task and environment generation become adaptive, errors in the generated experience can feed directly into subsequent policy updates. On the task side, poor difficulty calibration and limited diversity can deprive the policy of useful learning signals. On the environment side, inaccurate or expensive simulations can undermine the trajectories they produce. Once these components are coupled, non-stationarity and mutual reinforcement create additional paths for errors to persist.

难以校准。Difficulty miscalibration.

如果不受约束的提议者倾向于琐碎或无法解决的任务，则群体奖励几乎恒定，并且 GRPO 优势If an unconstrained proposer drifts toward trivial or unsolvable tasks, group rewards become nearly constant and the GRPO advantagesA^i\hat{A}_{i}在等式中in Eq.6消失，导致学习停滞。方程中的可学习性信号。vanish, causing learning to stall. The learnability signal in Eq.18，方程中的遗憾目标。, the regret objective in Eq.20，以及 R-Zero 中的前沿目标挑战者, and the frontier-targeting challenger in R-Zero(Huang et al., 2025a)所有人都试图保留一个信息丰富的难度范围。它们的有效性取决于对解决率的估计all seek to preserve an informative difficulty range. Their effectiveness depends on estimates of the solve ratep¯θS\bar{p}_{\theta_{S}}，这在训练初期可能会很吵。, which may be noisy early in training.

协同自适应奖励黑客。Co-adaptive reward hacking.

当Whenℛ\mathcal{R}, Π\Pi, 和, and(𝒮,𝒫)(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{P})是在学习循环中生成的，求解器可能会利用验证器中的盲点而不是获取真正的能力，从而扩展了第 1 节中讨论的奖励黑客问题are generated within the learning loop, the solver may exploit blind spots in the verifier instead of acquiring genuine capability, extending the reward-hacking problem discussed in Section3。当验证者随策略变化时，漂移变得更难检测，并且生成器和分级器可能会收敛于内部一致但外部无效的约定。 CURE 中的单元测试. Drift becomes harder to detect when the verifier changes with the policy, and the generator and grader may converge on conventions that are internally consistent but externally invalid. Unit tests in CURE(Wang et al., 2025k)，自我挑战代理中的代码验证, code verification in Self-Challenging agents(Zhou et al., 2025c)，以及 SPIRAL 中的游戏结果, and game outcomes in SPIRAL(Liu et al., 2025a)限制这种行为而不消除生成的测试或游戏中的系统偏差。内部评分造成了进一步的不对称，因为模型可能在生成答案方面有所改进，但在检查答案时却变得同样可靠constrain this behavior without eliminating systematic bias in the generated tests or games. Internal grading creates a further asymmetry because models may improve at producing answers without becoming equally reliable at checking them(Chen et al., 2026b)。因此，可靠的验证可能需要其自己的培训目标和独立的审核。. Reliable verification may therefore require its own training objective and independent audits.

分布缩小和模式崩溃。Distributional narrowing and mode collapse.

自生成的任务流可以集中在模型已经处理良好的区域，从而减少了任务的多样性Self-generated task streams can concentrate on regions the model already handles well, reducing the diversity ofΠ\Pi历经几代人。 SeRL 中的多维过滤over successive generations. Multidimensional filtering in SeRL(Fang et al., 2025b)，LSP的对抗目标, the adversarial objective of LSP(Kuba et al., 2025)在等式中in Eq.19以及开放式学习中的兴趣模型, and interestingness models in open-ended learning(Faldor et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2019)引入多样性压力有助于保持课程的扩展。introduce diversity pressure that helps keep the curriculum expanding.

非平稳性和不稳定性。Non-stationarity and instability.

在完全耦合的循环中，环境或对手会随着策略的学习而变化，这偏离了单轮 RLVR 中常见的固定数据和奖励假设。由此产生的非平稳性会增加多智能体训练中的梯度方差，尽管角色条件或组相对基线In fully coupled loops, the environment or opponent changes as the policy learns, departing from the fixed data and reward assumptions common in single-turn RLVR. The resulting non-stationarity can increase gradient variance in multi-agent training, although role-conditioned or group-relative baselines(Liu et al., 2025a)可以提高稳定性。这也加剧了长期信用分配的难度。can improve stability. It also compounds the long-horizon credit-assignment difficulty of theT>1T{>}1机制，其中单个标量结果必须分布在整个自生成轨迹上。regime, where a single scalar outcome must be distributed over an entire self-generated trajectory.

环境保真度和成本。Environment fidelity and cost.

构建环境的可靠性取决于构建和评估它的模型。视觉语言奖励估计The reliability of a constructed environment depends on the models that build and evaluate it. Vision-language reward estimation(Yang et al., 2025b)和世界国家建模and world-state modeling(Sun et al., 2025d)从其底层模型继承错误和偏差，这些错误和偏差可以通过环境中收集的每个轨迹传播。验证生成的模拟器或测试套件还可能重新引入自主体验生成旨在减少的大部分监督成本。来自闭环自我进化的证据进一步表明，内部生成的监管可能会稳定在预言机监管之下inherit errors and biases from their underlying models, which can propagate through every trajectory collected in the environment. Verifying a generated simulator or test suite may also reintroduce much of the supervision cost that autonomous experience generation seeks to reduce. Evidence from closed-loop self-evolution further suggests that internally generated supervision may plateau below oracle supervision(Qi et al., 2026b)，即使在很大程度上自主的体验管道中也支持使用独立锚点。, supporting the use of independent anchors even in a largely autonomous experience pipeline.

领域格局和评估影响。Domain landscape and evaluation implications.

自主体验生成的主要限制因领域而异。在数学和代码中，可执行检查器为生成的任务提供了相对较强的基础，尽管提议者仍然可能在没有语料库基础的情况下崩溃到回收模板The main constraint on autonomous experience generation differs by domain. In mathematics and code, executable checkers provide relatively strong grounding for generated tasks, although proposers may still collapse to recycled templates without corpus grounding(Liu et al., 2025b; Kwan et al., 2025)。在工具使用和计算机控制中，环境保真度通常是比问题稀缺性更严格的约束. In tool use and computer control, environment fidelity is often a tighter constraint than question scarcity(Wang et al., 2026e; Tu et al., 2026; Zhou et al., 2025c)。因此，评估必须测量模拟器的正确性并转移到保留的应用程序，如第 1 节中所述. Evaluation must therefore measure simulator correctness and transfer to held-out applications as discussed in Section5.3。在开放式和社交环境中，自我游戏可以产生适应性课程. In open-ended and social settings, self-play can produce an adaptive curriculum(Liu et al., 2025a; Guertler et al., 2025)，但同样的耦合会放大提议者和法官之间的勾结, but the same coupling can amplify collusion between the proposer and judge(Yuan et al., 2024).

我们的目标不是以牺牲接地为代价来最大化自主权。稳定的学习可能仍然需要共识、执行反馈、语料库基础或偶尔的人工审核。相关问题是，在仍然有足够的独立证据来检测漂移和崩溃的同时，是否可以减少持续的人工管理，这激发了下一节中制定的评估标准。The goal is not to maximize autonomy at the expense of grounding. Stable learning may still require consensus, execution feedback, corpus grounding, or occasional human audits. The relevant question is whether continual human curation can be reduced while enough independent evidence remains to detect drift and collapse, which motivates the evaluation criteria developed in the next section.

5. 评估、数据集和基准5. Evaluation, Datasets, and Benchmarks

评估超越人类监督的学习需要关于三个不同对象的证据：政策、用于训练政策的反馈以及政策学习的经验。较高的保留分数表明能力发生了变化，但并不能证明培训奖励仍然可靠，或者生成的课程仍然广泛和有效。因此，我们的评估框架将政策能力、反馈保真度和体验质量分开。随着奖励获取和经验生成进入学习循环，这些形式的证据变得共同重要。Evaluating learning beyond human supervision requires evidence about three distinct objects: the policy, the feedback used to train it, and the experience from which it learns. A higher held-out score establishes a change in capability, but does not establish that the training reward remained faithful or that a generated curriculum remained broad and valid. Our evaluation framework consequently separates policy capability, feedback fidelity, and experience quality. These forms of evidence become jointly important as reward acquisition and experience generation move into the learning loop.

这三种形式的证据之间的平衡在第二节的阶梯上发生了变化。The balance among these three forms of evidence changes across the ladder in Sec.2.5。在 L0 处，人类的判断直接定义了坚持的标准。在 L1 和 L2 中，评估还必须测试可重用的评估器和用于学习的奖励源。在 L3 中，任务和环境质量成为明确的评估目标，而 L4 则需要关于耦合学习循环稳定性的纵向证据。表格. At L0, human judgments directly define the held-out standard. At L1 and L2, evaluation must also test the reusable evaluators and reward sources used for learning. At L3, task and environment quality become explicit evaluation targets, while L4 requires longitudinal evidence about the stability of the coupled learning loop. Tables4和and5总结代表性套件和指标。表summarize representative suites and metrics. Table6将三部分框架转化为五个阶梯级别的报告要求。translates the three-part framework into reporting requirements for the five ladder levels.

5.1. 能力评估5.1. Capability Evaluation

能力评估衡量政策是否Capability evaluation measures whether the policyπθ\pi_{\theta}解决现有标准下的预期问题。培训奖励的来源并不能决定适当的能力测试。使用模型衍生奖励训练的策略仍然可以在人类编写的数学问题上进行评估，而在不断变化的环境中训练的代理仍然可以在固定的沙箱中进行测试。我们根据输出的评估方式对能力基准进行分组，因为这种选择决定了评估的可靠性、成本和可扩展性。solves the intended problems under a held-out standard. The source of the training reward does not determine the appropriate capability test. A policy trained with model-derived rewards may still be evaluated on human-authored mathematics problems, while an agent trained in evolving environments may still be tested in a fixed sandbox. We group capability benchmarks by how their outputs are assessed because this choice determines the reliability, cost, and scalability of evaluation.

5.1.1. 难以验证的推理5.1.1. Hard-verifiable reasoning

硬验证任务允许通过精确的数字或符号匹配、单元测试、游戏结果或约束满足进行确定性检查。它们支持可扩展的评估，并为减少监督的训练方法提供相对可靠的外部锚点。Hard-verifiable tasks admit deterministic checking through exact numerical or symbolic matching, unit tests, game outcomes, or constraint satisfaction. They support scalable evaluation and provide a comparatively reliable external anchor for methods trained with reduced supervision.

数学推理。Mathematical reasoning.

数学是评估 LRM 最广泛使用的领域之一Mathematics is among the most widely used domains for evaluating LRMs(Ahn et al., 2024)。已建立的套件包括 GSM8K. Established suites include GSM8K(Cobbe et al., 2021)、数学和数学500, MATH and MATH500(Hendrycks et al., 2021b; Lightman et al., 2023)，以及AMC和AIME的竞争问题, and competition problems from AMC and AIME(Li et al., 2024b; Mathematical Association of America, 2024; Mathematical Association of America, 2025)。全数学. Omni-MATH(Gao et al., 2024), 奥林匹克长凳, OlympiadBench(He et al., 2024a), 前沿数学, FrontierMath(Glazer et al., 2024), LiveMathBench (Liu et al., 2024b)，和随机计算, and RandomCalculation(Wu et al., 2025b)将覆盖范围扩展到更难或更动态的设置。 PutnamBench 评估形式定理证明extend coverage to harder or more dynamic settings. PutnamBench evaluates formal theorem proving(Tsoukalas et al., 2024)，而 SciBench 则测试大学水平的科学问题解决能力, while SciBench tests college-level scientific problem solving(Wang et al., 2023b).

MathArena 通过持续维护的集合拓宽了这一领域，涵盖奥林匹克问题、以证明为导向的竞赛、来自 arXiv 的研究问题以及精益中的正式证明生成MathArena broadens this landscape with a continuously maintained collection spanning olympiad problems, proof-oriented competitions, research questions drawn from arXiv, and formal proof generation in Lean(Dekoninck et al., 2026)。其任务将最终答案的准确性与证明构造和形式化区分开来。这种区别很重要，因为 Pass@. Its tasks distinguish final-answer accuracy from proof construction and formalization. This distinction matters because Pass@11不能揭示模型在样本之间是否一致或其中间推理是否有效。通过@does not reveal whether a model is consistent across samples or whether its intermediate reasoning is valid. Pass@kk，平均样本准确度，G-Pass@, mean sample accuracy, G-Pass@kk，和 CoT-Pass@, and CoT-Pass@kk提供补充证据，如第 2 节所述。provide complementary evidence, as discussed in Sec.5.5。结果还受益于难度和子集细分。数学的单一汇总可以掩盖收益是否仅限于常规代数或扩展到奥林匹克级别的问题. Results also benefit from difficulty and subset breakdowns. A single aggregate on MATH can conceal whether gains are confined to routine algebra or extend to olympiad-level problems(Gao et al., 2024; Glazer et al., 2024)。固定的评分脚本进一步改进了模型之间的比较。 GSM-Symbolic 通过更改名称、值和模板来添加稳健性检查，同时保留底层推理结构. Fixed grading scripts further improve comparisons across models. GSM-Symbolic adds a robustness check by changing names, values, and templates while preserving the underlying reasoning structure(Mirzadeh et al., 2024).

代码生成和软件工程。Code generation and software engineering.

可执行测试提供了代码生成中功能正确性的直接测量。人类评估Executable tests provide a direct measure of functional correctness in code generation. HumanEval(Chen et al., 2021),MBPP, MBPP(Austin et al., 2021)、和评估加号, and EvalPlus(Liu et al., 2023a)被广泛用于此目的。大代码平台are widely used for this purpose. BigCodeBench(Zhuo et al., 2024), LiveCodeBench (Jain et al., 2025), 代码竞赛, CodeContests(Li et al., 2022), 关键问题评估, CRUXEval(Gu et al., 2024a), 代码Elo, CodeElo(Quan et al., 2025)和 MHPP, and MHPP(Dai et al., 2024)扩大功能和分布范围。相似性指标，例如 CodeBLEUexpand functional and distributional coverage. Similarity metrics such as CodeBLEU(Ren et al., 2020), 代码BERT评分, CodeBERTScore(Zhou et al., 2023b)和 ICE 分数, and ICE-Score(Zhuo, 2024)可以补充执行，但当有可靠的测试可用时，它们不是任务完成情况的直接衡量标准。can complement execution, but they are less direct measures of task completion when reliable tests are available.

存储库级基准评估更广泛的软件工程形式。 SWE-长凳Repository-level benchmarks assess a broader form of software engineering. SWE-bench(Jimenez et al., 2024), 多 SWE 工作台, Multi-SWE-bench(Zan et al., 2025)和 SWE-bench 多式联运, and SWE-bench Multimodal(Yang et al., 2024a)评估存储库补丁、多语言问题解决方案和基于视觉的任务。 Active-SWE 删除提供的问题报告并要求代理发现并修复缺陷evaluate repository patches, multilingual issue resolution, and visually grounded tasks. Active-SWE removes the supplied issue report and requires agents to discover and repair defects(Li et al., 2026a)。 SWE-bench Science 涵盖科学存储库中的问题驱动修复、专家探索和系统集成. SWE-bench Science covers issue-driven repair, expert exploration, and system integration in scientific repositories(Xu et al., 2026c)。这些套件一起评估问题识别、工具使用、环境交互和补丁生成。对于使用可执行奖励进行训练的方法，评估工具必须与训练测试脱节，以限制泄漏。 SciCode 通过科学家策划的研究编程问题提供额外的转移测试. These suites evaluate problem identification, tool use, environment interaction, and patch generation together. For methods trained with executable rewards, the evaluation harness must be disjoint from the training tests to limit leakage. SciCode provides an additional transfer test through scientist-curated research programming problems(Tian et al., 2024).

逻辑谜题、游戏和多项选择。Logic puzzles, games, and multiple choice.

斑马逻辑ZebraLogic(Lin et al., 2025a), 自动逻辑, AutoLogi(Zhu et al., 2025b)，和数独台, and Sudoku-Bench(Seely et al., 2025)通过约束满足来评估结构化推理。 TextArena 使用竞争性语言游戏通过可观察的结果来评估战略行为evaluate structured reasoning through constraint satisfaction. TextArena uses competitive language games to assess strategic behavior through observable outcomes(Guertler et al., 2025)。多项选择套件，例如 AQuA. Multiple-choice suites such as AQuA(Ling et al., 2017),代码MMLU, CodeMMLU(Nguyen et al., 2025a), 逻辑质量保证, LogiQA(Liu et al., 2021)、 和 BBH, and BBH(Suzgun et al., 2022)实现廉价且可重复的比较。它们的格式可以低估生成完整解决方案的难度，并且可以奖励消除选项。 General365 通过使用仅需要 K-12 背景知识的推理问题来解决不同的困惑enable inexpensive and reproducible comparisons. Their format can understate the difficulty of generating a complete solution and can reward option elimination. General365 addresses a different confound by using reasoning problems that require only K-12 background knowledge(Liu et al., 2026a)。它的受控变体有助于将推理困难与专门的事实知识分开。. Its controlled variants help separate reasoning difficulty from specialized factual knowledge.

5.1.2. 半可验证和专家分级的任务5.1.2. Semi-verifiable and expert-graded tasks

有些任务有简洁的答案，但需要专业知识或检索支持的证据才能进行可靠的评分。 GP质量保证Some tasks have concise answers but require expertise or retrieval-backed evidence for reliable grading. GPQA(Rein et al., 2024), 人类的最后考试, Humanity’s Last Exam(Phan et al., 2025),MMLU, MMLU(Hendrycks et al., 2021a), MMLU-Pro (Wang et al., 2024c)和超级GPQA, and SuperGPQA(Du et al., 2025)测试跨学科的广泛知识和多步骤推理。它们昂贵的标签使它们成为评估数学和代码之外的迁移的有用外部锚点。test broad knowledge and multi-step reasoning across disciplines. Their expensive labels make them useful external anchors for assessing transfer beyond mathematics and code.

特定领域套件涵盖医学Domain-specific suites cover medicine(Jin et al., 2021; Chen et al., 2025b; Zuo et al., 2025a), 金融, finance(Mateega et al., 2025; Chen et al., 2024d; Xie et al., 2024a; Islam et al., 2023), 法律, law(Fei et al., 2024; Li et al., 2024a; Guha et al., 2023; Fan et al., 2025), 常识推理, commonsense reasoning(Talmor et al., 2019; Sun et al., 2024)，和实用生物学研究, and practical biology research(Laurent et al., 2024)。 SimpleQA 隔离简短的事实. SimpleQA isolates short-form factuality(Wei et al., 2024)，而 CRAG 评估检索增强生成, while CRAG evaluates retrieval-augmented generation(Yang et al., 2024b)。即使模型使用不依赖于直接人类评估的奖励进行训练，这些基准仍保留外部评分标准。因此，他们衡量的是剩余专家基础下的转移，而不是培训奖励本身的独立性。. These benchmarks retain an external grading standard even when the model was trained with rewards that did not depend on direct human evaluation. They consequently measure transfer under residual expert grounding, not the independence of the training reward itself.

5.1.3. 开放式生成和交互偏好5.1.3. Open-ended generation and interactive preference

开放式任务缺乏独特的参考答案，因此评估依赖于偏好、评分标准或实时比较。当相关的法学硕士法官同时用于培训和测试时，这种设置会产生评估员纠缠的特定风险。Open-ended tasks lack a unique reference answer, so evaluation relies on preferences, rubrics, or live comparison. This setting creates a particular risk of evaluator entanglement when related LLM judges are used for both training and testing.

遵循指令、聊天和竞技场。Instruction following, chat, and arenas.

IFE值IFEval(Zhou et al., 2023a), 羊驼毛评估, AlpacaEval(Li et al., 2023b; Dubois et al., 2024), MT 工作台, MT-Bench(Zheng et al., 2023), MT-长凳-101, MT-Bench-101(Bai et al., 2024a), 竞技场-困难, Arena-Hard(Li et al., 2024d), 野生基准, WildBench(Lin et al., 2024a)和混合评估, and MixEval(Ni et al., 2024)评估指令遵循和对话质量。头盔evaluate instruction following and conversational quality. HELM(Liang et al., 2022)和烧瓶and FLASK(Ye et al., 2023)提供更广泛的评估框架。竞技场式的胜率捕捉了大规模的比较偏好，但对冗长、响应顺序和法官选择仍然敏感provide broader evaluation frameworks. Arena-style win rates capture comparative preference at scale, but remain sensitive to verbosity, response order, and judge choice(Shi et al., 2024; Dubois et al., 2024)。更强大的协议固定评审小组并衡量其与独立人类子集的一致性。. A stronger protocol fixes the judge panel and measures its agreement with an independent human subset.

创造性、社会性和长期背景评估。Creative, social, and long-context evaluation.

点亮长凳LitBench(Fein et al., 2025), CS4 (Atmakuru et al., 2024)和 SS-GEN, and SS-GEN(Feng et al., 2025b)探索创意和叙事写作。 EQ 基准probe creative and narrative writing. EQ-Bench(Paech, 2023), 工作台, ToMBench(Chen et al., 2024g), 自我社交竞技场, EgoSocialArena(Hou et al., 2024)和社会评估, and SocialEval(Zhou et al., 2025a)检查社会推理和心理理论。长上下文套件包括 LongBenchexamine social reasoning and theory of mind. Long-context suites include LongBench(Bai et al., 2024b), 长凳 v2, LongBench v2(Bai et al., 2025), ∞\infty长凳Bench(Zhang et al., 2024b), 头盔, HELMET(Yen et al., 2025), 标尺, RULER(Hsieh et al., 2024)和诺利玛, and NoLiMa(Modarressi et al., 2025)。多模态评估以MMMU-Pro为代表. Multimodal evaluation is represented by MMMU-Pro(Yue et al., 2024a), 字符 Xiv, CharXiv(Wang et al., 2024e)，和视频MME, and Video-MME(Fu et al., 2024a)。这些基准通常将客观项目与基于法学硕士的评分标准和选择性人工审查相结合. These benchmarks commonly combine objective items with LLM-based rubric scores and selective human review(Zheng et al., 2023; Liu et al., 2023b)。它们的规模很有价值，但判断偏差可能会导致文体流畅性被误认为是实质性质量。. Their scale is valuable, but judge bias can cause stylistic fluency to be mistaken for substantive quality.

安全性和对抗鲁棒性。Safety and adversarial robustness.

HarmBench 标准化了自动红队并衡量有害的合规性和过度拒绝HarmBench standardizes automated red teaming and measures both harmful compliance and excessive refusal(Mazeika et al., 2024)。 JailbreakBench 提供可重复的攻击、防御和威胁模型协议. JailbreakBench supplies reproducible attack, defense, and threat-model protocols(Chao et al., 2024)。当培训课程适应时，固定的外部安全套件仍然很重要，因为自我生成的经验可能会低估罕见的伤害或放大不安全行为。. Fixed external safety suites remain important when the training curriculum adapts because self-generated experience can underrepresent rare harms or amplify unsafe behavior.

能力基准确定在指定评估者的指导下性能是否得到改善。他们无法自行确定培训期间使用的反馈是否忠实，或者经验分布是否仍然提供信息。一种方法可以通过校准不佳的内在奖励来提高 GSM8K 准确性，在生成逐渐缩小的任务时改进 HumanEval，或者通过利用法官偏好来获得竞技场胜利Capability benchmarks establish whether performance improved under a specified evaluator. They cannot by themselves determine whether the feedback used during training was faithful or whether the experience distribution remained informative. A method may raise GSM8K accuracy through a poorly calibrated intrinsic reward, improve HumanEval while generating progressively narrower tasks, or gain arena wins by exploiting judge preferences(Dubois et al., 2024; Liu et al., 2024e)。接下来的两小节直接检查这些缺失的证据形式。. The next two subsections examine these missing forms of evidence directly.

5.2. 反馈保真度5.2. Feedback Fidelity

反馈评估测试学习信号是否奖励其旨在促进的行为。这需要检查奖励机制本身，而不仅仅是最终政策的能力。相关对象可以是结果奖励模型、过程奖励模型、LLM 法官或模型导出的信号（例如确定性或共识）。Feedback evaluation tests whether the learning signal rewards the behavior it is intended to promote. This requires examining the reward mechanism itself, not only the capability of the resulting policy. The relevant object may be an outcome reward model, a process reward model, an LLM judge, or a model-derived signal such as certainty or consensus.

5.2.1. 结果奖励模型5.2.1. Outcome reward models

RewardBench 通过聊天、推理和安全性偏好三元组的成对准确性来评估结果奖励模型RewardBench evaluates outcome reward models through pairwise accuracy on preference triples spanning chat, reasoning, and safety(Lambert et al., 2024)。 RewardBench 2 添加了看不见的人工提示、四局两胜选择以及下游效用测试. RewardBench 2 adds unseen human prompts, best-of-four selection, and tests of downstream utility(Malik et al., 2025)。长格式 RewardBench 将评估范围扩展到需要文档级评估的响应，并测试对响应长度和错误位置的敏感性. Long-form RewardBench extends evaluation to responses that require document-level assessment and tests sensitivity to response length and error position(Huang et al., 2026c)。个性化 RewardBench 检查当响应具有相似的整体质量时奖励模型是否遵循用户特定的规则. Personalized RewardBench examines whether a reward model follows user-specific rubrics when responses have similar overall quality(Ma et al., 2026).

一些基准测试针对特定的错误源。 RM-Bench 以不同的样式重写语义上等效的响应，以暴露长度和格式的虚假偏好Several benchmarks target specific sources of error. RM-Bench rewrites semantically equivalent responses in different styles to expose spurious preferences for length and formatting(Liu et al., 2024e)。 PPE 测试奖励模型代理指标是否可以预测下游 RLHF 结果. PPE tests whether reward-model proxy metrics predict downstream RLHF outcomes(Frick et al., 2025)。 RewardMATH 研究数学奖励模型的鲁棒性和过度优化. RewardMATH studies robustness and overoptimization in mathematical reward models(Kim et al., 2024a)，VL-RewardBench 将奖励评估扩展到视觉语言反应, and VL-RewardBench extends reward evaluation to vision-language responses(Li et al., 2024c)。帮助转向2. HelpSteer2(Wang et al., 2024d)并奖励任何东西and RewardAnything(Yu et al., 2025e)支持多属性或原则条件评估。在这些设置中，核心问题是是否support multi-attribute or principle-conditioned evaluation. Across these settings, the central question is whetherℛϕ\mathcal{R}_{\phi}代表预期的标准或其方便的关联。represents the intended criterion or a convenient correlate of it.

5.2.2. 流程奖励模型5.2.2. Process reward models

过程奖励模型需要在分配信用的级别进行评估。工艺台Process reward models require evaluation at the level where credit is assigned. ProcessBench(Zheng et al., 2025a)和 PRMBenchand PRMBench(Song et al., 2025b)要求验证者识别数学轨迹中的错误步骤。 Socratic-PRMBench 引入了细粒度错误，并用 PRM-Score 总结了它们的检测ask verifiers to identify erroneous steps in mathematical trajectories. Socratic-PRMBench introduces fine-grained errors and summarizes their detection with PRM-Score(Li et al., 2025g)。 VILBENCH 将过程奖励评估扩展到视觉语言推理. VILBENCH extends process-reward evaluation to vision-language reasoning(Tu et al., 2025)。最终答案辨别和过程错误检测测量不同的属性，因此结果准确性必须伴随步骤级 F1 或 PRM-Score。. Final-answer discrimination and process-error detection measure different properties, so outcome accuracy must be accompanied by step-level F1 or PRM-Score.

5.2.3. LLM 法官和验证者实用程序5.2.3. LLM judges and verifier utility

可以测试生成法官与人的一致性、事实歧视以及推理或批评管道中的效用。 G-评估Generative judges can be tested for agreement with people, factual discrimination, and utility within an inference or critique pipeline. G-Eval(Liu et al., 2023b), 熊猫LM, PandaLM(Wang et al., 2023d), 法官LM, JudgeLM(Zhu et al., 2025a), 自动J, Auto-J(Li et al., 2023a), 普罗米修斯 2, Prometheus 2(Kim et al., 2024b), 火焰, FLAMe(Vu et al., 2024)和 BiGGen 工作台, and BiGGen Bench(Kim et al., 2025)涵盖评分标准、成对比较和细粒度技能评估。 JudgeBench 使用来自可验证来源的正确和错误答案来减少对风格线索的依赖cover rubric scoring, pairwise comparison, and fine-grained skill assessment. JudgeBench uses correct and incorrect responses drawn from verifiable sources to reduce reliance on stylistic cues(Tan et al., 2025)。 CodeJudgeBench 将相同的原则应用于执行检查的代码. CodeJudgeBench applies the same principle to execution-checked code(Jiang et al., 2025)，而 JETTS 则评估重排序、波束搜索和批评细化中的判断效用, while JETTS evaluates judge utility in reranking, beam search, and critique refinement(Zhou et al., 2025e).

其他套件探讨了跨模式和环境的判断可靠性。 MLLM 作为法官涵盖多模式输入Other suites probe judge reliability across modalities and contexts. MLLM-as-a-Judge covers multimodal inputs(Chen et al., 2024a)。 VerifyBench 将专业验证者与一般法学硕士评委在科学和数学方面的专家反应进行比较. VerifyBench compares specialized verifiers with general LLM judges on expert responses in science and mathematics(Li et al., 2026c)。 LongJudgeBench 在不同的评审协议下评估长篇输出. LongJudgeBench evaluates long-form outputs under different judging protocols(Chen et al., 2026a)，JudgeArena 支持基准、判断、提示和推理后端的受控变化, and JudgeArena supports controlled variation of the benchmark, judge, prompt, and inference backend(Lushtaku et al., 2026)。 LLMBar 将指令遵守与肤浅偏好区分开来. LLMBar distinguishes instruction adherence from superficial preference(Zeng et al., 2024)，而 CriticBench 将生成、批评和纠正作为管道进行评估, while CriticBench evaluates generation, critique, and correction as a pipeline(Lin et al., 2024b)。位置偏差测试通过重复和交换比较来量化顺序效应. Position-bias tests quantify order effects through repeated and swapped comparisons(Shi et al., 2024).

当法学硕士法官提供培训奖励时，重复使用同一法官家庭进行最终评估会形成循环测试。不相交的法官、坚持的人类子集或可验证的代理提供了更可信的改进措施。When an LLM judge supplies the training reward, reusing the same judge family for final evaluation creates a circular test. A disjoint judge, a held-out human subset, or a verifiable proxy provides a more credible measure of improvement.

5.2.4. 内在确定性和共识信号5.2.4. Intrinsic certainty and consensus signals

从不确定性、自我一致性或相对于参考的可能性获得的奖励需要与偏好准确性不同的诊断。可以根据可验证任务的正确性来校准信心。共识奖励可以通过样本大小、温度和分布变化进行测试。基于参考的信号需要对参考措辞的变化进行敏感性测试。在每种情况下，外部检查器都可以揭示优化是改善推理还是仅仅放大模型已经青睐的模式。Rewards derived from uncertainty, self-consistency, or likelihood relative to a reference require diagnostics that differ from preference accuracy. Confidence can be calibrated against correctness on verifiable tasks. Consensus rewards can be tested across sample sizes, temperatures, and distribution shifts. Reference-based signals require sensitivity tests for changes in reference wording. In every case, an external checker can reveal whether optimization improves reasoning or merely amplifies patterns already favored by the model.

反馈审核必须与培训机制相匹配。奖励模式政策需要对冻结的资金进行评估The feedback audit must match the training mechanism. Reward-model policies require evaluation of the frozenℛϕ\mathcal{R}_{\phi}通过 RewardBench 式测试或下游效用措施through RewardBench-style tests or downstream utility measures(Lambert et al., 2024; Frick et al., 2025)。受过法官培训的政策需要直接评估. Judge-trained policies require direct evaluation ofπv\pi_{v}以及独立评估员的二次评分and a secondary score from an independent evaluator(Tan et al., 2025; Jiang et al., 2025)。过程监督策略需要步骤错误检测和最终结果测量. Process-supervised policies require both step-error detection and final-outcome measures(Zheng et al., 2025a; Song et al., 2025b)。经过内在奖励训练的政策需要校准和外部检查员审计。将审计与奖励来源相匹配将政策改进与产生政策的信号的可靠性分开。. Policies trained with intrinsic rewards require calibration and external-checker audits. Matching the audit to the reward source separates policy improvement from the reliability of the signal that produced it.

5.3. 体验品质5.3. Experience Quality

经验评估询问提供给学习的任务和环境是否仍然有效、多样化和信息丰富。任务分配的时候就会出现这个问题Experience evaluation asks whether the tasks and environments supplied to learning remain valid, diverse, and informative. This issue arises when the task distributionΠ\Pi或环境动态or environment dynamics(𝒮,𝒫)(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{P})在训练期间生成或调整。不断增加的内部奖励可能与缩小的课程范围、不准确的环境或共同适应政策的验证者共存。因此，评估必须检查经验流以及经过培训的政策。are generated or adapted during training. A rising internal reward can coexist with a narrowing curriculum, an inaccurate environment, or a verifier that co-adapts to the policy. Evaluation must consequently examine the experience stream as well as the trained policy.

5.3.1. 代理和交互环境5.3.1. Agentic and interactive environments

网络和计算机使用基准为长期代理提供了外部指定的成功标准。网络竞技场Web and computer-use benchmarks provide externally specified success criteria for long-horizon agents. WebArena(Zhou et al., 2024), 视觉网络竞技场, VisualWebArena(Koh et al., 2024), 工作竞技场, WorkArena(Drouin et al., 2024), 网络航行者, WebVoyager(He et al., 2024b), 助理替补, AssistantBench(Yoran et al., 2024), 浏览比较, BrowseComp(Wei et al., 2025a)和浏览器健身房, and BrowserGym(Chezelles et al., 2025)涵盖导航、企业知识工作、多模式浏览和信息查找。操作系统世界cover navigation, enterprise knowledge work, multimodal browsing, and information seeking. OSWorld(Xie et al., 2024b), Windows 代理竞技场, Windows Agent Arena(Bonatti et al., 2025), 安卓世界, AndroidWorld(Rawles et al., 2025)，和应用程序世界, and AppWorld(Trivedi et al., 2024)将评估扩展到桌面、移动和多应用程序工作流程。extend evaluation to desktop, mobile, and multi-application workflows.

工具使用套件包括 ToolLLM 和 ToolBenchTool-use suites include ToolLLM and ToolBench(Qin et al., 2024), 稳定工具台, StableToolBench(Guo et al., 2024)，以及 Berkeley 函数调用排行榜, and the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard(Patil et al., 2025)。代理工作台. AgentBench(Liu et al., 2024d), 盖亚, GAIA(Mialon et al., 2024), τ\tau-长凳-bench(Yao et al., 2024), 代理公司, TheAgentCompany(Xu et al., 2025a), 科学代理平台, ScienceAgentBench(Chen et al., 2024e)，和巴尔罗格, and BALROG(Paglieri et al., 2024)扩大一般、科学、工作场所和游戏环境的覆盖范围。 AgentDojo 通过动态工具环境中的提示注入攻击和防御添加安全评估broaden coverage across general, scientific, workplace, and game settings. AgentDojo adds security evaluation through prompt-injection attacks and defenses in dynamic tool environments(Debenedetti et al., 2024).

相同的套件可以评估政策能力和环境质量。统一的框架可以改变代理支架和环境波动性，以将政策绩效与执行条件分开The same suites can evaluate both policy capability and environment quality. A unified framework can vary agent scaffolds and environmental volatility to separate policy performance from execution conditions(Zhu et al., 2026a)。培训台测试工作场所事件的安排、信息收集和经验重用. Trainee-Bench tests scheduling, information gathering, and reuse of experience across workplace episodes(Fu et al., 2026)。 AgentWorldBench 评估语言世界模型是否再现了在已建立的代理基准中观察到的状态转换. AgentWorldBench evaluates whether a language world model reproduces state transitions observed in established agent benchmarks(Zuo et al., 2026)。对于综合环境，相关措施包括验证通过率、转换再现性、成功标准正确性、冻结策略下的解决率以及转移到公共代理套件。这些措施直接适用于第 2 节中讨论的环境生成方法。. For synthesized environments, relevant measures include validation pass rate, transition reproducibility, success-criterion correctness, solve rate under a frozen policy, and transfer to a public agent suite. These measures apply directly to the environment-generation methods discussed in Sec.4.2.2.

5.3.2. 自创课程下的任务质量5.3.2. Task quality under self-generated curricula

当学习过程中生成任务分配时，求解器的准确性必须附有有关所提议课程的证据。让When the task distribution is generated during learning, solver accuracy must be accompanied by evidence about the proposed curriculum. Letp¯θS​(𝒒)\bar{p}_{\theta_{S}}(\bm{q})表示查询的经验解决率denote the empirical solve rate of query𝒒\bm{q}。可学习性得分. The learnability scorep¯​(1−p¯)\bar{p}(1-\bar{p})在等式中in Eq.18在中间求解率时最大，并在每个采样解成功或失败时消失。它识别当前求解器学习前沿附近的任务，尽管它不衡量新颖性或语义多样性。is largest at intermediate solve rates and vanishes when every sampled solution succeeds or fails. It identifies tasks near the current solver’s learning frontier, although it does not measure novelty or semantic diversity.

补充统计数据包括解决率分布、嵌入或Complementary statistics include the solve-rate distribution, embedding ornn-克多样性、相对于种子集的新颖性以及无效、有毒或接近重复提案的拒绝率。基于语料库的提议者可以将文档级归因作为外部新颖性检查-gram diversity, novelty relative to the seed set, and rejection rates for invalid, toxic, or near-duplicate proposals. Corpus-grounded proposers enable document-level attribution as an external novelty check(Liu et al., 2025b; Kwan et al., 2026)。反复关注一小部分源子集标志着课程的循环利用。跟踪引导和指令演化管道也需要保留率和过滤标准，因为这些选择决定了最终的任务分配. Repeated concentration on a small source subset signals curriculum recycling. Trace-bootstrapping and instruction-evolution pipelines also need retention rates and filtering criteria because these choices shape the final task distribution(Zelikman et al., 2022; Wang et al., 2022; Xu et al., 2023; Fang et al., 2025b).

能力排行榜通常会隐藏经验流中的失败。在群体相关优化下，琐碎或不可能的任务会产生微弱的学习信号。生成器可以重复采样策略已经掌握的技能Capability leaderboards often conceal failures in the experience stream. Trivial or impossible tasks produce weak learning signals under group-relative optimization. A generator may repeatedly sample skills the policy has already mastered(Fang et al., 2025b; Kuba et al., 2025)。在交互式设置中，幻觉奖励、不稳定的测试和未指定的工具可能会破坏生成环境中收集的每个轨迹. In interactive settings, hallucinated rewards, flaky tests, and underspecified tools can corrupt every trajectory collected in a generated environment(Yang et al., 2025b; Shi et al., 2026)。需要课程和环境指标来区分真正的经验扩展和这些形式的崩溃。. Curriculum and environment metrics are needed to distinguish genuine expansion of experience from these forms of collapse.

5.3.3. 闭环评估5.3.3. Closed-loop evaluation

从超越人类设计的经验到自主共同进化的转变需要最强大的评估协议。当政策、奖励、任务或环境一起适应时，内部进展可能会与外部衡量的能力产生偏差。相关风险包括分布缩小、对手或竞技场不稳定The transition from experience beyond human design to autonomous co-evolution requires the strongest evaluation protocol. When policies, rewards, tasks, or environments adapt together, internal progress can diverge from externally measured capability. Relevant risks include distributional narrowing, non-stationary opponents or arenas(Liu et al., 2025a; Guertler et al., 2025; Dong et al., 2026)，以及生成和验证之间的不对称, and asymmetry between generation and verification(Chen et al., 2026b)。可信的闭环评估需要冻结的外部测试、课程多样性的轨迹、培训和保留验证者之间的协议，以及冻结循环各个组成部分的消融。. A credible closed-loop evaluation needs frozen external tests, trajectories of curriculum diversity, agreement between training and held-out verifiers, and ablations that freeze individual components of the loop.

PAST-Bench 比较有或没有保留经验的匹配任务序列，以确定后续收益是否遵循预期的保存、检索和更新路径PAST-Bench compares matched task sequences with and without retained experience to determine whether later gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway(Xue et al., 2026)。 AI4AI-Bench 测试代理是否可以改进冷冻研究存储库内的学习算法. AI4AI-Bench tests whether agents can improve learning algorithms inside frozen research repositories(Chi et al., 2026)。修改后的程序从头开始重新运行，并由固定评估员评分。该协议测试递归改进的具体组件，而不将该组件的成功视为完全自主循环的证据。. The modified procedures are rerun from scratch and scored by a fixed evaluator. This protocol tests a concrete component of recursive improvement without treating success on that component as evidence of a fully autonomous loop.

公共代理套件和合成环境发挥着互补作用。如果生成世界中的成功不转移到 WebArena 或 OSWorld 等环境中，则可以反映生成器特定的规律Public agent suites and synthetic environments play complementary roles. Success in generated worlds can reflect generator-specific regularities if it does not transfer to environments such as WebArena or OSWorld(Zhou et al., 2024; Xie et al., 2024b)。当生成的训练环境的有效性未知时，公共套件的收益也是不完整的证据。可信评估将合成环境中的学习曲线与同一操作界面下转移到冻结的公共环境相结合。. Public-suite gains are also incomplete evidence when the validity of the generated training environments is unknown. Credible evaluation combines learning curves in synthetic environments with transfer to frozen public environments under the same action interface.

5.4. 数据集和基准套件5.4. Datasets and Benchmark Suites

数据集的作用取决于它如何进入学习和评估管道。可验证的集合可以作为培训经验、保留的能力测试或构建评估器的来源。必须明确说明这些角色，因为跨它们的重用可能会使评估的明显独立性失效。The role of a dataset depends on how it enters the learning and evaluation pipeline. A verifiable collection can serve as training experience, a held-out capability test, or a source for constructing an evaluator. These roles must be stated explicitly because reuse across them can invalidate the apparent independence of evaluation.

可验证的培训和评估集。Verifiable training and evaluation sets.

GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval、MBPP 和竞赛代码语料库对于培训很有吸引力，因为它们的检查器便宜且客观。它们的广泛使用也造成了饱和和污染风险GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, MBPP, and competition-code corpora are attractive for training because their checkers are inexpensive and objective. Their broad use also creates saturation and contamination risks(Singh et al., 2024; Xu et al., 2024)。现场工作台. LiveBench(White et al., 2025), LiveCodeBench (Jain et al., 2025), LiveMathBench (Liu et al., 2024b), 迪瓦尔, DyVal(Zhu et al., 2024), 防漏台, AntiLeakBench(Wu et al., 2024), VarBench (Qian et al., 2024), 和 MMLU-CF, and MMLU-CF(Zhao et al., 2024)通过新数据、受控扰动或封闭测试构建来降低特定泄漏风险。它们并不能消除协议偏差，因此静态和更新的套件仍然可以作为补充视图。reduce particular leakage risks through fresh data, controlled perturbations, or closed-test construction. They do not eliminate protocol bias, so static and refreshed suites remain useful as complementary views.

偏好和评估者发展集。Preference and evaluator-development sets.

结果和过程奖励模型是根据偏好比较、步骤标签和标题注释开发的。 RewardBench 风格的数据Outcome and process reward models are developed from preference comparisons, step labels, and rubric annotations. RewardBench-style data(Lambert et al., 2024; Malik et al., 2025), 帮助转向2, HelpSteer2(Wang et al., 2024d), 工艺台, ProcessBench(Zheng et al., 2025a), PRMBench (Song et al., 2025b)、苏格拉底-PRMBench, and Socratic-PRMBench(Li et al., 2025g)体现不同的判断标准。对这些分布之一进行训练的评估者需要进行不相交测试。否则，基准测试会衡量对其注释方案的熟悉程度以及一般评估者的保真度。instantiate different standards of judgment. An evaluator trained on one of these distributions requires a disjoint test. Otherwise, the benchmark measures familiarity with its annotation scheme as much as general evaluator fidelity.

交互日志和环境。Interaction logs and environments.

WebArena 风格的环境WebArena-style environments(Zhou et al., 2024; Xie et al., 2024b; Chezelles et al., 2025)和工具使用语料库and tool-use corpora(Qin et al., 2024; Patil et al., 2025)为长期评估提供轨迹。经验生成方法还需要合成环境的冻结分割，以及生成器提示和验证脚本supply trajectories for long-horizon evaluation. Experience-generating methods additionally need a frozen split of synthesized environments, together with generator prompts and validation scripts(Mei et al., 2026; Zhu et al., 2026a)。这些工件使环境保真度声明变得可审计。. These artifacts make environment-fidelity claims auditable.
