表Table6将这些数据集角色映射到阶梯。本质区别不在于数据集是否最初由人生成。而是相同的数据、评估者或环境是否参与学习和最终评估。随着越来越多的组件进入学习循环，冻结的外部套件和体验质量度量变得越来越重要。maps these dataset roles to the ladder. The essential distinction is not whether a dataset was originally produced by a person. It is whether the same data, evaluator, or environment participates in both learning and final assessment. As more components enter the learning loop, frozen external suites and experience-quality measures become increasingly important.

采样、解码和可比性。Sampling, decoding, and comparability.

同一数据集的结果可能因温度、样本数量而异Results on the same dataset can vary with temperature, sample countGG、代币预算、工具访问以及推理轨迹的要求。报告需要完整的解码配置文件，包括温度、顶部, token budget, tool access, and requirements on the reasoning trace. Reports need a complete decoding profile that includes temperature, top-pp、最大令牌、停止规则和随机种子。当抽样是方法的一部分时，贪婪和抽样结果回答不同的问题并需要单独报告。代理评估还需要行动预算、观察格式、可用工具、重置程序和环境版本, maximum tokens, stopping rules, and random seeds. When sampling is part of the method, greedy and sampled results answer different questions and require separate reporting. Agent evaluations also require the action budget, observation format, available tools, reset procedure, and environment version(Zhou et al., 2024; Xie et al., 2024b; Chezelles et al., 2025)。如果没有这些控制，协议差异可能会主导学习信号的影响。. Without these controls, protocol differences can dominate the effect attributed to a learning signal.

跨域传输。Cross-domain transfer.

Transfer 为在狭窄的可验证领域训练的方法提供了有用的压力测试。完整的报告包括训练领域的表现、目标领域的表现以及它们之间的差距。领域内收益大而转移少的现象与多种解释是一致的，包括过度拟合、奖励利用和课程缩小。尽管仍需要额外的分析来确定原因，但科学 QA、工具使用或开放式交互方面的改进提供了更强有力的泛化证据Transfer provides a useful stress test for methods trained on a narrow verifiable domain. A complete report includes performance in the training domain, performance in the target domain, and the gap between them. Large in-domain gains with little transfer are compatible with several explanations, including overfitting, reward exploitation, and curriculum narrowing. Improvements across science QA, tool use, or open-ended interaction provide stronger evidence of generalization, although additional analysis is still needed to identify the cause(Wei et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2023b; Mialon et al., 2024).

5.5. 指标、协议和再现性5.5. Metrics, Protocols, and Reproducibility

结果和稳定性指标。Outcome and stability metrics.

精确匹配、数值等价和单元测试 Pass@Exact match, numerical equivalence, and unit-test Pass@kk仍然是可验证域的主要指标。让remain the primary metrics for verifiable domains. LetGG是每个查询的样本数，be the number of samples for each query andcc正确样本的数量。公正的通行证@the number of correct samples. The unbiased Pass@kk估计量是estimator is

偏好和判断指标。Preference and judge metrics.

成对准确性衡量结果奖励模型和偏好标签之间的一致性。给定三元组Pairwise accuracy measures agreement between an outcome reward model and preference labels. Given triples(𝒒,𝒚c,𝒚r)(\bm{q},\bm{y}_{c},\bm{y}_{r}),

Accuracy=1|𝒟|∑(𝒒,𝒚c,𝒚r)∈𝒟𝕀[ℛϕ(𝒒,𝒚c)>ℛϕ(𝒒,𝒚r)],\text{Accuracy}=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}|}\sum_{(\bm{q},\bm{y}_{c},\bm{y}_{r})\in\mathcal{D}}\mathbb{I}\!\left[\mathcal{R}_{\phi}(\bm{q},\bm{y}_{c})>\mathcal{R}_{\phi}(\bm{q},\bm{y}_{r})\right], (23)

RewardBench 2 将此协议扩展为四局最佳选择，而 PPE 通过与下游 RLHF 结果的关系来评估指标RewardBench 2 extends this protocol to best-of-four selection, while PPE assesses metrics through their relationship with downstream RLHF outcomes(Malik et al., 2025; Frick et al., 2025)。开放式评估通常会报告胜率、评分标准分数和结构化 G-Eval 分数. Open-ended evaluation commonly reports win rates, rubric scores, and structured G-Eval scores(Liu et al., 2023b)。过程保真度通过步骤级 F1 或 PRM-Score 进行测量. Process fidelity is measured with step-level F1 or PRM-Score(Li et al., 2025g; Song et al., 2025b)。每个基于裁判的结果还需要对位置、长度和风格偏差进行诊断. Every judge-based result also needs a diagnostic for position, length, and style bias(Shi et al., 2024; Liu et al., 2024e; Dubois et al., 2024).

体验质量指标。Experience-quality metrics.

自生成的任务流需要解决率分布，意味着可学习性Self-generated task streams require solve-rate distributions, mean learnability𝔼​[p¯​(1−p¯)]\mathbb{E}[\bar{p}(1-\bar{p})]、多样性、新颖性和过滤统计。综合环境需要可执行性、验证通过率、转换保真度以及转移到公共代理基准。闭环需要内部奖励和外部保留能力的轨迹。这两个信号之间的持续分歧警告了协同适应性奖励剥削或崩溃，尽管它本身并不能确定单一原因。, diversity, novelty, and filtering statistics. Synthesized environments require executability, validation pass rate, transition fidelity, and transfer to public agent benchmarks. Closed loops require trajectories of both internal reward and external held-out capability. Persistent divergence between the two signals warns of co-adaptive reward exploitation or collapse, although it does not identify a single cause on its own.

污染、偏差和工具。Contamination, bias, and tooling.

康塔姆ConTAM(Singh et al., 2024), 康斯塔, ConStat(Dekoninck et al., 2024)，以及相关分析, and related analyses(Xu et al., 2024; Fu et al., 2024b)说明静态套件的泄漏漏洞。更新且程序多样的基准降低了这种风险illustrate the vulnerability of static suites to leakage. Refreshed and procedurally varied benchmarks reduce this risk(White et al., 2025; Zhu et al., 2024; Wu et al., 2024; Qian et al., 2024; Zhao et al., 2024)。基于法学硕士的评估者还需要对职位和风格偏见进行明确的审核. LLM-based evaluators also require explicit audits for position and style bias(Shi et al., 2024; Liu et al., 2024e).

社区工具包括 lm-evaluation-harness、Community tools including lm-evaluation-harness,11 1 https://github.com/EleutherAI/lm-evaluation-harness光评估，LightEval,22 2 https://github.com/huggingface/lighteval零评估，ZeroEval,33 3 https://github.com/WildEval/ZeroEval数学评估线束，Math-evaluation-harness,44 4 https://github.com/ZubinGou/math-evaluation-harness和 BigCode-evaluation-harnessand BigCode-evaluation-harness55 5 https://github.com/bigcode-project/bigcode-evaluation-harness标准化提示、解码和聚合。可重现的报告必须识别解码种子、判断提示、环境设置和准确的线束修订，因为这些选择可能会极大地改变比较。standardize prompting, decoding, and aggregation. Reproducible reports must identify decoding seeds, judge prompts, environment settings, and exact harness revisions because these choices can materially change the comparison.

推荐的最低记分卡。Recommended minimum scorecard.

我们的论文建议在培训领域和至少一种转移环境中进行冻结能力测试，以及与反馈源相匹配的审核。产生经验的方法还需要直接测量任务或环境质量。基于大量重复使用的基准的声明需要进行污染分析，并且所有比较都需要在共享协议下计算匹配的基线。接近 L4 的方法还需要对内部奖励和冻结的外部评估进行纵向比较。表Our paper recommends frozen capability tests in the training domain and at least one transfer setting, together with an audit matched to the feedback source. Methods that generate experience also need direct measurements of task or environment quality. Claims based on heavily reused benchmarks require a contamination analysis, and all comparisons need compute-matched baselines under a shared protocol. Methods approaching L4 additionally require a longitudinal comparison between internal reward and frozen external evaluation. Table6按阶梯级别提出相同的要求。presents the same requirements by ladder level.

5.6. 局限性和开放的评估差距5.6. Limitations and Open Evaluation Gaps

目前的评估实践对于政策能力的评估仍然比对于反馈保真度或体验质量的评估要强得多。公共排行榜很少测试培训评委是否同意独立评估员的观点。课程多样性、环境保真度和闭环稳定性的指标也分散在各个方法中。这些弱点使得内部奖励有可能上升，而一般能力的证据却保持不变Current evaluation practice remains much stronger for policy capability than for feedback fidelity or experience quality. Public leaderboards rarely test whether a training judge agrees with an independent evaluator. Metrics for curriculum diversity, environment fidelity, and closed-loop stability are also fragmented across individual methods. These weaknesses make it possible for internal reward to rise while the evidence for general capability remains unchanged(Qi et al., 2026b; Chen et al., 2026b).

几个实际差距使这个问题变得更加复杂。在一小部分基准之外，污染控制和动态评估的采用并不均衡Several practical gaps compound this problem. Contamination controls and dynamic evaluation are unevenly adopted outside a small set of benchmarks(White et al., 2025; Zhu et al., 2024; Singh et al., 2024)。内在奖励方法和基于验证者的 RLVR 很少在匹配的计算、数据访问和解码预算下进行比较。代理基准测试提供了真实的交互，但仍然昂贵且部分不可复制，因为实时网站和图形界面会随着时间的推移而变化. Intrinsic-reward methods and verifier-based RLVR are seldom compared under matched compute, data access, and decoding budgets. Agent benchmarks offer realistic interaction but remain expensive and partly non-reproducible because live websites and graphical interfaces change over time(Zhou et al., 2024; Chezelles et al., 2025; Xie et al., 2024b)。尽管越来越多地使用多模式、使用工具的代理，但多模式和长上下文评估与监督减少的学习工作的联系也很弱. Multimodal and long-context evaluation is also only weakly connected to work on learning with reduced supervision, despite the growing use of multimodal, tool-using agents(Chen et al., 2024a; Yen et al., 2025; Hsieh et al., 2024).

由三部分组成的评估框架为这些差距提供了实际的回应。能力是在冻结的可验证的、专家的、开放式的或代理的套件上衡量的。使用与训练方法相匹配的奖励模型、过程模型、判断或内在信号审核来测试反馈。经验通过课程质量、环境有效性和闭环稳定性进行评估。更新的套件可以减少能力测试中的污染The three-part evaluation framework provides a practical response to these gaps. Capability is measured on frozen verifiable, expert, open-ended, or agentic suites. Feedback is tested with reward-model, process-model, judge, or intrinsic-signal audits matched to the training method. Experience is assessed through curriculum quality, environment validity, and closed-loop stability. Refreshed suites can reduce contamination in capability tests(White et al., 2025; Zhu et al., 2024; Jain et al., 2025)，而专家检查和冻结的评审小组则为反馈和经验提供了稀疏的外部锚点。, while expert checks and frozen judge panels provide sparse external anchors for feedback and experience.

没有任何一个排行榜可以确定自我维持学习的进展。声明必须确定哪些组件经过外部评估以及哪些组件保留在培训循环内。缺少证据并不会使方法无效，但它限制了从报告的成果中得出的结论。No single leaderboard can establish progress toward self-sustaining learning. Claims must identify which components were evaluated externally and which remained inside the training loop. Missing evidence does not invalidate a method, but it limits the conclusion that can be drawn from its reported gains.

6. 挑战和未来方向6. Challenges and Future Directions

前面几节讨论的方法扩展了大型推理模型可用的奖励和经验的来源。可重复使用的评估器减少了重复人类判断的需要，而自我生成的任务和环境允许训练在固定语料库之外继续进行。然而，同样的机制削弱了一些外部约束，使传统培训易于审核。代理剥削、课程崩溃、生成器与验证者串通以及培训和评估之间的重叠都可能隐藏在不断上升的内部奖励背后。因此，核心挑战是在减少直接干预的情况下维持学习，同时保留独立于学习循环的证据。The methods discussed in the preceding sections expand the sources of reward and experience available to large reasoning models. Reusable evaluators reduce the need for repeated human judgment, while self-generated tasks and environments allow training to continue beyond a fixed corpus. Yet the same mechanisms weaken some of the external constraints that make conventional training easy to audit. Proxy exploitation, curriculum collapse, generator-verifier collusion, and overlap between training and evaluation can all remain hidden behind rising internal reward. Accordingly, the central challenge is to sustain learning with less direct intervention while preserving evidence that remains independent of the learning loop.

6.1. 稳定奖励超出每个实例的人类评估6.1. Stabilizing Rewards beyond Per-Instance Human Evaluation

部分Section3揭示了奖励的可扩展性与其证据的独立性之间的权衡。熵、自我确定性、共识和自我判断可以以较低的边际成本获得大量的推广。然而，长时间的优化可能会加剧策略分布，加剧多数错误，并使信心与正确性脱钩。执行、游戏和形式验证提供了更强大的外部基础，但不完整的测试和可利用的规则仍然为意外行为留下了空间reveals a tradeoff between the scalability of a reward and the independence of its evidence. Entropy, self-certainty, consensus, and self-judgment can score large numbers of rollouts at low marginal cost. However, prolonged optimization can sharpen the policy distribution, reinforce majority errors, and decouple confidence from correctness. Execution, games, and formal verification provide stronger external grounding, but incomplete tests and exploitable rules still leave room for unintended behavior(Zhang et al., 2025k; Shafayat et al., 2025; He et al., 2026; Huang et al., 2025c; Zhao et al., 2025c; Wang et al., 2026d)。因此，沿着奖励轴的进展取决于保留独立证据，而不重新创建密集的每个实例监督。. Progress along the reward axis consequently depends on preserving independent evidence without recreating dense per-instance supervision.

内在奖励和共识奖励的独立锚点。Independent anchors for intrinsic and consensus rewards.

多数投票和置信最大化避免了奖励时的外部标签，但这两个信号都无法检测到策略自身样本共享的错误Majority voting and confidence maximization avoid external labels at reward time, but neither signal can detect an error shared by the policy’s own samples(Shafayat et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025k)。稀疏的可执行检查、来自独立模型系列的冻结评估器、基于语料库的一致性测试以及定期专家审核可以提供补充证据。这些锚点需要保持在优化循环之外，以便它们检测内部奖励和保留能力之间的差异。他们的目的不是恢复密集监管。相反，他们建立了一个参考，可以根据该参考来校准和监控模型衍生的奖励。. Sparse executable checks, frozen evaluators from an independent model family, corpus-grounded consistency tests, and periodic expert audits can provide complementary evidence. These anchors need to remain outside the optimization loop so that they detect divergence between internal reward and held-out capability. Their purpose is not to restore dense supervision. Instead, they establish a reference against which model-derived rewards can be calibrated and monitored.

验证器在优化压力下的稳健性。Verifier robustness under optimization pressure.

学习验证器和生成验证器涵盖了脆性字符串匹配无法评估的输出Learned and generative verifiers cover outputs that brittle string matching cannot assess(Chen et al., 2025a; Xu et al., 2025c; Zhang et al., 2025d)。这种灵活性创造了更大的攻击面，因为在策略优化下误报可能会变得非常有价值. This flexibility creates a larger attack surface because false positives can become highly rewarding under policy optimization(Zhao et al., 2025c; Huang et al., 2025c; Helff et al., 2026; Wang et al., 2026c)。因此，稳健性必须在采用优化策略后进行测试，而不仅仅是静态基准测试。对抗性评估、针对更严格检查者的差异测试以及不对称目标可以暴露或阻止生成者和验证者之间的勾结. Robustness therefore has to be tested after exposure to an optimizing policy, not only on a static benchmark. Adversarial evaluation, differential testing against stricter checkers, and asymmetric objectives can expose or discourage collusion between generators and verifiers(Zha et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2025k)。集成通过减少对单个评估者的依赖来提供额外的防御. Ensembles provide an additional defense by reducing dependence on a single evaluator(Verga et al., 2024; Coste et al., 2023)。过程级奖励提供了超出最终答案匹配的证据，尽管它们的中间判断需要相同的对抗性审查. Process-level rewards offer evidence beyond final-answer matching, although their intermediate judgments require the same adversarial scrutiny(Cui et al., 2025a; She et al., 2025b)。一个悬而未决的问题是，当 RewardBench 式探测的成功并不能保证新优化输出的稳健性时，如何证明评估者的保真度能够经受住策略优化的考验. An open problem is how to certify that evaluator fidelity survives policy optimization when success on RewardBench-style probes does not guarantee robustness to newly optimized outputs(Lambert et al., 2024; Malik et al., 2025).

半可验证和开放式任务。Semi-verifiable and open-ended tasks.

可扩展奖励的证据仍然集中在数学和代码中，因为这些领域支持廉价的结果检查。科学推理、长篇事实、多模式交互和偏好敏感生成允许多种有效响应和更多依赖于上下文的标准。将强化学习扩展到这些设置需要来自标题、声明级验证、检索和校准法官的结构化证据Evidence for scalable reward remains concentrated in mathematics and code because these domains support inexpensive outcome checks. Scientific reasoning, long-form factuality, multimodal interaction, and preference-sensitive generation admit multiple valid responses and more context-dependent criteria. Extending RL to these settings requires structured evidence from rubrics, claim-level verification, retrieval, and calibrated judges(Viswanathan et al., 2025; Gunjal et al., 2025; Chen et al., 2025f; Fein et al., 2025)。这些评估者必须认识到实质性的差异，而不会将质量简化为风格或冗长。混合设计通过将可验证的子问题发送给确定性检查器并将开放式组件发送给学习评估器，提供了一种实用的中间方法. Such evaluators must recognize substantive variation without collapsing quality into style or verbosity. Hybrid designs provide a practical intermediate approach by sending verifiable subproblems to deterministic checkers and open-ended components to learned evaluators(Seed et al., 2025)。未解决的问题是哪些标准可以安全地委托，哪些仍然需要独立的外部标准。. The unresolved question is which criteria can safely be delegated and which still require an independent external standard.

6.2. 在不导致课程崩溃的情况下扩展体验6.2. Scaling Experience without Curriculum Collapse

随着任务和环境生成进入学习循环，主要困难从收集更多轨迹转变为保留其教育价值。提议者可以生成同样简单、实际上不可能的任务，或者仅限于求解器已知的模式的任务。合成环境也可以在内部保持一致，但无法代表外部世界。难度错误校准、模式崩溃、非平稳性和自适应奖励开发已经出现在提议者-求解器系统和生成的环境中As task and environment generation enter the learning loop, the main difficulty shifts from collecting more trajectories to preserving their educational value. A proposer can generate tasks that are uniformly easy, effectively impossible, or confined to patterns the solver already knows. A synthesized environment can also be internally consistent while failing to represent the external world. Difficulty miscalibration, mode collapse, non-stationarity, and co-adaptive reward exploitation already appear in proposer-solver systems and generated environments(Huang et al., 2025a; Zhao et al., 2025a; Fang et al., 2025b; Qi et al., 2026b).

随着时间的推移，可学习性和多样性。Learnability and diversity over time.

前沿目标提议器和基于遗憾的环境设计寻求接近求解器当前学习边界的任务Frontier-targeting proposers and regret-based environment design seek tasks near the solver’s current learning boundary(Dennis et al., 2020; Huang et al., 2025a; Zhao et al., 2025a)。早期解决率估计. Early solve-rate estimatesp¯\bar{p}然而，噪音很大，并且经过良好校准的难度分布仍然可能缺乏语义多样性。因此，有用的课程需要对可学习性和覆盖范围进行纵向测量。基于语料库和基于交互的提议者可以通过利用外部内容来减少模板回收are noisy, however, and a well-calibrated difficulty distribution can still lack semantic diversity. A useful curriculum therefore requires longitudinal measurements of both learnability and coverage. Corpus-grounded and interaction-grounded proposers can reduce template recycling by drawing on external content(Liu et al., 2025b; Kwan et al., 2025; Lu et al., 2025b)。未解决和未分配任务的档案提供了一种补充机制，因为它们为以后的策略保留了困难的经验，而不是在当前求解器失败时将其丢弃。. Archives of unsolved and out-of-distribution tasks provide a complementary mechanism because they preserve difficult experience for later policies instead of discarding it when the current solver fails.

环境保真度和可执行性接地。Environment fidelity and executable grounding.

生成的模拟器、单元测试和工具堆栈继承了构建它们的模型的错误Generated simulators, unit tests, and tool stacks inherit errors from the models that construct them(Yang et al., 2025b; Sun et al., 2025d; Zhou et al., 2025c)。因此，环境生成必须与验证相结合。分阶段的可执行性检查可以在训练之前拒绝畸形组件，而针对 SWE-bench、OSWorld 和 WebArena 等公共工具的差异测试可以揭示任务结果的差异. Environment generation must consequently be paired with validation. Staged executability checks can reject malformed components before training, while differential testing against public harnesses such as SWE-bench, OSWorld, and WebArena can reveal discrepancies in task outcomes(Jimenez et al., 2024; Xie et al., 2024b; Zhou et al., 2024)。从合成训练环境转移到真实交互比单独在生成的环境中取得成功提供了更强的保真度测试。如果没有这些控制，学习循环可能会针对不支持其自身模拟器之外的功能的人工动力学进行优化。. Transfer from synthetic training environments to held-out real interactions provides a stronger test of fidelity than success inside the generated environment alone. Without these controls, a learning loop may optimize against artificial dynamics that do not support capability outside its own simulator.

稳定的闭环。Stable closed loops.

CURE、自我挑战代理、螺旋和达尔文哥德尔机耦合了生成器、验证者、对手和代理修改的不同组合CURE, Self-Challenging agents, SPIRAL, and the Darwin Gödel Machine couple different combinations of generators, verifiers, opponents, and agent modification(Wang et al., 2025k; Zhou et al., 2025c; Liu et al., 2025a; Zhang et al., 2025b)。目前还没有一个能够关闭整个学习循环，因为固定的运行时间、游戏规则、任务源或评估目标继续提供外部结构。更完整的共同进化将需要优化和诊断来考虑不断变化的对手和评估者。角色条件基线可以减少不对称自我游戏的方差. None yet closes the entire learning loop because fixed runtimes, game rules, task sources, or evaluation objectives continue to supply external structure. More complete co-evolution will require optimization and diagnostics that account for changing opponents and evaluators. Role-conditioned baselines can reduce variance in asymmetric self-play(Liu et al., 2025a)。冻结的能力测试可以揭示与内部奖励的差异，而显式验证者训练可以解决评估随着生成而自动改进的假设. Frozen capability tests can reveal divergence from internal reward, while explicit verifier training can address the assumption that evaluation improves automatically with generation(Chen et al., 2026b)。当内部奖励上升但外部转移下降时，也需要停止标准。现有的闭环实验表明，初始策略有所改进，随后出现低于预言机监督训练的平台期. Stopping criteria are also needed when internal reward rises but external transfer declines. Existing closed-loop experiments show improvement over the initial policy followed by a plateau below oracle-supervised training(Qi et al., 2026b)。在不允许循环验证其自身错误的情况下扩展此限制仍然是自主协同进化的一个决定性挑战。. Extending this limit without allowing the loop to validate its own errors remains a defining challenge for autonomous co-evolution.

6.3. 体验采样的效率和代币效用6.3. Efficiency of Experience Sampling and Token Utility

降低注释成本会增加推理和交互成本。基于组的 RL 对每个查询进行多次部署采样，共识奖励需要重复生成，而自我生成的经验会添加提议者、验证者或环境构建过程。即使它们的代币不影响最终奖励，长推理轨迹也会进一步消耗计算量。根据方程。Reducing annotation cost can increase inference and interaction cost. Group-based RL samples several rollouts per query, consensus rewards require repeated generation, and self-generated experience adds proposer, verifier, or environment-construction passes. Long reasoning traces further consume computation even when their tokens do not affect the final reward. Under Eq.6，具有几乎恒定的群体奖励的任务可能会花费大量的计算，同时产生很少的学习信号。, tasks with nearly constant group rewards can spend substantial compute while producing little learning signal.

因此，经验生成是一个资源分配问题，其中每个采样轨迹都必须证明其计算成本是合理的。样本可以集中在可学习性边界附近，并且可以在完全生成之前拒绝低信息的推出。较短的流程反馈可以保留信用分配，而无需对每个令牌进行评分，而重播缓冲区可以在提议者和求解器更新之间重复使用昂贵的交互。在推理时，不确定性和估计的任务难度可以指导测试时计算的分配。这些机制将效率与能力联系起来，因为策略可用的有用经验的数量和多样性受到计算预算的限制。可信的报告将能力增益与获得它们所需的生成、验证和环境交互的数量配对。Experience generation is consequently a resource-allocation problem in which every sampled trajectory must justify its computational cost. Samples can be concentrated near the learnability frontier, and low-information rollouts can be rejected before full generation. Shorter process feedback may preserve credit assignment without scoring every token, while replay buffers can reuse expensive interactions across proposer and solver updates. At inference time, uncertainty and estimated task difficulty can guide the allocation of test-time computation. These mechanisms connect efficiency to capability because the number and diversity of useful experiences available to a policy are limited by the compute budget. Credible reports pair capability gains with the amount of generation, verification, and environment interaction required to obtain them.

6.4. 退出监督下的评估6.4. Evaluation under Receding Supervision

能力、反馈保真度和体验质量提供了补充证据，如第 1 节中所述Capability, feedback fidelity, and experience quality provide complementary evidence, as developed in Section5。当前的排行榜强调静态测试集的能力，而奖励稳健性、课程多样性、环境保真度和闭环稳定性仍然不太标准化. Current leaderboards emphasize capability on static test sets, while reward robustness, curriculum diversity, environment fidelity, and closed-loop stability remain less standardized(White et al., 2025; Qi et al., 2026b; Chen et al., 2026b)。重复使用相关法官进行培训和评估会产生额外的风险，因为相同的偏见可以提高政策的奖励及其报告的分数。. The reuse of related judges for training and evaluation creates an additional risk because the same bias can improve both the policy’s reward and its reported score.

表Table6总结了随着更多组件进入学习循环所需的最低证据。奖励生成方法需要与反馈来源相匹配的审核。生成任务或环境的方法还需要测量多样性、可解决性、有效性和迁移。实时和程序更新的基准减少了对固定评估分布的依赖summarizes the minimum evidence required as more components enter the learning loop. Reward-generating methods need audits matched to the source of feedback. Methods that generate tasks or environments additionally need measurements of diversity, solvability, validity, and transfer. Live and procedurally refreshed benchmarks reduce dependence on fixed evaluation distributions(White et al., 2025; Zhu et al., 2024; Jain et al., 2025)，尽管新鲜度本身并不能排除污染或方案偏差。共享评估工具同样重要，因为内在奖励、基于验证者和自我对弈方法之间的比较需要匹配的计算、数据访问和解码预算。仅当其外部锚点被冻结且独立于训练时，自动评估才保持可信。, although freshness alone cannot rule out contamination or protocol bias. Shared evaluation harnesses are equally important because comparisons among intrinsic-reward, verifier-based, and self-play methods require matched compute, data access, and decoding budgets. Automated evaluation remains credible only when its external anchors are frozen and independent of training.

6.5. 自主下的信任、安全和价值保存6.5. Trust, Safety, and Value Preservation under Autonomy

当直接监督变得稀疏时，人类价值观不会消失。他们的影响力渗透到初始数据、构成、工具、环境、目标和评估标准Human values do not disappear when direct supervision becomes sparse. Their influence moves into initial data, constitutions, tools, environments, objectives, and evaluation criteria(Bai et al., 2022; Sun et al., 2023)。当这些组件开始共同适应时，支持学习的反馈渠道也会放大失调。自我奖励可能会强化阿谀奉承或不安全的高度自信的反应。生成的课程可能会省略罕见但重要的安全案例，并且可利用的法官可能会偏爱有说服力的行为而不是正确或安全的行为. As these components begin to co-adapt, the feedback channels that support learning can also amplify misalignment. Self-reward may reinforce sycophancy or unsafe high-confidence responses. Generated curricula may omit rare but important safety cases, and exploitable judges may favor persuasive behavior over correct or safe behavior(Zhou, 2026; Yuan et al., 2024).

因此，安全评价必须考察与能力评价相同的三个对象。政策测试衡量有害行为和过度拒绝，反馈审计测试评估者在对抗性优化下的可靠性，经验审计评估生成的课程是否涵盖风险相关状态，而不是仅选择方便的案例。稀疏的人工审查和宪法审计可以作为外部否决权，但前提是它们保持独立于政策、奖励模型和课程生成器。更广泛的目标不是消除人类的参与。它是为了保留人类的判断力来设定目标、检测偏差以及在自我生成的证据变得不可靠时进行干预。Accordingly, safety evaluation must examine the same three objects as capability evaluation. Policy tests measure harmful behavior and over-refusal, feedback audits test evaluator reliability under adversarial optimization, and experience audits assess whether generated curricula cover risk-relevant states instead of selecting only convenient cases. Sparse human review and constitutional audits can serve as external vetoes, but only if they remain independent of the policy, reward model, and curriculum generator. The broader aim is not to eliminate human involvement. It is to reserve human judgment for setting objectives, detecting drift, and intervening when self-generated evidence becomes unreliable.

6.6。 迈向更广泛的训练范式和超级智能6.6. Toward Broader Training Paradigms and Superintelligence

本文考虑的大多数方法在预训练后都应用强化学习。一个长期的问题是，稀疏的外部锚点、生成的课程和封闭的经验循环是否也可以支持预训练或持续的中期训练，其中直接注释无法覆盖数据量和交互Most methods considered in this paper apply RL after pretraining. A longer-term question is whether sparse external anchors, generated curricula, and closed experience loops can also support pretraining or continual mid-training, where direct annotation cannot cover the volume of data and interaction(Silver & Sutton, 2025)。扩展这些机制将需要在大规模优化下保持稳定的奖励信号、扩大语义覆盖范围的课程以及在漂移复合之前检测到漂移的评估程序。迭代自我蒸馏、涌现课程和智能体-环境共同进化提供了这一过程的一部分，但还没有一个能够证明安全、开放式的能力增长。. Extending these mechanisms will require reward signals that remain stable under large-scale optimization, curricula that expand semantic coverage, and evaluation procedures that detect drift before it compounds. Iterative self-distillation, emergent curricula, and agent-environment co-evolution provide pieces of this process, but none yet demonstrates safe, open-ended capability growth.

五级阶梯描述了维持学习的责任的可能转移。即使人类无法表现出最佳行为或检查每个推理步骤，他们也可能会继续指定目标、约束和外部锚点。只有当执行、交互、冻结审核和价值约束保持足够独立以区分真正的改进和越来越有说服力的代理时，朝着更加自主的学习的进展才有意义。这种独立性是否能够大规模保留，将决定超越人类直接监督的学习是否支持超级智能的可靠进展，或放大无根据的自我强化。The five-level ladder describes a possible transfer of responsibility for sustaining learning. Humans may continue to specify goals, constraints, and external anchors even when they cannot demonstrate optimal behavior or inspect every reasoning step. Progress toward more autonomous learning is meaningful only if execution, interaction, frozen audits, and value constraints remain sufficiently independent to distinguish genuine improvement from an increasingly persuasive proxy. Whether this independence can be preserved at scale will determine if learning beyond direct human supervision supports reliable progress toward superintelligence or amplifies ungrounded self-reinforcement.

7. 结论和局限性7. Conclusion and Limitations

我们的论文研究了当人类直接监督不再随着经验的数量或难度而变化时，大型推理模型如何能够继续学习。我们沿着两个相连的轴组织这个问题。奖励轴涉及用于评估部署的证据，而经验轴涉及学习进行的任务和环境。五层梯子通过跟踪哪些组件仍然需要持续的人力供应来连接这些轴。Our paper examines how large reasoning models can continue to learn when direct human supervision no longer scales with the volume or difficulty of their experience. We organize this problem along two connected axes. The reward axis concerns the evidence used to evaluate a rollout, while the experience axis concerns the tasks and environments from which learning proceeds. The five-level ladder connects these axes by tracking which components still require continued human provision.

沿着奖励轴，可重复使用的评估器和模型衍生的、参考锚定的或基于环境的信号减少了直接判断的需要。然而，它们的可扩展性与它们使用的证据的独立性和有效性密不可分。沿着经验轴，任务生成和环境构建使训练分布能够适应策略，但会引入难度错误校准、课程缩小、环境错误以及生成器-验证者串通。因此，持续改进需要保持忠诚的奖励和保持信息丰富的体验流，因为两者都变得更具适应性。Along the reward axis, reusable evaluators and model-derived, reference-anchored, or environment-grounded signals reduce the need for direct judgment. Their scalability, however, is inseparable from the independence and validity of the evidence they use. Along the experience axis, task generation and environment construction allow the training distribution to adapt with the policy, but introduce difficulty miscalibration, curriculum narrowing, environment errors, and generator-verifier collusion. Continued improvement consequently requires rewards that remain faithful and experience streams that remain informative as both become more adaptive.

这些发现共同推动了一个由三部分组成的评估框架，该框架将政策结果与学习循环的有效性分开。策略能力衡量冻结外部任务的性能。反馈保真度测试奖励在优化下是否保留其预期含义。体验质量评估生成的任务和环境的多样性、有效性和可转移性。当在同一学习循环中产生反馈或经验时，仅获得能力是不够的。超出人类监督范围也不会消除人类意图。它将这种意图重新定位到可重用的评估器、目标、任务种子、环境和稀疏的外部审计中。这种学习循环可能有助于超级智能的进步，但监督自主既不构成一般智能，也不保证可靠的能力增长。Together, these findings motivate a three-part evaluation framework that separates policy outcomes from the validity of the learning loop. Policy capability measures performance on frozen external tasks. Feedback fidelity tests whether the reward preserves its intended meaning under optimization. Experience quality assesses the diversity, validity, and transferability of generated tasks and environments. Capability gains alone are insufficient when feedback or experience is produced within the same learning loop. Scaling beyond human supervision also does not remove human intent. It relocates that intent into reusable evaluators, objectives, task seeds, environments, and sparse external audits. Such learning loops may contribute to progress toward superintelligence, but supervisory autonomy neither constitutes general intelligence nor guarantees reliable capability growth.

局限性。Limitations.

梯子是一个分析框架，而不是一组相互排斥的方法类。方法通常占据过渡地位，因为奖励获取、任务生成、环境构建和策略优化不会同时变得自主。该框架还跟踪学习过程中监督的操作来源，而不是嵌入在预训练模型、参考文献、语料库、工具或环境中的人类知识的历史量。The ladder is an analytical framework, not a set of mutually exclusive method classes. Methods often occupy transitional positions because reward acquisition, task generation, environment construction, and policy optimization do not become autonomous simultaneously. The framework also tracks the operational source of supervision during learning, not the historical amount of human knowledge embedded in pretrained models, references, corpora, tools, or environments.

不同方法之间的直接比较仍然受到模型、计算、数据、解码、验证器和评估协议差异的限制。我们对安全、多语言推理、多模式交互和长情境学习的报道必然是有选择性的，而对超级智能的讨论仍然是概念性的。该框架并不意味着当前的系统是超级智能的，或者沿着阶梯的移动将不可避免地产生超级智能的行为。Direct comparison across methods remains constrained by differences in models, compute, data, decoding, verifiers, and evaluation protocols. Our coverage of safety, multilingual reasoning, multimodal interaction, and long-context learning is necessarily selective, and the discussion of superintelligence remains conceptual. The framework does not imply that current systems are superintelligent or that movement along the ladder will inevitably produce superintelligent behavior.

尽管存在这些限制，双轴框架澄清了在人类直接监督之外取得可信进展所需的证据。可靠的能力增长取决于能够承受优化压力的奖励、持续支持学习的经验以及与预期目标保持联系的外部锚点。Despite these limitations, the dual-axis framework clarifies the evidence required for credible progress beyond direct human supervision. Reliable capability growth depends on rewards that withstand optimization pressure, experience that continues to support learning, and external anchors that preserve contact with intended objectives.

报告错误的说明Instructions for reporting errors

我们正在继续改进论文的 HTML 版本，您的反馈有助于增强可访问性和移动性 支持。要报告 HTML 中的错误以帮助我们改进转换和呈现，请选择以下任一选项： 方法如下：We are continuing to improve HTML versions of papers, and your feedback helps enhance accessibility and mobile support. To report errors in the HTML that will help us improve conversion and rendering, choose any of the methods listed below:

  • 点击“报告问题”Click the "Report Issue"()按钮，位于页眉中。button, located in the page header.

提示：Tip:您可以先选择相关文本，将其包含在报告中。You can select the relevant text first, to include it in your report.

我们的团队已经确定Our team has already identifiedthe following issues。感谢您花时间查看和报告渲染错误，我们 可能还没有找到。您的努力将帮助我们为所有读者改进 HTML 版本，因为残疾 不应成为获得研究的障碍。感谢您对倡导开放获取的持续支持 所有。. We appreciate your time reviewing and reporting rendering errors we may not have found yet. Your efforts will help us improve the HTML versions for all readers, because disability should not be a barrier to accessing research. Thank you for your continued support in championing open access for all.
